## File: START-HERE.md

```text
# Portable Anchor handoff

`ANCHOR-HANDOFF.md` is one readable file carrying the complete Notepad Fidelity 9
source, contracts, tests, a standard-library receiver, and bridge instructions.
It is a development and execution handoff. For blind model assessment, give the
model only `notepad/blind_public/`; the complete handoff reveals reference code.
No independent external-model response file was supplied or scored.

## Read and run

A receiving LLM can read the `START-HERE.md` section, then
`notepad/LOGICAL-INTERPRETATION.md`, `notepad/LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json`,
`notepad/GATE-ECONOMY.json`, and `notepad/Notepad.anchor.txt` directly in the file.
Use the public challenge's result contract for the precise benchmark response
shape; the live shell has its own documented trace envelope.

Copy the complete `receiver.py` section to a new file after reviewing it. Python
3.11–3.14 and its standard library suffice for extraction and the logical shell:

```sh
python receiver.py inspect ANCHOR-HANDOFF.md
python receiver.py extract ANCHOR-HANDOFF.md --directory received
cd received/notepad
python logical_shell.py --port 8765
```

Open http://127.0.0.1:8765/. The shell has editing, history, search/replace,
virtual files and virtual clipboard. Browser file selection and download remain
explicit user interactions. `python notepad_surface.py` opens the separate Tk
application, requiring Tk and a display; that application uses actual guarded
file/clipboard capabilities. Extraction does not start either application.

With KPP available, the same receiver ships as `receiver.anchor` using the
existing EMIT_TEXT/0.2 profile:

```sh
python -m kpp anchor bridge-python receiver.anchor --out receiver.py
python receiver.py inspect ANCHOR-HANDOFF.md
```

The command writes the receiver; run it separately. The readable Fidelity 9
`Notepad.anchor.txt` is a design contract, not a native executable profile.
Fidelity 9 and the repository's NOTEPAD_LLM/1.3 are distinct contracts.

## Write and revise

Extract to a new directory, edit the source and matching contracts deliberately,
run the tests, then use:

```sh
python receiver.py repack ANCHOR-HANDOFF.md --directory received --output REVISED.md
```

Repacking preserves the original inventory, recalculates payload hashes and records
the parent handoff digest. It does not certify changes or update preservation pins.
For intentional additions/removals, use `pack(files, metadata=...)` from receiver.py
with an explicit mapping of portable relative paths to UTF-8 bytes, then inspect
the resulting file. Retain prior artifacts for comparison. Change the contract
version when changing its meaning. Keep private keys and live state out of public
handoffs. Imported provenance records describe the original bytes; subsequent
edits require a new provenance record rather than reusing the original claim.

## Encode in another language

The normative starting point is the supplied logical contract and per-action Gate
clauses, augmented by v2 public challenge result and observation contracts. The
32 trace cases are public development conformance vectors. They are not independent
LLM scores. Reimplementing prose requires explicit decisions where underspecified;
record those as a new version rather than inferring new Anchor syntax.

Port these values faithfully: Unicode code-point offsets and half-open ranges;
exact object fields; Boolean distinct from integer; text-change-only revision
increments; baseline-derived dirty status; bounded undo/redo with stale-history
rejection; conflict observations; unsuccessful effects; successful no-match
`{"kind":"none"}`; one history record per Replace All. Python regex ignore-case
semantics are not interchangeable with JavaScript Unicode case folding. A port
must match those cases or return UNDETERMINED where required semantics are absent.

A host can instead call `python notepad/bridge.py` as a persistent JSONL child
process. Each input is exactly `{"action":...,"payload":...}`; each output is
one shell response. The process owns one in-memory document and returns simulated
effects only. `node notepad/bridge-client.js` demonstrates this from JavaScript,
using `PYTHON` or `python3` and passing stdin/stdout unchanged. Other languages can
use the same protocol. This is verified Python-backed execution, not an independent
JavaScript implementation. A restart creates a fresh document; protocol state is
not an exported durable snapshot.

## Verify

```sh
cd received/notepad
python -m unittest discover -s . -p 'test_*.py' -q
python notepad_blueprint_gate.py --verify
```

Use Xvfb on headless Linux for the Tk tests. A skipped GUI test is unverified.
Run a port against the trace corpus and the same negative cases before claiming
compatibility. Model predictions, reference implementation results, actual host
effects, and Baker acceptance require their own evidence.

## Container format: anchor.handoff/1

All bytes are UTF-8. The receiver enforces a 32 MiB total input limit and at most
512 files. The fixed prefix precedes one JSON manifest and its closing marker.
The manifest has exactly schema, files, metadata and authority. Authority is NONE.
Each file entry has path, bytes and sha256. A file header introduces a readable
fenced section; read exactly `bytes` UTF-8 bytes, verify SHA-256, then require the
closing fence/marker. Embedded fences and marker strings are data within those
lengths. Reject duplicate/case-colliding paths, reserved device names, traversal,
file/directory collisions, malformed lengths, changed bytes, and trailing content.
Metadata is descriptive and does not authorize actions. Hashes verify integrity,
not origin or equivalence of code and contracts. No automatic migration, execution,
model API, new Anchor definition or execution profile is introduced.

The receiver validates all sections before creating a new destination. Extraction
and repacking refuse existing outputs. The outer receiver must be reviewed under
the receiving host's policy; a carried receiver cannot authenticate itself.

## Build from the repository

```sh
PYTHONPATH=src python -m mantleos.anchor_handoff_build --output NEW-HANDOFF.md
```

Sources live under `src/mantleos/kpp/resources/examples/notepad-fidelity9`.
The generator includes all selected source bytes, receiver, EMIT_TEXT receiver,
and component notices in one file. The owner supplied the Fidelity 9 ZIP;
IMPORT-PROVENANCE.json records its digest and imported source hashes. No new
license grant is inferred from public visibility. The original private key and
control answers remain outside Git and the handoff. The source-containing handoff
is unsuitable for source-hidden benchmark testing even without that key.

```
<!-- END ANCHOR FILE -->

## File: notepad/AGENT-FEEDBACK-RECONCILIATION.md

```text
# Fidelity 9 — External-agent feedback reconciliation and blind-packet repair

**Date:** 2026-11-13. **Status:** Benchmark contract revised; actual external-model answers have **NOT** been independently scored. No additions to the **nine AnchorApplications**, **nineteen detailed GateConditions**, or **nine proposed Gate families**. No changes to the nine existing Anchor Python implementation modules, app semantics, or 41 benchmark expected outcomes.

## Source and evidence boundary

The user supplied a transcript of another agent examining `Notepad-Anchor-Model-Only-Challenge(1).zip`. It reports: 41 trials answered, 31 predicted successes, 10 rejections; structural review passed; it raised two contract issues. The raw `responses.jsonl` bytes were **not accessible** to this working environment, despite its filename appearing in the pasted log. Therefore **no model score, field match, per-trial correctness, token count, or latency can be independently claimed**. A 31/10 class distribution merely matches the reference aggregate counts; incorrect per-trial assignments could still share those totals.

## What was reproduced locally

| Feedback | Independent inspection | Decision / adjustment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 41 answers: 31 successes and 10 rejects | The private reference key has precisely 31 `PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS` and 10 `REJECTED_BY_GATE` outcomes | **Not a measured model score.** Preserve as an unverified external report pending the actual JSONL |
| Successful `result.kind` forms unspecified | Per-action clause used `DocumentSnapshot` and generic response schema said {kind, selection?, count?}, while evaluator expected literal lowercase JSON variants | **Confirmed.** Add `result_contract.result_kinds` for `snapshot`, `selection`, `match`, `none`, `replace`, with exact fields and `allowed_result_kinds_by_action` for 17 supported commands. Strictly parse success result structure |
| Save's expected disk digest absent | Blind public state omitted private reference `expected_digest`; one Save follows an Open and requires a match check | **Confirmed.** Provide `file_session_observation.disk_comparison` as a trusted pre-command virtual-host observation, with five explicit statuses. This is input evidence, not a positive effect result, and requires no SHA-256 arithmetic from an LLM |
| 9 family / 19 clause taxonomy not separately exposed | Public had only eight family IDs and 17 runtime clause IDs represented directly by the scenarios | **Confirmed.** Provide `gate_registry` with all nine families and all nineteen detailed conditions; explicitly distinguish offline `G-BUILD-01` and projection `G-UI-01` from 17 command clauses |
| Other sample outcomes (Unicode edits, undo, no-op replacement, clipboard-failure cut) | The reference key contains these behavior classes, but the purported agent output was unavailable | Keep as reported illustrations, **not verified external successes** |

## What changed in the model-facing packet

- Public schema: `notepad.blind-logic-challenge/1` -> `/2`; private key schema `/1` -> `/2` to prevent accidental mixing. The response schema stays `/1`: the six top-level response keys and ten state fields are unchanged.
- New top-level `result_contract` and `gate_registry`, including a complete 17-action output-kind map and an exact Gate inventory. These are **descriptions of the existing system**, not additional Anchors, runtime gates or unreviewed host capabilities.
- New top-level `file_session_observation_contract` and a per-trial `file_session_observation` with `active_path`, `disk_comparison`, and `observation_source`. Statuses: `NO_ASSOCIATION`, `MATCH`, `MISMATCH`, `MISSING`, `UNKNOWN`. The packet never asks the model to calculate or fabricate a hidden digest.
- `blind_trial.load_responses` now rejects unknown success-result kinds, extra/missing result keys, malformed selection ranges, and invalid counts instead of silently accepting structurally ambiguous JSON.
- Existing 41 user actions and their **exact expected states, results, reasons, and Gate families are unchanged**, verified by direct structural/key comparison. No new Gate condition or Anchor was added to address these feedback points.

## Mechanical evidence

- Prior challenge SHA-256 (canonical JSON): `e53d5991080c03584ff00f15f42f9894e0a41b37d392a1da176f37871c3ddd8b`.
- Revised challenge SHA-256 (canonical JSON): `dbef9137e256902ec89f5ccc9924f53fd245dbc8523b0a22373692b40a0a1c06`.
- Original and revised expected answers compared: **41/41 byte-equivalent Python objects** (including classifications, state outputs, trial IDs, and source scenario tags). Original trial payloads and preconditions retained; the per-trial new observation is additive.
- Reference-control scorer: **41/41** exact trial matches, **574/574** field checks. Deliberately modified answer: **40/41**, **573/574** field checks.
- Local full `unittest` suite: **254/254** passed under Xvfb, zero skips. Blueprint verifier: **254/254**, zero skips. (Previous checkpoint: 248/248; six tests added to `test_blind_trial.py`.)
- Nine Anchor implementation module bytes matched the previous package, verified by SHA-256. Local checks are not independent formal certification or Baker acceptance.

## How to finish the actual external-model test

Obtain the **real** `responses.jsonl` file produced in the other session. Keep it read-only and record its SHA-256, exact model name/configuration, instructions, challenge version originally seen, retries, time/tokens and access controls. Then score against the **matching** public/private pair:

```sh
python blind_trial.py score \
  --challenge blind_public/challenge.json \
  --key blind_private/private-key.json \
  --responses /path/to/actual-responses.jsonl \
  --report /new/evaluator-report.json
```

The v1 responses can also be scored against this v2 key **if they retain the same six-key response schema**, because the 41 trial IDs and expected answers were preserved. Report that as a retrospective comparison; it is not equivalent to a model taking the *revised* challenge because the additional observations were unavailable to the original model. For original-protocol evaluation, use the retained v1 challenge and v1 key from the preceding package.

## Important model / host distinction

Logical `PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS` only establishes what should occur **given the declared virtual-host observations**. It cannot prove a real filesystem write, clipboard update, display mount, or Baker acceptance. This format targets frontier LLMs, is model-provider-independent, and makes no claim that lower-capability models are a required or verified runtime.

**Architectural decision:** Keep nine Anchors and nineteen detailed Gates. Strengthen existing contract semantics and evidence exposure rather than creating extra Gate identities to repair ambiguity. Formal KPP Satisfaction and explicit Baker approval remain outstanding.

```

## File: notepad/ANCHOR-DESIGN.md

```text
# Notepad — Fidelity 9 global Anchor recalculation

**Exactly one new Anchor:** `WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE` (**WhiteHole**, build-time). Total **9**. The previous eight runtime Anchor implementations remain **byte-identical** to the Fidelity 8 artifact because a runtime rebuild would introduce risk without removing an architectural conflict. The optimization is **separation of enforcement concerns**: the prior runtime guard keeps runtime command/effect authorization; the new WhiteHole checks the entire build blueprint and preservation records before a coding agent can present a candidate as locally ready.

This is a draft AnchorForge `anchorforge.organ-spec/1` and human-readable design. There is **no claim** of formal AnchorForge review, a formally satisfied `kpp.recipe/4`, or an executable native `.anchor` profile.

## Nine-Anchor recalculation

| # | Anchor | Type | Global review disposition |
|--:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `BH-EDITOR-INPUT` | BlackHole | Preserved input-as-data truth; source unchanged; build gate requires exact ID/class. |
| 2 | `N-DOCUMENT` | Neutral | Preserved sole text, revision and baseline owner; build gate pins declared state ownership and rejects protected duplicate writes. |
| 3 | `WH-COMMAND-GUARD` | WhiteHole | Preserved runtime command authorization; build gate now audits literal action allowlist, effect guard and display dispatch path. |
| 4 | `N-EDIT-HISTORY` | Neutral | Preserved undo/redo and document-revision ownership; build gate checks required fields/owner declarations. |
| 5 | `N-EDITOR-SURFACE` | Neutral | Preserved GUI projection and BH → WH route; build gate checks the source dispatch references. |
| 6 | `N-FILE-SESSION` | Neutral | Preserved guarded UTF-8 read/write/path ownership; build gate checks effect executor and selected raw file calls. |
| 7 | `N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION` | Neutral | Preserved clipboard capability, Unicode selection and host effects; build gate checks declared executor and source boundaries. |
| 8 | `N-SEARCH-REPLACE` | Neutral | Preserved pure literal match semantics; build gate rejects source-level raw file/clipboard calls in this Anchor. |
| 9 | **`WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE`** | **WhiteHole** | **New.** Audit nine-Anchor candidate mapping and build obligations before readiness; no editor state or user-intent execution. |

## Two complementary WhiteHole boundaries

```text
RUNTIME (unchanged):
Tk UI / controller (N-EDITOR-SURFACE)
    -> BH-EDITOR-INPUT (capture intent verbatim)
    -> WH-COMMAND-GUARD (payload validation, action/capability authorization)
       -> N-DOCUMENT / N-EDIT-HISTORY / N-FILE-SESSION /
          N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION / N-SEARCH-REPLACE
    -> authoritative snapshot projected by UI

BUILD TIME (new, outside user command path):
anchorforge-spec.json (draft) + blueprint-manifest.json
    + nine mapped Anchor source modules + all regression tests
       -> WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE (fail-closed consistency checks)
       -> full cumulative Python test execution with --verify
       -> evidence for a reviewed candidate; NEVER automatic Baker acceptance
```

## Optimized global architecture decisions

- **Distinct gates, no duplication of runtime semantics:** `WH-COMMAND-GUARD` remains the only runtime gate for user commands. `WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE` does not parse editor requests or hold document state. Building policy is not added to every editing operation.
- **No gratuitous rebuild:** Eight previous modules already have one authoritative document/history/file owner, bound clipboard/search operations and a guarded GUI. Rewriting them would not simplify the observed state graph. Existing design is therefore preserved; the optimization adds missing *build* verification without runtime coupling.
- **One canonical draft, one machine mapping:** `anchorforge-spec.json` owns responsibilities, flows, state, effects, obligations. `blueprint-manifest.json` maps this content to source classes and preservation IDs; it is not a second source of behavioral truth.
- **Additive evidence:** 181 inherited test identities and all 23 previous mandatory obligation IDs are preserved. Four new testable draft obligations cover role/state conformance, host effects/actions, obligation/test preservation and scoped acceptance.
- **Cumulative gate constraints:** unique Anchor roles/classes; no loss of count; exact source mapping; unique Neutral state owners; mapped protected field writers; effect requester/guard/executor/confirmation; runtime action allowlist; flow endpoints; mandatory obligation coverage and acyclic prerequisites; inherited test-method identity; and limited static host-call checks.

## State and effect ownership

13 previous declared state items remain owned by their prior Neutral Anchors. The new build-time WhiteHole owns **no runtime state**; its report is a transient value returned to a CLI caller. Four previously declared file/clipboard host effects retain `WH-COMMAND-GUARD`, their original requesters/executors and explicit confirmations. Running the offline test CLI executes Python processes in the development host, but is **not an editor runtime effect** and grants no app capability.

## Evidence, Chase and limits

Under a headless Linux Xvfb display, **213/213 tests passed**: all **181** prior tests unchanged plus **32** new tests for valid conformance, intentionally broken copied candidates, fail-closed CLI, and non-mutation of the audited sources. The new offline gate itself reports no violations for the shipped candidate.

**Chase:** original basic Notepad scope remains locally met. Fidelity 9 adds developer-blueprint preservation/hardening, locally met as scoped. Structural static checks cannot prove arbitrary runtime semantics or the integrity of a hostile coding agent that can edit the checker itself. Native AnchorForge review, KPP satisfaction and Baker acceptance remain **pending**.

## Fidelity 9 end-state Chase overlay (no new AnchorApplication)

The Baker clarified that minimizing AnchorApplication count while completely specifying the output is more important than continuing automatic `+1` rounds after the Chase target is covered. The original nine identities are preserved. See `CHASE-END-STATE-REVIEW.md` for the explicit stop assessment.

`LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json` adds **19 proposed GateCondition records** (one per runtime command plus one UI-projection Gate and one build-conformance Gate) referencing the existing nine applications; it does not redefine the canonical three Anchor types. `LOGICAL-INTERPRETATION.md` explains bounded stimulus-response prediction by a receiving LLM, and `LOGICAL-TRACE-CORPUS.json` provides 32 expected-behavior cases. This sidecar does not modify or certify `anchorforge.organ-spec/1`, and it does not create native `.anchor` execution semantics. Both the nine-Anchor count and the 27 draft obligations remain unchanged.

**End-state assessment:** original Notepad feature Chase locally covered; full KPP satisfaction and explicit Baker acceptance pending. Stop adding Anchors absent a distinct unmet responsibility.


## Fidelity 9 next-step economy / logical shell addendum

- AnchorApplication count stays at **nine**. No new canonical type, responsibility, runtime state owner, effect authority or Anchor is created.
- Detailed existing GateCondition clauses stay at **19**: seventeen verb clauses and two cross-cutting clauses. `GATE-ECONOMY.json` groups them under nine candidate independent Gate responsibilities, with exact clause ID traceability and many-to-many action participation. No new Gate is created.
- The **project optimization rule** jointly minimizes necessary Anchors and Gates while retaining measurable state, transition, host effects, failure and output truths. It does not override the immutable source-pinned `N-RULE-ECONOMY` singularity.
- `logical_engine.py` provides source-independent, deterministic interpretation of the command contract for testing/visualization. It is a test aid, NOT a new Anchor, KPP admission, LLM invocation, or native `.anchor` execution profile.
- `logical_shell.py` projects the interpreter state in a lightweight browser surface; all file/clipboard actions remain explicitly virtual except user-chosen browser file reads/download requests.
- A receiving LLM should be tested blindly against packets from `reasoning_prompt()`, without access to Python implementations or expected answers. Independent model accuracy is still **unverified**.

## NEW — v2 blind contract improvements from external-agent feedback

The externally reported 41 predictions (31 success / 10 rejected) have **not** been independently scored: the actual `responses.jsonl` content was not available with the supplied transcript. The aggregates match our reference key, but they are not accuracy evidence.

`blind_public/challenge.json` is now `notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2`, with:

- A **complete** nine-family/19-condition `gate_registry`, including build-time and UI clauses absent from the directly exercised runtime subset.
- An explicit `result_contract` naming allowed JSON result variants and allowed kinds per command; `DocumentSnapshot` is descriptive prose, not an output literal.
- An explicit trusted virtual file-session comparison observation for every trial, so Save conflict status never depends on a hidden hash calculation. No host success is assumed from this observation.
- Strict result-kind, result-field, count, and selection validation in the private evaluator.

All 41 stimuli, preexisting trial fields, and sealed expected answers remain identical to v1. We changed the public challenge fingerprint, not the behavioral target. See `AGENT-FEEDBACK-RECONCILIATION.md` and `CHALLENGE-COMPATIBILITY-LEDGER.json`. The public model-only ZIP must be distributed separately from this complete package.

## File: notepad/CODING-AGENT-CONTRACT.md

```text
> **2026-11-13 economy/end-state clarification:** The original automatic +1-per-advance rule is superseded at a covered Chase endpoint. See `CHASE-END-STATE-REVIEW.md` and `LOGICAL-INTERPRETATION.md`. Nine AnchorApplications, 19 additional contract GateConditions (17 runtime + 2 lifecycle), and 27 draft obligations are three separate counts. No native KPP Gate schema is claimed.

# Fidelity 9 — Coding-agent blueprint and truth gates

## Authority and order

**Draft truth input:** `anchorforge-spec.json` (`anchorforge.organ-spec/1`). **Build conformance overlay:** `blueprint-manifest.json`. **Implementation:** nine mapped `.py` Anchor modules. **Local evidence:** full `test_*.py` regression suite, and `python notepad_blueprint_gate.py --verify`. **Acceptance authority:** Baker alone. The implementation does not derive authority from merely passing tests.

Before coding, a coding agent MUST read the full draft specification and manifest. It MUST treat all 27 mandatory obligations as cumulative and preserve all 181 inherited tests, or explicitly flag any proposed exception for human/Baker review before claiming readiness. Altering the spec, manifest, test set and runtime in concert does **not** constitute independent review or Baker authorization. No agent may certify its own revision as a formally approved truth.

## Canonical responsibilities / state

| Anchor | Type | Exclusive truth |
|---|---|---|
| `BH-EDITOR-INPUT` | BlackHole | Preserve observed intent without interpreting or executing it |
| `N-DOCUMENT` | Neutral | Sole authoritative document text, revision, clean baseline / dirty derivation |
| `WH-COMMAND-GUARD` | WhiteHole | Authorize and dispatch untrusted runtime commands and opt-in host effects |
| `N-EDIT-HISTORY` | Neutral | Sole bounded undo/redo stacks and expected revision |
| `N-EDITOR-SURFACE` | Neutral | Transient Tk view/dialog state; route GUI intents through BH then runtime WH |
| `N-FILE-SESSION` | Neutral | Active path/digest; authorized UTF-8 open/new/save effects and baseline handshake |
| `N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION` | Neutral | Selection range semantics and injected clipboard callback capability |
| `N-SEARCH-REPLACE` | Neutral | Stateless literal match/replace calculations, no second text or history store |
| `WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE` | WhiteHole | Offline candidate-build conformance checks; no document state or runtime dispatch |

Runtime entry: `N-EDITOR-SURFACE -> BH-EDITOR-INPUT -> WH-COMMAND-GUARD -> relevant Neutral owner`. `WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE` audits declared boundaries at build time and must not be inserted into the editor's per-keystroke path.

## Every `advance`: required agent procedure

1. **Stop when the Chase target is met.** Do not automatically increase Anchor count merely because the Baker says `advance`: first evaluate whether the completed output has a distinct unmet Anchor responsibility. If a new Anchor is genuinely needed in a future feature advance, introduce exactly one, preserving existing identities. Never reduce the existing count or leave an Anchor without meaningful truth. Reconfigure existing Anchors/Gates where that is more economical.
2. Recalculate all Anchors globally: identify the complete state graph, ownership, host effects, prerequisites and redundancies. Reassign relationships or rebuild code where justified by simplicity, coverage and preservation. Do not rewrite stable code merely to show activity.
3. Identify prior behavioral truths and any phase-specific assertions intentionally superseded. Describe the *reason* and tests for each exception; no silent regressions.
4. Update the draft specification, manifest, the design and tests together, preserving recognized prior obligation/test IDs unless reviewed. Adjust manifest fidelity and source maps as appropriate for the new round.
5. Run `python notepad_blueprint_gate.py --verify` against the **integrated output**. Check the source/flow map, protected writers, allowed runtime actions, guarded effects and test identities; then execute the test suite. A `valid` static report is not the same as a passing runtime suite.
6. Record the evidence, formal tool limitations, remaining Chase targets and Baker-acceptance status in `status.json` and design review. Offer the artifact for Baker review, not a claim of automatic completion.

## Current offline gate's explicit limits

The gate uses source-level Python AST checks and a preservation registry. It can detect missing identities, changed literal role declarations, changed declared state owners, missing obligation dependencies or flow endpoints, selected duplicate state writes, direct known host calls in unauthorized source modules, removed inherited test method names, and runtime action-allowlist drift.

It **cannot** prove semantic correctness, exhaustive effect confinement, test quality, or authorization against a hostile party able to edit all the gate and manifest files. It does not sign artifacts, sandbox execution, run AnchorForge's native review, execute KPP's full satisfaction workflow or obtain the Baker's approval. Human review and cumulative executed tests remain necessary.


## Fidelity 9 next-step economy / logical shell addendum

- AnchorApplication count stays at **nine**. No new canonical type, responsibility, runtime state owner, effect authority or Anchor is created.
- Detailed existing GateCondition clauses stay at **19**: seventeen verb clauses and two cross-cutting clauses. `GATE-ECONOMY.json` groups them under nine candidate independent Gate responsibilities, with exact clause ID traceability and many-to-many action participation. No new Gate is created.
- The **project optimization rule** jointly minimizes necessary Anchors and Gates while retaining measurable state, transition, host effects, failure and output truths. It does not override the immutable source-pinned `N-RULE-ECONOMY` singularity.
- `logical_engine.py` provides source-independent, deterministic interpretation of the command contract for testing/visualization. It is a test aid, NOT a new Anchor, KPP admission, LLM invocation, or native `.anchor` execution profile.
- `logical_shell.py` projects the interpreter state in a lightweight browser surface; all file/clipboard actions remain explicitly virtual except user-chosen browser file reads/download requests.
- A receiving LLM should be tested blindly against packets from `reasoning_prompt()`, without access to Python implementations or expected answers. Independent model accuracy is still **unverified**.


## Current blind frontier-model trial addition (no new Anchor/Gate)

The new `blind_trial.py` plus `test_blind_trial.py` are **evaluation utilities**,
not Anchor applications, GateConditions, new runtime rules, or authoritative
host permissions. They prepare model-only packets from the reviewed proposed
logical contract and test them against a private reference answer key. Do not
mistake a `control` submission for a separate model run, and never expose
`blind_private/private-key.json` or application source to the interpreting model.
Never count benchmark tooling as new fidelity. An actual independent frontier-model
interpretation result remains open until executed and scored with provenance.

## NEW — v2 blind contract improvements from external-agent feedback

The externally reported 41 predictions (31 success / 10 rejected) have **not** been independently scored: the actual `responses.jsonl` content was not available with the supplied transcript. The aggregates match our reference key, but they are not accuracy evidence.

`blind_public/challenge.json` is now `notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2`, with:

- A **complete** nine-family/19-condition `gate_registry`, including build-time and UI clauses absent from the directly exercised runtime subset.
- An explicit `result_contract` naming allowed JSON result variants and allowed kinds per command; `DocumentSnapshot` is descriptive prose, not an output literal.
- An explicit trusted virtual file-session comparison observation for every trial, so Save conflict status never depends on a hidden hash calculation. No host success is assumed from this observation.
- Strict result-kind, result-field, count, and selection validation in the private evaluator.

All 41 stimuli, preexisting trial fields, and sealed expected answers remain identical to v1. We changed the public challenge fingerprint, not the behavioral target. See `AGENT-FEEDBACK-RECONCILIATION.md` and `CHALLENGE-COMPATIBILITY-LEDGER.json`. The public model-only ZIP must be distributed separately from this complete package.

## File: notepad/ECONOMY-DECISION.md

```text
# Anchor + Gate Economy — Fidelity 9 preservation decision

**Decision:** Do not add Anchor #10. Do not add Gate #20. Keep every pre-existing truth and reconfigure the 19 condition entries into **nine proposed independent Gate families**. The original 19 IDs remain as detailed condition *clauses* in `LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json` and every clause has exactly one primary Gate-family owner. Actions can pass through multiple families. The family counts are an engineering proposal, not an adopted KPP schema or mathematical optimum.

## Revised project economy rule

> Use the **fewest independently necessary Anchors AND Gates** that completely specify required input, state, transitions, ownership, effects, failures, observable outputs, and preservation. Before creating a new Anchor or Gate, analyze and, where appropriate, reconfigure the complete existing set. Never reduce the count by hiding a distinct truth, collapsing independent effect authority, or weakening a testable invariant.

An Anchor application carries a distinct responsibility; a Gate family carries an independently enforceable constraint. They are **many-to-many**: a Gate can refer to multiple AnchorApplications and an Anchor can participate in multiple Gates. A command's routing/transition clause is not automatically a new independent Gate. A Gate family is not a fourth Anchor type. WhiteHole remains the canonical runtime/build boundary role where specified.

**Canonical immutability:** KPP's source-defined `N-RULE-ECONOMY` singularity currently reads `Use as few rules as possible while still covering all needed variables.` It must not be silently changed in the pinned framework or forged by application metadata. The rule above is a proposed project-level optimization interpretation. To make it canonical, submit an explicit versioned KPP change, followed by review and MantleOS source parity checks. This artifact modifies **no canonical source file**.

## Consolidation result

| Gate family (candidate) | Primary coverage of prior condition clauses | Independent reason to remain separate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| G-ADMISSION | read, select-all | Reject malformed or undeclared commands before any state or effect |
| G-COMMIT | edit, insert, delete | Sole authoritative document mutation and revision/no-op invariants |
| G-HISTORY | undo, redo | Stack freshness, backward/forward invariants and edit replay |
| G-SESSION | new | Dirty-discard confirmation, baseline/history lifecycle |
| G-FILE-EFFECT | open, save, save-as | Host file read/write, conflict, association and verification boundary |
| G-CLIPBOARD-EFFECT | copy, cut, paste | Clipboard host read/write ordering before guarded edits |
| G-SEARCH | find, replace-one, replace-all | Literal search, selected match and atomic replace-all |
| G-UI-PROJECTION | UI-projection | UI projection must never own duplicate document truth |
| G-BUILD-CONFORMANCE | candidate-review | Offline build preservation and unskipped regression evidence |

Every runtime command also participates in G-ADMISSION; other family membership is defined in `GATE-ECONOMY.json`. The table gives each family’s *primary existing clause ownership*; it does **not** imply, for example, that file commands bypass admission or history binding.

### Why not fewer or more?

- **19 standalone gates:** 17 primarily mirror verbs rather than distinct failure domains; unnecessary conceptual duplication.
- **One monolithic gate:** Compact count, but obscures independently testable ownership/effect/failure boundaries and encourages superficial "valid" claims.
- **Nine candidates:** Distinct fault domains and evidence obligations remain independently testable while all 19 clause IDs survive. A future Scan/optimization review may discover a defensible grouping with fewer families; there is no claim of proven global minimum.
- **Tenth Anchor:** No uncovered state owner, ingress, relationship, or WhiteHole boundary was observed; adding one would violate the revised economy objective.

## Preservation properties

- Nine named AnchorApplications unchanged; original eight runtime module files and the ninth offline gate code unchanged.
- Original 17 action routes, exact payload requirements, 19 clauses and 27 draft recipe obligations retained.
- 13 state owner entries and four guarded effect categories retained.
- 32 expected stimulus-response scenarios retained and exercised by a **separate** logical reference interpreter, without importing existing Notepad implementation modules.
- Original 218 suite checks retained, with 14 additional reference/shell tests in the new prototype.

## Scope of evidence

Static coverage and a deterministic independent interpreter can disprove many omissions. They do not prove that independent LLMs, including intended frontier-model interpreters, will reliably interpret the rules, that a browser download succeeded on disk, or that the virtual file/clipboard profile matches real host behavior. Those are separate trials, and Baker acceptance plus formal KPP satisfaction remain pending.

```

{
  "schema": "notepad.logical-contract/1",
  "status": "PROPOSED_LOCAL_PREDICTIVE_CONTRACT",
  "relation_to_kpp": "Supplementary explicit interpretation contract; not an adopted KPP schema or native executable Anchor program.",
  "project": "Anchor Notepad",
  "anchor_round": 9,
  "anchor_count": 9,
  "original_chase_target": "A desktop plain-text editor supporting new/open/edit/save/save-as, undo/redo, cut/copy/paste/select-all, find/replace, keyboard shortcuts, and unsaved-change protection.",
  "anchor_definition_types": {
    "BlackHole": "Receive the declared input.",
    "Neutral": "Apply the declared relationship.",
    "WhiteHole": "Enforce the declared boundary."
  },
  "anchors": [
    {
      "id": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
      "type": "BlackHole",
      "responsibility": "Receive editor, file, clipboard, selection, search and replacement intents verbatim; no authorization, state change or host effect from capture."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of document text, clean baseline, revision and derived dirty status; expose trusted clean-load and save-confirmation hooks invoked only after guarded file actions."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "type": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Authorize exact, typed editing, history, file, clipboard and document-bound search/replace commands; reject unbound capabilities, invalid ranges and mismatches; route permitted state changes only to their Neutral owners."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of bounded, document-bound undo/redo transitions and its expected revision; use N-DOCUMENT to apply reversals without owning text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Project Tk document/history/path snapshots; route menu, keyboard, file, clipboard and Find/Replace interactions through BlackHole/WhiteHole; hold only transient visual selection and find dialog input, not document text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of active file path and last confirmed on-disk content digest. Read UTF-8, atomically write UTF-8, detect external conflicts, and coordinate fresh document/history baselines only upon authorized file operations."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Interpret document-codepoint selection ranges and own the explicit, document/history-bound clipboard capability using injected host callbacks. Return selected text / host acknowledgement to WH, without owning document text, undo history, or a parallel clipboard buffer."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Document/history-bound literal search and replace semantics, Unicode codepoint match ranges, case/wrap/direction options, and pure replacement calculations. Own no document text, search result cache or undo history."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "type": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Build-time, fail-closed conformance gate for the complete nine-Anchor blueprint: check distinct Anchor identities and roles, source mapping, Neutral state owners, effect boundaries, dependency-ready mandatory obligations, preserved test identities and command allowlist; never dispatch runtime user intents or claim formal KPP satisfaction."
    }
  ],
  "semantics": {
    "state": "(document.text, document.clean_baseline, document.revision, history.undo_stack, history.redo_stack, history.expected_revision, file_session.active_path, file_session.expected_digest, transient_selection, host_environment)",
    "dirty": "document.text != document.clean_baseline",
    "index_units": "Unicode codepoints, half-open [start,end) ranges. bool is not an integer.",
    "mutation": "Text revision increments once iff text value changes. An effective edit pushes one (before,after) undo entry, clears redo and synchronizes expected_revision; no-op edit leaves undo/redo unchanged.",
    "history_limit": "Configured positive integer (default 100); discard oldest undo transitions beyond limit. The initial history is empty.",
    "input_ingress": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT captures action and payload without authorization, execution, or state changes.",
    "runtime_guard": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD rejects unsupported/invalid actions and authorizes exactly one path. Neutral owners perform approved computation/transitions.",
    "host_effects": "External file and clipboard responses are input observations with explicit success/failure. No logical interpreter may assume a host effect succeeded or actually perform one.",
    "unknowns": "Missing host outcome, unsupported Unicode regex equivalence, unspecified OS semantics, or incomplete initial state => UNDETERMINED rather than success.",
    "postcondition": "After a successful transition, render document snapshot from N-DOCUMENT and any action-specific result. A rejected command leaves document/history untouched, but an external effect might already have occurred."

      "transition": "Find literal, nonoverlapping regex-escaped query matches; match_case=false uses Python Unicode regex IGNORECASE. Forward chooses first start >= start, backward chooses last end <= start; wrap selects from opposite portion. No state change.",
      "result": "MatchRange(start,end) or null",
      "external_effects": [],
      "rejection": "CommandRejected; document and history state preserved; no successful transition recorded",
      "notes": [
        "Unicode regex equivalence for unusual casefolds depends on specified Python regex profile; if interpreter lacks semantics, report UNDETERMINED, not an invented result"
      ],
      "phase": "RUNTIME"
    },
    {
      "gate_id": "G-CMD-16",
      "action": "replace-one",
      "participants": [
        "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
        "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
        "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
        "N-DOCUMENT",
        "N-EDIT-HISTORY"
      ],

        "type": "object",
        "exact_fields": {

          "replacement": "string",
          "start": "nonnegative integer",
          "end": "nonnegative integer",
          "match_case": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "preconditions": [
        "search/history bound/current",
        "0<=start<end<=len(text)",
        "selected slice fully matches literal query under specified case semantics"
      ],
      "transition": "Replace selected range with replacement; commit one revision iff text changed; record one undo transition iff changed. Report match count=1 even if replacement leaves text unchanged.",
      "result": "ReplaceResult(snapshot, count=1)",
      "external_effects": [],
      "rejection": "CommandRejected; document and history state preserved; no successful transition recorded",
      "notes": [],
      "phase": "RUNTIME"
    },
    {
      "gate_id": "G-CMD-17",
      "action": "replace-all",
      "participants": [

        "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
        "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
        "N-DOCUMENT",
        "N-EDIT-HISTORY"
      ],
      "input_shape": {
        "type": "object",
        "exact_fields": {
          "query": "nonempty string",
          "replacement": "string",
          "match_case": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "preconditions": [
        "search/history bound/current",
        "nonempty query"
      ],
      "transition": "Replace all nonoverlapping literal matches (Python Unicode regex case semantics); commit at most one document revision for the entire result; one history transition iff text changed; match count may be positive with no text change.",
      "result": "ReplaceResult(snapshot, count=N)",
      "external_effects": [],
      "rejection": "CommandRejected; document and history state preserved; no successful transition recorded",
      "notes": [

    },
    {

      ],

      },
      "preconditions": [

        "only authoritative document snapshots may be projected as document text"
      ],
      "transition": "UI never owns a second authoritative document text. On guarded response, project snapshot and ephemeral selection/find-dialog state; prompting for discard/overwrite occurs in the UI before guarded intent is sent.",
      "result": "Rendered editor text/dirty/path state or an explicitly reported rejected/undetermined UI action.",
      "external_effects": [],

## File: notepad/LOGICAL-INTERPRETATION.md

```text
# Notepad — LLM logical interpretation contract (Fidelity 9)

**Purpose:** A receiving LLM should be able to explain or predict the response of the nine-Anchor Notepad to a stated stimulus **from the contracts**, without reading or executing Python application code. The Python implementation remains a comparison target, **not** the source of truth for the logical answer.

**Status:** Proposed local contract, not a released `anchor.design`, `kpp.recipe`, GateCondition schema or executable `.anchor` profile. `LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json` is the detailed machine-readable companion. The 32 cases in `LOGICAL-TRACE-CORPUS.json` are expected observations and can be used as a held-out reasoning prompt; they are not actual proof of another LLM's ability.

## Canonical layers — do not conflate them

- `AnchorDefinition`: BlackHole receives declared input; Neutral applies declared relationships; WhiteHole enforces declared boundaries.
- `AnchorApplication`: the **nine** named project responsibilities. They own/receive/guard, but are **not** KPP recipe obligations.
- `GateCondition`: the **19** separately identified conditions (17 runtime command gates and two additional GUI/build gates) in `LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json`. Each names the involved AnchorApplications; these do not create additional Anchors or grant host authority.
- `RecipeObligation`: the **27** cumulative draft obligations in `anchorforge-spec.json`. They describe measurably required results. They are not equivalent to the nine applications or 19 contract Gates.
- `LogicalTransition`: a proposed contract interpretation of a command under a known state, boundary predicates, and confirmed host observations.
- `ExecutableAnchorProgram`: an `.anchor` in a qualified execution profile. `Notepad.anchor.txt` in this package is **not** such a program.

## Interpretation procedure — use without code execution

1. **Read state.** Record authoritative text `T`, clean baseline `B`, revision `R`, bounded undo stack `U`, redo stack `D`, expected history revision `H`, active file path `P`, expected on-disk digest `K`, transient UI selection, and any explicit host observations. If a required value is unavailable, return `UNDETERMINED` for the affected question.
2. **Receive input (BlackHole).** Capture `(action,payload)` exactly. Capturing is not authorization. Never transform missing values into defaults.
3. **Find exactly one command Gate.** Match the action in `LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json`. Unknown commands are rejected by `WH-COMMAND-GUARD`. A Gate links several Anchors but does not create a new type.
4. **Evaluate preconditions in order.** Validate the *exact* payload shape, types (notably Boolean is not integer), Unicode-codepoint offsets, required bindings, history freshness and any action-specific guard. If a precondition is false, return a rejection with no authorized document/history change. If a needed observation is missing, say `UNDETERMINED` rather than inventing permission or a host result.
5. **Resolve external effects only if explicitly observed.** Filesystem and clipboard operations cannot be performed mentally. For `open`, the input must include successful file read bytes or a failure. For saves, include a confirmed write/replace and conflict check. For clipboard read/write, include a callback result. Do not claim those effects occurred based on a generated prediction.
6. **Apply one authorized transition via the listed Neutral owners.** Compute changed text; increment `R` **only when text changes**. Dirty is always `T != B`. A changed undoable edit pushes `(before,after)` on the bounded undo stack and clears redo; unchanged text leaves history untouched. `new`/`open` reset history after successful baseline change. `save`/`save-as` change the clean baseline only after confirmed writes; do not erase history. Undo/redo preserve baseline.
7. **Return observable result.** For `read`/edits/files/undo, return snapshot; `find` returns match range or null; `select-all` returns Unicode-codepoint range; replace operations return snapshot plus replacement count. Do not derive UI appearance when the view contract does not specify it; describe snapshot/dirty indicator only where grounded.
8. **Report provenance and uncertainty.** Label output `PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS`, `REJECTED_BY_GATE`, or `UNDETERMINED`. A prediction is not an executed test, a host authorization, native `.anchor` execution or Baker acceptance.

## Example stimulus/response without program execution

```text
Known initial state:
 T="Hello"; B="Hello"; R=0; U=[]; D=[]; H=0
 input=("insert", {"offset":5,"text":" World"})

BH-EDITOR-INPUT receives (unchanged).
G-CMD-02 links BH-EDITOR-INPUT, WH-COMMAND-GUARD, N-DOCUMENT and N-EDIT-HISTORY.
WH-COMMAND-GUARD checks exact object shape, offset 0..5, current history binding.
N-DOCUMENT computes "Hello"[:5]+" World"+"Hello"[5:]=="Hello World".
Value differs => R=1. N-EDIT-HISTORY pushes ("Hello","Hello World") and sets H=1.
B remains "Hello", so dirty=True.

PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS:
 text="Hello World"; revision=1; dirty=True; undo_count=1; redo_count=0
No external effect. No Python application code was needed for this prediction.
```

## Interpretation edge cases that matter

| Scenario | Required prediction |
|---|---|
| Insert text identical to current document's resulting text | No new revision and no history entry |
| Undo after an edit | Restore former text, increment revision, preserve baseline, move undo entry to redo |
| New/Open when dirty and `discard=false` | Reject, preserve text, history, file association |
| Open with unreadable/non-UTF-8 file | Reject; no clean baseline installation |
| Save with no associated file | Reject; require Save As |
| Save on externally changed file | Reject, do not silently overwrite |
| Cut and clipboard write fails | Reject before editing document |
| Copy and clipboard write succeeds but callback changes state | Document edit rejected; clipboard effect may already have occurred |
| Replace All with two matches | At most **one** new document revision and one undo record |
| Find missing query | Return null match, no document/history mutation |
| Case-insensitive Unicode matching uncertain | Mark outcome `UNDETERMINED` if exact Python regex semantics are unavailable |

## Scope and stopping condition

The original Chase target is a single-document plain-text editor with New/Open/Save/Save As, editing, undo/redo, clipboard, selection, Find/Replace, shortcuts and unsaved-change protection. Its 17 internal commands are represented in the logical contract; GUI shortcut mapping is preserved by the existing Tk code and regression tests, not yet modeled as a complete portable event grammar. Therefore **this is not an unqualified universal logical executable**. It supports bounded predictions when inputs, state and host observations are fully declared.

The Chase can stop adding AnchorApplications when no distinct responsibility is missing. **Do not create Anchor #10 just to satisfy a round number.** If the Baker requests an additional feature with genuinely separate responsibility, re-evaluate the full design and add an Anchor only when justified. If scope is fully covered, proceed to review, KPP satisfaction when available, and explicit Baker acceptance.


## Fidelity 9 next-step economy / logical shell addendum

- AnchorApplication count stays at **nine**. No new canonical type, responsibility, runtime state owner, effect authority or Anchor is created.
- Detailed existing GateCondition clauses stay at **19**: seventeen verb clauses and two cross-cutting clauses. `GATE-ECONOMY.json` groups them under nine candidate independent Gate responsibilities, with exact clause ID traceability and many-to-many action participation. No new Gate is created.
- The **project optimization rule** jointly minimizes necessary Anchors and Gates while retaining measurable state, transition, host effects, failure and output truths. It does not override the immutable source-pinned `N-RULE-ECONOMY` singularity.
- `logical_engine.py` provides source-independent, deterministic interpretation of the command contract for testing/visualization. It is a test aid, NOT a new Anchor, KPP admission, LLM invocation, or native `.anchor` execution profile.
- `logical_shell.py` projects the interpreter state in a lightweight browser surface; all file/clipboard actions remain explicitly virtual except user-chosen browser file reads/download requests.
- A receiving LLM should be tested blindly against packets from `reasoning_prompt()`, without access to Python implementations or expected answers. Independent model accuracy is still **unverified**.

## NEW — v2 blind contract improvements from external-agent feedback

The externally reported 41 predictions (31 success / 10 rejected) have **not** been independently scored: the actual `responses.jsonl` content was not available with the supplied transcript. The aggregates match our reference key, but they are not accuracy evidence.

`blind_public/challenge.json` is now `notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2`, with:

- A **complete** nine-family/19-condition `gate_registry`, including build-time and UI clauses absent from the directly exercised runtime subset.
- An explicit `result_contract` naming allowed JSON result variants and allowed kinds per command; `DocumentSnapshot` is descriptive prose, not an output literal.
- An explicit trusted virtual file-session comparison observation for every trial, so Save conflict status never depends on a hidden hash calculation. No host success is assumed from this observation.
- Strict result-kind, result-field, count, and selection validation in the private evaluator.

All 41 stimuli, preexisting trial fields, and sealed expected answers remain identical to v1. We changed the public challenge fingerprint, not the behavioral target. See `AGENT-FEEDBACK-RECONCILIATION.md` and `CHALLENGE-COMPATIBILITY-LEDGER.json`. The public model-only ZIP must be distributed separately from this complete package.

```
<!-- END ANCHOR FILE -->

## File: notepad/Notepad.anchor.txt

```text
# Notepad Fidelity 9 — human-readable design, NOT executable native .anchor
# 9 Anchors; exactly one new from Fidelity 8: WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE (WhiteHole)

BH-EDITOR-INPUT        BlackHole    capture raw intent, no permission or state change
N-DOCUMENT            Neutral      sole authoritative text/revision/clean baseline
WH-COMMAND-GUARD      WhiteHole    authorize runtime commands and host effects
N-EDIT-HISTORY        Neutral      sole undo/redo history + expected revision
N-EDITOR-SURFACE      Neutral      Tk GUI projection and runtime BH→WH routing
N-FILE-SESSION        Neutral      authorized UTF-8 file path/digest/persistence
N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION Neutral      selection and injected clipboard capability
N-SEARCH-REPLACE      Neutral      stateless literal search/replacement computation
WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE     WhiteHole    OFFLINE candidate build conformity (NEW)

RUNTIME: UI -> BH -> WH-COMMAND-GUARD -> Neutral owner(s) -> UI snapshot
OFFLINE: draft spec + mapping + Anchor sources + tests -> WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE
          -> cumulative Python tests -> REVIEW EVIDENCE (not Baker acceptance)

All 13 previous declared state ownership records remain unchanged. Four declared
file/clipboard effects remain runtime-guarded. The new WhiteHole is stateless
and does not participate in runtime editor command flow.

Draft mandatory obligations: 27 (23 inherited + 4 for new gate).
Tests: 213/213 local under Xvfb (181 inherited unchanged + 32 added).
Chase basic features locally met. Formal KPP satisfaction and Baker acceptance
remain pending; this design is not a native Anchor profile.

# Completion-readiness logical overlay (no new Anchor added):
# LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json defines 17 independent GateConditions linked to these nine Anchors.
# LOGICAL-INTERPRETATION.md defines an LLM prediction method; this remains NOT native executable .anchor.
# Feature Chase locally covered; formal KPP satisfaction and Baker acceptance pending.

```

{
  "schema": "anchorforge.organ-spec/1",
  "id": "generic-notepad-round-09",
  "goal": "Build a usable generic notepad while retaining all previously established obligations after each new Anchor. Enforce cumulative blueprint integrity as a build-time WhiteHole gate for coding agents.",
  "target": "A desktop plain-text editor supporting new/open/edit/save/save-as, undo/redo, cut/copy/paste/select-all, find/replace, keyboard shortcuts, and unsaved-change protection.",
  "mode": "composition",
  "product_kind": "software",
  "tissue": "python",
  "fidelity": 9,
  "ingredients": [],
  "responsibilities": [
    {
      "id": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
      "definition": "BlackHole",
      "responsibility": "Receive editor, file, clipboard, selection, search and replacement intents verbatim; no authorization, state change or host effect from capture."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of document text, clean baseline, revision and derived dirty status; expose trusted clean-load and save-confirmation hooks invoked only after guarded file actions."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "definition": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Authorize exact, typed editing, history, file, clipboard and document-bound search/replace commands; reject unbound capabilities, invalid ranges and mismatches; route permitted state changes only to their Neutral owners."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of bounded, document-bound undo/redo transitions and its expected revision; use N-DOCUMENT to apply reversals without owning text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Project Tk document/history/path snapshots; route menu, keyboard, file, clipboard and Find/Replace interactions through BlackHole/WhiteHole; hold only transient visual selection and find dialog input, not document text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of active file path and last confirmed on-disk content digest. Read UTF-8, atomically write UTF-8, detect external conflicts, and coordinate fresh document/history baselines only upon authorized file operations."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Interpret document-codepoint selection ranges and own the explicit, document/history-bound clipboard capability using injected host callbacks. Return selected text / host acknowledgement to WH, without owning document text, undo history, or a parallel clipboard buffer."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "definition": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Document/history-bound literal search and replace semantics, Unicode codepoint match ranges, case/wrap/direction options, and pure replacement calculations. Own no document text, search result cache or undo history."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "definition": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Build-time, fail-closed conformance gate for the complete nine-Anchor blueprint: check distinct Anchor identities and roles, source mapping, Neutral state owners, effect boundaries, dependency-ready mandatory obligations, preserved test identities and command allowlist; never dispatch runtime user intents or claim formal KPP satisfaction."
    }
  ],
  "flows": [
    {
      "from": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
      "to": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "relationship": "Pass captured, untrusted intent for validation."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "Dispatch only validated in-memory actions to the state owner."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "relationship": "After validation, record successful edits or request undo/redo from the bound history owner."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "Apply undo/redo through the document state owner, preserving its clean baseline."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "to": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
      "relationship": "Forward GUI text changes, read/history, file, selection and clipboard intents through capture before WH authorization."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "relationship": "Return authoritative document snapshots or a pure selected-range result for projection in Tk."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "relationship": "After authorization and confirmation validation, request New/Open/Save/Save As on the bound file session."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "Install new/opened text as clean baseline, or acknowledge completed save without taking over document ownership."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "to": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "relationship": "Reset the bound edit timeline after New or Open; preserve history on Save/Save As."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "to": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "relationship": "Render the confirmed active file path from an immutable session snapshot; not an unguarded file command."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "relationship": "Dispatch authorized copy/cut/paste/select-all selections after type, range, binding and history validation."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "to": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "relationship": "Return host clipboard read content or confirmed copy acknowledgement; WH rechecks history and text snapshot before any Cut/Paste mutation."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "to": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "relationship": "Inject only the explicit Tk host clipboard adapter; physical selection is transient UI state, not authoritative document text."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "After successful guarded cut/paste external interaction, apply one validated codepoint selection replacement, with N-EDIT-HISTORY recording one undoable transition."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "relationship": "After exact payload and capability/history validation, request pure literal find/match/replacement calculations."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "to": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "relationship": "Return immutable match ranges or calculated replacement text/count; no state mutations or external effects."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "Apply approved Replace or Replace All as a single document-owned text revision if changed."
    },
    {
      "from": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "to": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "relationship": "Bind search semantics to the existing document/history; transient query/replacement widgets remain UI-only inputs."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "to": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "relationship": "During candidate-build review, inspect the runtime guard role, approved commands and external-effect guard declarations; offline audit only, never editor-command dispatch."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "to": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "relationship": "During candidate-build review, verify user commands still enter through BH capture and the runtime WH guard; offline audit only."
    },
    {
      "from": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "to": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "relationship": "During candidate-build review, check preserved Neutral ownership of text, baseline and revision; offline audit only."
    }
  ],
  "state": [

    },

    }
  ],
  "effects": [

## File: notepad/blind_public/START-HERE.md

```text
# Notepad Anchor Code — Model-independent blind interpretation challenge

This is the **model-facing** packet only. The sealed expected results, reference
Python implementation, scenario labels and scoring machinery are intentionally absent.
This benchmark targets frontier models but does not depend on a model, SDK,
provider, chain-of-thought disclosure, Python runtime, or hosted API.

For **each** item in `challenge.json.trials`, use only its declared initial state,
stimulus, canonical Anchor types, the linked Gate condition and shared semantics.
Compute the **updated** state and user-visible result. Submit one standalone JSON
object per line to the evaluator as `responses.jsonl`; 41 lines, any order.

Required JSON keys exactly: `trial_id`, `outcome`, `result`, `state`,
`gate_families`, `reason`. The full state has 10 keys documented by the
`expected_response_schema`. Copy the initial state's virtual file values unless
an approved virtual effect changes them; do not invent real file/clipboard writes.
For a missing host observation or irreducible ambiguity, use `UNDETERMINED` and
explain why rather than fabricating a success. For rejected commands, use
`REJECTED_BY_GATE`, null result and preserve all state that should remain stable.
For successes use `PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS` and the clause-specific result object.

The explicit `result_contract` provides the exact allowed JSON result variants
and maps the 17 supported actions to those variants. `gate_registry` enumerates all
9 families and all 19 condition clauses. The build-time family is offline only,
not an additional runtime dispatch requirement. Each trial includes a pre-command
`file_session_observation.disk_comparison` from the trusted virtual host: MATCH,
MISMATCH, MISSING, NO_ASSOCIATION, or UNKNOWN. On Save, MISMATCH rejects, MISSING
may allow a new virtual write, and UNKNOWN requires UNDETERMINED if equality
is required. Do not calculate, invent, or assume a hidden expected SHA-256.
The observation is a precondition, not proof that a later write succeeded.

This is a **virtual host** exercise. Initial `virtual_files` values use either
`{"text":"..."}` for valid UTF-8 content or `{"bytes_hex":"fffe"}` for
invalid UTF-8. Do not compute SHA-256; it is intentionally excluded from the
required response. The provided prestate and contract still control the rules.
Document positions use Unicode code points, with half-open ranges.

Do not inspect any reference program, evaluator key or answered corpus. Do not
execute Python. All cases are independent snapshots, not a sequential API session.
The expectation is a falsifiable structured result, not disclosure of hidden model
reasoning. An operator should keep token/latency/retry/provenance logs separately.

```

{
  "schema": "notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2",
  "scope": "VIRTUAL_IN_MEMORY_ONLY; never claim OS file or clipboard effects",
  "purpose": "Predict each independent response using only declarative Anchor and Gate contracts.",
  "anchor_count": 9,
  "gate_family_count": 9,
  "gate_condition_count": 19,
  "canonical_anchor_types": {
    "BlackHole": "Receive the declared input.",
    "Neutral": "Apply the declared relationship.",
    "WhiteHole": "Enforce the declared boundary."
  },
  "anchor_applications": [
    {
      "id": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT",
      "type": "BlackHole",
      "responsibility": "Receive editor, file, clipboard, selection, search and replacement intents verbatim; no authorization, state change or host effect from capture."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-DOCUMENT",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of document text, clean baseline, revision and derived dirty status; expose trusted clean-load and save-confirmation hooks invoked only after guarded file actions."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD",
      "type": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Authorize exact, typed editing, history, file, clipboard and document-bound search/replace commands; reject unbound capabilities, invalid ranges and mismatches; route permitted state changes only to their Neutral owners."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDIT-HISTORY",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of bounded, document-bound undo/redo transitions and its expected revision; use N-DOCUMENT to apply reversals without owning text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-EDITOR-SURFACE",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Project Tk document/history/path snapshots; route menu, keyboard, file, clipboard and Find/Replace interactions through BlackHole/WhiteHole; hold only transient visual selection and find dialog input, not document text."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-FILE-SESSION",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Sole owner of active file path and last confirmed on-disk content digest. Read UTF-8, atomically write UTF-8, detect external conflicts, and coordinate fresh document/history baselines only upon authorized file operations."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Interpret document-codepoint selection ranges and own the explicit, document/history-bound clipboard capability using injected host callbacks. Return selected text / host acknowledgement to WH, without owning document text, undo history, or a parallel clipboard buffer."
    },
    {
      "id": "N-SEARCH-REPLACE",
      "type": "Neutral",
      "responsibility": "Document/history-bound literal search and replace semantics, Unicode codepoint match ranges, case/wrap/direction options, and pure replacement calculations. Own no document text, search result cache or undo history."
    },
    {
      "id": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
      "type": "WhiteHole",
      "responsibility": "Build-time, fail-closed conformance gate for the complete nine-Anchor blueprint: check distinct Anchor identities and roles, source mapping, Neutral state owners, effect boundaries, dependency-ready mandatory obligations, preserved test identities and command allowlist; never dispatch runtime user intents or claim formal KPP satisfaction."
    }

    "dirty": "document.text != document.clean_baseline",
    "index_units": "Unicode codepoints, half-open [start,end) ranges. bool is not an integer.",
    "mutation": "Text revision increments once iff text value changes. An effective edit pushes one (before,after) undo entry, clears redo and synchronizes expected_revision; no-op edit leaves undo/redo unchanged.",
    "history_limit": "Configured positive integer (default 100); discard oldest undo transitions beyond limit. The initial history is empty.",
    "input_ingress": "BH-EDITOR-INPUT captures action and payload without authorization, execution, or state changes.",
    "runtime_guard": "WH-COMMAND-GUARD rejects unsupported/invalid actions and authorizes exactly one path. Neutral owners perform approved computation/transitions.",
    "host_effects": "External file and clipboard responses are input observations with explicit success/failure. No logical interpreter may assume a host effect succeeded or actually perform one.",
    "unknowns": "Missing host outcome, unsupported Unicode regex equivalence, unspecified OS semantics, or incomplete initial state => UNDETERMINED rather than success.",

    "origin": "TRUSTED_VIRTUAL_HOST_PRECOMMAND",
    "comparison_values": {
      "NO_ASSOCIATION": "No active file path; Save rejects until associated through Open or Save As",
      "MISSING": "Active path absent from virtual files; Save may create file after other guards and confirmed FS_WRITE",
      "MATCH": "Current bytes at active path match last confirmed expected digest; not proof a future write succeeded",
      "MISMATCH": "Bytes at active path differ from last confirmed digest; Save and same-path Save As reject",
      "UNKNOWN": "Expected digest or required observation missing; do not assume equality"

    "effect_order": "Check preconditions, perform virtual effect if authorized and available, then acknowledge baseline/path; effect failure must not be presented as a clean save",
