## File: notepad/blind_trial.py

```text
"""Model-neutral, source-hidden Notepad Anchor reasoning benchmark.

Prepare creates TWO disjoint exports: an interpreter-facing challenge containing
only declarative contract/stimuli and a private evaluator key containing expected
results. Scoring NEVER executes candidate-supplied code, imports a model SDK,
performs host effects, or claims independent LLM competence.

CLI:
  python blind_trial.py prepare --challenge-dir NEW --sealed-dir NEW
  python blind_trial.py score --key KEY.json --challenge CHALLENGE.json --responses FILE.jsonl --report NEW.json
  python blind_trial.py control --key KEY.json --output NEW.jsonl
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from copy import deepcopy
from hashlib import sha256
import json
from pathlib import Path
import random
import sys

from logical_engine import LogicalMachine, digest

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CHALLENGE_SCHEMA = 'notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2'
KEY_SCHEMA = 'notepad.blind-logic-private-key/2'
RESPONSE_SCHEMA = 'notepad.blind-logic-response/1'
REPORT_SCHEMA = 'notepad.blind-logic-score/1'
FIELDS = ('trial_id', 'outcome', 'result', 'state', 'gate_families', 'reason')
STATE_FIELDS = ('text', 'clean_baseline', 'revision', 'dirty', 'undo_stack',
                'redo_stack', 'history_expected_revision', 'active_path',
                'clipboard', 'virtual_files')
VALID_OUTCOMES = {'PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS', 'REJECTED_BY_GATE', 'UNDETERMINED'}
MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 4096 * 41  # Enough for 41 complete bounded answers

# These are value-level output projections, not new Gates or Anchor definitions.
# One JSON variant is selected after applying the existing G-CMD-xx clause.
RESULT_VARIANTS = {
    'snapshot': {'required_keys': ['kind'], 'kind': 'snapshot', 'meaning': 'Document snapshot; no extra result fields'},
    'selection': {'required_keys': ['kind','selection'], 'kind': 'selection',
                  'selection': 'Two integer code-point offsets [start,end], half-open'},
    'match': {'required_keys': ['kind','selection'], 'kind': 'match',
              'selection': 'Matching code-point range [start,end], half-open'},
    'none': {'required_keys': ['kind'], 'kind': 'none',
             'meaning': 'Find completed successfully; no match exists under the declared search options'},
    'replace': {'required_keys': ['kind','count'], 'kind': 'replace',
                'count': 'Nonnegative integer number of matches replaced, including no-op replacements'},
}
ACTION_RESULT_KINDS = {
    'read':['snapshot'],'edit':['snapshot'],'insert':['snapshot'],'delete':['snapshot'],
    'undo':['snapshot'],'redo':['snapshot'],'new':['snapshot'],'open':['snapshot'],
    'save':['snapshot'],'save-as':['snapshot'],'copy':['snapshot'],'cut':['snapshot'],
    'paste':['snapshot'],'select-all':['selection'],'find':['match','none'],
    'replace-one':['replace'],'replace-all':['replace'],
}


def file_session_observation(machine):
    """Expose equality evidence, not a hidden SHA-256 value or presumed effect success.

    This is the virtual host's *pre-command* confirmed file-session observation.
    An associated file can be absent (Save then creates it) or externally altered.
    UNKNOWN is distinct from MISMATCH: unknown observations cannot be guessed.
    """
    path = machine.active_path
    if path is None:
        status = 'NO_ASSOCIATION'
    elif path not in machine.files:
        status = 'MISSING'
    elif machine.expected_digest is None:
        status = 'UNKNOWN'
    elif digest(machine.files[path]) == machine.expected_digest:
        status = 'MATCH'
    else:
        status = 'MISMATCH'
    return {'active_path':path, 'disk_comparison':status,
            'observation_source':'TRUSTED_VIRTUAL_HOST_PRECOMMAND'}


FILE_SESSION_OBSERVATION_CONTRACT = {
    'origin':'TRUSTED_VIRTUAL_HOST_PRECOMMAND',
    'comparison_values':{
        'NO_ASSOCIATION':'No active file path; Save rejects until associated through Open or Save As',
        'MISSING':'Active path absent from virtual files; Save may create file after other guards and confirmed FS_WRITE',
        'MATCH':'Current bytes at active path match last confirmed expected digest; not proof a future write succeeded',
        'MISMATCH':'Bytes at active path differ from last confirmed digest; Save and same-path Save As reject',
        'UNKNOWN':'Expected digest or required observation missing; do not assume equality',
    },
    'effect_order':'Check preconditions, perform virtual effect if authorized and available, then acknowledge baseline/path; effect failure must not be presented as a clean save',
    'not_an_output':'This input observation is intentionally not one of the ten public state projection fields or an independent authorization Gate.'
}


def complete_gate_registry(machine):
    """Show 9 families and all 19 clauses; no expected trial outcomes exposed."""
    conditions = deepcopy(machine.contract['gate_conditions'])
    families = deepcopy(machine.economy['gate_families'])
    assert len(conditions) == 19 and len(families) == 9
    return {'status':'PROJECT_PROPOSAL_NOT_CANONICAL_KPP',
            'gate_families':families, 'gate_conditions':conditions,
            'interpretation':'RUNTIME clauses govern user stimuli; G-UI-01 governs projection; G-BUILD-01 is offline build conformance and is not a user command gate.',
            'gate_counting':'19 independently testable clauses grouped into 9 proposed families; groups do not create new Gate conditions.'}


def declared_result_contract():
    return {'result_kinds':deepcopy(RESULT_VARIANTS),
            'allowed_result_kinds_by_action':deepcopy(ACTION_RESULT_KINDS),
            'rejection_or_undetermined':'result is exactly null; reason is nonempty',
            'success':'result is exactly one specified variant with no extra keys; reason is empty',
            'unsupported_command':'REJECTED_BY_GATE, result=null, gate_families=[], nonempty reason',
            'all_successful_commands':'One JSON result object, never a human-readable DocumentSnapshot label.'}


def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                      separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False)


def fingerprint(value):
    return sha256(canonical(value).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()


def parse_json(path, *, max_bytes=2_000_000):
    p=Path(path)
    if p.stat().st_size > max_bytes:
        raise ValueError('JSON input exceeds bound')
    def unique_pairs(pairs):
        result={}
        for key, value in pairs:
            if key in result:
                raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON key: '+key)
            result[key]=value
        return result
    return json.loads(p.read_text('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=unique_pairs,
                      parse_constant=lambda value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON'))) 


def file_projection(content):
    try:
        return {'text':content.decode('utf-8')}
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return {'bytes_hex':content.hex()}


def state_projection(machine):
    return {
        'text':machine.text,
        'clean_baseline':machine.baseline,
        'revision':machine.revision,
        'dirty':machine.dirty,
        'undo_stack':deepcopy(machine.undo),
        'redo_stack':deepcopy(machine.redo),
        'history_expected_revision':machine.expected_revision,
        'active_path':machine.active_path,
        'clipboard':machine.clipboard,
        'virtual_files':{k:file_projection(v) for k,v in sorted(machine.files.items())},
    }


def _scenario_machine(case):
    files={};aliases={}
    for key, value in case.get('files',{}).items():
        path='/virtual/'+key+'.txt'
        aliases['@'+key]=path
        files[path]=bytes.fromhex(value['hex']) if 'hex' in value else value['text'].encode('utf-8')
    for key in case.get('expected_files',{}):
        aliases.setdefault('@'+key,'/virtual/'+key+'.txt')
    machine=LogicalMachine(case['initial_text'],clipboard=case.get('clipboard',''),files=files)
    if case.get('fail_clipboard_read'):machine.fail_effects.add('CLIPBOARD_READ')
    if case.get('fail_clipboard_write'):machine.fail_effects.add('CLIPBOARD_WRITE')
    return machine, aliases


def build_trial_data():
    corpus=parse_json(HERE/'LOGICAL-TRACE-CORPUS.json')
    if len(corpus['cases'])!=32:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected scenario corpus count')
    trials=[]
    key_entries=[]
    reference=LogicalMachine()
    for case in corpus['cases']:
        machine, aliases=_scenario_machine(case)
        for step_index, step in enumerate(case['steps']):
            payload=deepcopy(step.get('payload'))
            if type(payload) is dict:
                payload={k:aliases.get(v,v) if type(v) is str else v for k,v in payload.items()}
            before=state_projection(machine)
            observed_file_session=file_session_observation(machine)
            result=machine.send(step['action'],payload)
            # Cross-check this independent interpreter against the existing frozen
            # corpus's per-step expected classification before sealing anything.
            wanted=step['outcome']
            if wanted=='rejected':
                if result['outcome']!='REJECTED_BY_GATE':
                    raise ValueError('Frozen scenario expectation failed: '+case['id'])
            else:
                if result['outcome']!='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS' or result['result']['kind']!=wanted:
                    raise ValueError('Frozen scenario expectation failed: '+case['id'])
            source_packet=machine.last_packet
            # The source packet already contains no expected result. Convert the
            # host state to a comparison-friendly, model-runnable projection.
            trial={
                'initial_state':before,
                'stimulus':deepcopy(source_packet['stimulus']),
                'simulated_effect_failures':sorted(machine.fail_effects),
                'action_clause':deepcopy(source_packet['action_clause']),
                'gate_families':list(source_packet['gate_families']),
                'host_profile':source_packet['host_profile'],
                'file_session_observation':observed_file_session,
            }
            expected={
                'outcome':result['outcome'],
                'result':deepcopy(result.get('result')),
                'state':state_projection(machine),
                'gate_families':list(source_packet['gate_families']),
                'reason':'' if result['outcome']=='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS' else str(result.get('reason','')),
            }
            trials.append(trial)
            key_entries.append({'source_case':case['id'],'source_step':step_index,'expected':expected})
        # A second preservation check pins each completed scenario's final state
        # to the original published expectations, independently of the new key.
        snap=machine.state()
        observed={name:snap[name] for name in ('text','revision','dirty','undo_count','redo_count')}
        if 'clipboard' in case['expected_final']:
            observed['clipboard']=snap['clipboard']
        if 'path' in case['expected_final']:
            observed['path']=next((alias for alias,path in aliases.items() if snap['path']==path),None)
        if observed!=case['expected_final']:
            raise ValueError('Frozen scenario final result drift: '+case['id'])
        for name,expected_text in case.get('expected_files',{}).items():
            if machine.files[aliases['@'+name]].decode('utf-8')!=expected_text:
                raise ValueError('Frozen scenario virtual file result drift: '+case['id'])
    if len(trials)!=41:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected stimulus count')
    # Opaque labels; original case names are private and cannot reveal correctness.
    order=list(range(len(trials)))
    random.Random(20261113).shuffle(order)
    public_trials=[];private_answers=[]
    for rank,offset in enumerate(order,1):
        ident=f'T{rank:04d}'
        entry={**trials[offset], 'id':ident}
        public_trials.append(entry)
        private_answers.append({'trial_id':ident,**key_entries[offset]['expected'],
                                'source_case':key_entries[offset]['source_case'],
                                'source_step':key_entries[offset]['source_step']})
    public={
        'schema':CHALLENGE_SCHEMA,
        'scope':'VIRTUAL_IN_MEMORY_ONLY; never claim OS file or clipboard effects',
        'purpose':'Predict each independent response using only declarative Anchor and Gate contracts.',
        'anchor_count':9,'gate_family_count':9,'gate_condition_count':19,
        'canonical_anchor_types':reference.contract['anchor_definition_types'],
        'anchor_applications':reference.contract['anchors'],
        'shared_semantics':reference.contract['semantics'],
        'rules':reference.economy['economy_rule'],
        'gate_registry':complete_gate_registry(reference),
        'file_session_observation_contract':deepcopy(FILE_SESSION_OBSERVATION_CONTRACT),
        'result_contract':declared_result_contract(),
        'trials':public_trials,
        'expected_response_schema':{
            'schema':RESPONSE_SCHEMA,
            'required_fields':list(FIELDS),
            'state_fields':list(STATE_FIELDS),
            'outcomes':sorted(VALID_OUTCOMES),
            'result':'null for rejected/undetermined; otherwise exact JSON variant in result_contract',
            'reason':'empty string for success; nonempty English explanation for rejection/unknown',
        },
    }
    private={'schema':KEY_SCHEMA,'challenge_sha256':fingerprint(public),
             'trial_count':len(private_answers),'answers':private_answers,
             'basis':'Independent reference interpreter + pre-existing scenario assertions; not a model accuracy score.'}
    return public,private


def prepare(challenge_dir, sealed_dir):
    ch=Path(challenge_dir);seal=Path(sealed_dir)
    if ch.resolve()==seal.resolve() or ch.resolve().is_relative_to(seal.resolve()) or seal.resolve().is_relative_to(ch.resolve()):
        raise ValueError('Challenge and sealed evaluator directories must be disjoint')
    if ch.exists() or seal.exists():
        raise ValueError('Output directories must be new; no overwrite')
    public, private=build_trial_data()
    ch.mkdir(parents=True);seal.mkdir(parents=True)
    (ch/'challenge.json').write_text(json.dumps(public,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    (seal/'private-key.json').write_text(json.dumps(private,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    (ch/'START-HERE.md').write_text(CHALLENGE_GUIDE,encoding='utf-8')
    (ch/'response-template.jsonl').write_text(canonical({'trial_id':'T0001',
        'outcome':'UNDETERMINED','result':None,
        'state':{key:None for key in STATE_FIELDS},
        'gate_families':[], 'reason':'Replace with contract-derived answer.'})+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    (seal/'SEAL-README.md').write_text(SEALED_GUIDE,encoding='utf-8')
    return {'public_sha256':private['challenge_sha256'],'trials':len(public['trials']),
            'public_dir':str(ch),'sealed_dir':str(seal)}


def load_responses(path):
    b=Path(path).read_bytes()
    if len(b)>MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Responses exceed byte limit')
    answers={}
    for index,line in enumerate(b.decode('utf-8').splitlines(),1):
        if not line.strip():continue
        value=json.loads(line,object_pairs_hook=lambda pairs:_unique(pairs),
                         parse_constant=lambda x: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON')))
        if type(value) is not dict or set(value)!=set(FIELDS):
            raise ValueError('Response line '+str(index)+' needs exactly the declared fields')
        tid=value['trial_id']
        if type(tid) is not str or tid in answers:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or duplicate trial_id at line '+str(index))
        if value['outcome'] not in VALID_OUTCOMES:
            raise ValueError('Invalid outcome at line '+str(index))
        if type(value['state']) is not dict or set(value['state'])!=set(STATE_FIELDS):
            raise ValueError('State projection fields missing at line '+str(index))
        if type(value['gate_families']) is not list or any(type(x) is not str for x in value['gate_families']):
            raise ValueError('Invalid Gate identifiers at line '+str(index))
        if type(value['reason']) is not str:
            raise ValueError('Invalid reason at line '+str(index))
        if value['outcome']=='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS':
            result=value['result']
            if type(result) is not dict or value['reason']:
                raise ValueError('Success needs a result and empty reason')
            kind=result.get('kind')
            if type(kind) is not str or kind not in RESULT_VARIANTS:
                raise ValueError('Unknown successful result kind at line '+str(index))
            if set(result) != set(RESULT_VARIANTS[kind]['required_keys']):
                raise ValueError('Successful result fields do not match kind at line '+str(index))
            if 'selection' in result:
                selection=result['selection']
                if (type(selection) is not list or len(selection)!=2 or
                    any(type(i) is not int or i<0 for i in selection) or
                    selection[0]>selection[1]):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid result selection at line '+str(index))
            if 'count' in result and (type(result['count']) is not int or result['count']<0):
                raise ValueError('Invalid result count at line '+str(index))
        elif value['result'] is not None or not value['reason']:
            raise ValueError('Rejection/unknown needs null result and reason')
        answers[tid]=value
    return answers


def _unique(pairs):
    o={}
    for k,v in pairs:
        if k in o:raise ValueError('Duplicate JSON field '+k)
        o[k]=v
    return o


def score(public,private,submitted):
    if public.get('schema')!=CHALLENGE_SCHEMA or private.get('schema')!=KEY_SCHEMA:
        raise ValueError('Wrong challenge/key schemas')
    if fingerprint(public)!=private['challenge_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Challenge fingerprint does not match sealed private key')
    trial_ids=[t['id'] for t in public['trials']]
    if len(trial_ids)!=len(set(trial_ids)) or len(trial_ids)!=private['trial_count']:
        raise ValueError('Malformed challenge cardinality')
    if set(submitted)-set(trial_ids):
        raise ValueError('Unknown trial IDs')
    expected={r['trial_id']:r['expected'] if 'expected' in r else {k:r[k] for k in FIELDS if k!='trial_id'} for r in private['answers']}
    if set(expected)!=set(trial_ids):
        raise ValueError('Incomplete sealed answer key')
    cases=[];all_pass=0;fields_checked=0;field_pass=0
    for tid in trial_ids:
        got=submitted.get(tid)
        correct=expected[tid]
        field_mismatches=[]
        if got is None:
            field_mismatches=['MISSING_SUBMISSION']
        else:
            for field in ('outcome','result','gate_families'):
                fields_checked+=1
                if got[field]!=correct[field]:field_mismatches.append(field)
                else:field_pass+=1
            for field in STATE_FIELDS:
                fields_checked+=1
                if got['state'][field]!=correct['state'][field]:field_mismatches.append('state.'+field)
                else:field_pass+=1
            fields_checked+=1
            ok_reason=(got['reason']=='' if correct['outcome']=='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS' else bool(got['reason']))
            if not ok_reason:field_mismatches.append('reason')
            else:field_pass+=1
        if not field_mismatches:all_pass+=1
        cases.append({'trial_id':tid,'pass':not field_mismatches,'mismatches':field_mismatches})
    return {'schema':REPORT_SCHEMA,'challenge_sha256':private['challenge_sha256'],
            'total_trials':len(trial_ids),'submitted_trials':len(submitted),
            'passed_trials':all_pass,'complete_pass':all_pass==len(trial_ids),
            'field_checks':fields_checked,'field_passes':field_pass,
            'results':cases,
            'scope':'MODEL-AGNOSTIC RESPONSE SCORING ONLY; no evidence of independent LLM unless execution provenance is separately established',
            'host_effect_claim':'VIRTUAL_ONLY; no real effects attested'}


def write_new(path,data):
    with Path(path).open('x',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n') as file:
        file.write(data)


def main(argv=None):
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    cmd=parser.add_subparsers(dest='cmd',required=True)
    p=cmd.add_parser('prepare',help='Produce separate public and private trial dirs')
    p.add_argument('--challenge-dir',required=True);p.add_argument('--sealed-dir',required=True)
    p=cmd.add_parser('control',help='Create a reference submission for verifier qualification, NOT an LLM run')
    p.add_argument('--key',required=True);p.add_argument('--output',required=True)
    p=cmd.add_parser('score',help='Score a response file without running candidate code or LLMs')
    p.add_argument('--challenge',required=True);p.add_argument('--key',required=True)
    p.add_argument('--responses',required=True);p.add_argument('--report',required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if args.cmd=='prepare':
            result=prepare(args.challenge_dir,args.sealed_dir)
        elif args.cmd=='control':
            private=parse_json(args.key)
            if private.get('schema')!=KEY_SCHEMA:raise ValueError('Wrong private-key schema')
            lines=[canonical({k:(e[k] if k!='trial_id' else e['trial_id']) for k in FIELDS})
                   for e in private['answers']]
            write_new(args.output,'\n'.join(lines)+'\n')
            result={'control_file':args.output,'trials':len(lines),'provenance':'REFERENCE ANSWERS, NOT MODEL OUTPUT'}
        else:
            public=parse_json(args.challenge);private=parse_json(args.key)
            scored=score(public,private,load_responses(args.responses))
            write_new(args.report,json.dumps(scored,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
            result={k:v for k,v in scored.items() if k not in ('results',)}
        print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True))
        return 0
    except (ValueError,KeyError,TypeError,FileNotFoundError,PermissionError,FileExistsError) as e:
        print('FAIL: '+str(e),file=sys.stderr)
        return 1


CHALLENGE_GUIDE='''# Notepad Anchor Code — Model-independent blind interpretation challenge

This is the **model-facing** packet only. The sealed expected results, reference
Python implementation, scenario labels and scoring machinery are intentionally absent.
This benchmark targets frontier models but does not depend on a model, SDK,
provider, chain-of-thought disclosure, Python runtime, or hosted API.

For **each** item in `challenge.json.trials`, use only its declared initial state,
stimulus, canonical Anchor types, the linked Gate condition and shared semantics.
Compute the **updated** state and user-visible result. Submit one standalone JSON
object per line to the evaluator as `responses.jsonl`; 41 lines, any order.

Required JSON keys exactly: `trial_id`, `outcome`, `result`, `state`,
`gate_families`, `reason`. The full state has 10 keys documented by the
`expected_response_schema`. Copy the initial state's virtual file values unless
an approved virtual effect changes them; do not invent real file/clipboard writes.
For a missing host observation or irreducible ambiguity, use `UNDETERMINED` and
explain why rather than fabricating a success. For rejected commands, use
`REJECTED_BY_GATE`, null result and preserve all state that should remain stable.
For successes use `PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS` and the clause-specific result object.

The explicit `result_contract` provides the exact allowed JSON result variants
and maps the 17 supported actions to those variants. `gate_registry` enumerates all
9 families and all 19 condition clauses. The build-time family is offline only,
not an additional runtime dispatch requirement. Each trial includes a pre-command
`file_session_observation.disk_comparison` from the trusted virtual host: MATCH,
MISMATCH, MISSING, NO_ASSOCIATION, or UNKNOWN. On Save, MISMATCH rejects, MISSING
may allow a new virtual write, and UNKNOWN requires UNDETERMINED if equality
is required. Do not calculate, invent, or assume a hidden expected SHA-256.
The observation is a precondition, not proof that a later write succeeded.

This is a **virtual host** exercise. Initial `virtual_files` values use either
`{"text":"..."}` for valid UTF-8 content or `{"bytes_hex":"fffe"}` for
invalid UTF-8. Do not compute SHA-256; it is intentionally excluded from the
required response. The provided prestate and contract still control the rules.
Document positions use Unicode code points, with half-open ranges.

Do not inspect any reference program, evaluator key or answered corpus. Do not
execute Python. All cases are independent snapshots, not a sequential API session.
The expectation is a falsifiable structured result, not disclosure of hidden model
reasoning. An operator should keep token/latency/retry/provenance logs separately.
'''

SEALED_GUIDE='''# Sealed evaluator material — DO NOT SHOW TO THE INTERPRETING MODEL

`private-key.json` maps opaque trial IDs to reference expected behavior and
original source-case labels. The cases were checked against existing Notepad
scenario expectations using the independent deterministic reference interpreter.
This does not establish frontier LLM accuracy, a universal formal proof, or KPP
Satisfaction/Baker acceptance. Keep the model-facing challenge directory separate.
`blind_trial.py control` can produce a reference response to qualify the scorer:
that control is **not** an LLM result and must never be reported as one.
'''

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```text
'use strict';
// Python-backed persistent protocol adapter; no independent JS Anchor semantics.
const {spawn} = require('node:child_process');
const path = require('node:path');
const child = spawn(process.env.PYTHON || 'python3', [path.join(__dirname, 'bridge.py')], {stdio: 'inherit'});
child.on('error', error => { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; });
child.on('exit', (code, signal) => { process.exitCode = code === null ? 1 : code; });
```

## File: notepad/bridge.py

```text
"""Persistent virtual-only JSONL bridge for authorized language hosts."""
import json
import sys
from logical_engine import LogicalMachine

MAX_LINE = 262144


def unique(pairs):
    result = {}
    for k, v in pairs:
        if k in result:
            raise ValueError('Duplicate key')
        result[k] = v
    return result


def main():
    machine = LogicalMachine()
    while True:
        line = sys.stdin.buffer.readline(MAX_LINE + 1)
        if not line:
            return 0
        if len(line) > MAX_LINE:
            print(json.dumps({'error': 'Oversized command; session closed'}), flush=True)
            return 2
        try:
            value = json.loads(line, object_pairs_hook=unique,
                               parse_constant=lambda _: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError('Nonfinite JSON')))
            if type(value) is not dict or set(value) != {'action', 'payload'}:
                raise ValueError('Expected action and payload')
            result = machine.send(value['action'], value['payload'])
        except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as error:
            result = {'error': str(error)}
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```text
"""Optional end-to-end Chromium check; requires playwright and local shell server.

Start `python logical_shell.py --port 8765` first. This test has no model calls.
"""
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from urllib.error import HTTPError
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import os

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PORT=int(os.environ.get("ANCHOR_SHELL_TEST_PORT","8765"))
ENDPOINT=f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"

with sync_playwright() as p:
    browser=p.chromium.launch(headless=True,executable_path=shutil.which('chromium') or shutil.which('google-chrome'),args=['--no-sandbox'])
    page=browser.new_page(accept_downloads=True)
    errors=[]
    page.on('pageerror',lambda e:errors.append(str(e)))
    # Chromium in this hosted test environment blocks direct loopback requests.
    # Exercise the real UI JavaScript with a narrow Playwright transport adapter
    # that forwards the same JSON API to the live local server from Python.
    def local_api(path, body):
        data=body.encode('utf-8') if body is not None else None
        req=Request(ENDPOINT+path,data=data,
                    method='POST' if data is not None else 'GET',
                    headers={'Content-Type':'application/json'} if data is not None else {})
        try:
            with urlopen(req,timeout=3) as response:
                return {'status':response.status,'body':response.read().decode('utf-8')}
        except HTTPError as ex:
            return {'status':ex.code,'body':ex.read().decode('utf-8')}
    page.expose_function('anchor_local_api',local_api)
    html=(ROOT/'shell.html').read_text('utf-8')
    html=html.replace('<link rel="stylesheet" href="/shell.css" />','')
    html=html.replace('<script src="/shell.js" defer></script>','')
    page.set_content(html)
    page.add_style_tag(content=(ROOT/'shell.css').read_text('utf-8'))
    page.evaluate('''(()=>{window.fetch=async (path, init={})=>{
        const reply=await window.anchor_local_api(path, init.body ?? null);
        return {ok:reply.status>=200 && reply.status<300,status:reply.status,
                json:async()=>JSON.parse(reply.body)};
    };return 'patched';})()''')
    local_api('/api/reset','{}')
    page.add_script_tag(content=(ROOT/'shell.js').read_text('utf-8'))
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#editor").value === "Hello, Anchor!"')
    page.locator('#editor').fill('foo foo')
    page.locator('#query').fill('foo')
    page.locator('#replacement').fill('bar')
    page.locator('#replaceall').click()
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#editor").value === "bar bar"')
    assert 'PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS' in page.locator('#trace').inner_text()
    page.locator('#undo').click()
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#editor").value === "foo foo"')
    page.locator('#redo').click()
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#editor").value === "bar bar"')
    page.locator('#packet').click()
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#packetview").value.includes("gate_families")')
    prompt=page.locator('#packetview').input_value()
    assert '"action": "redo"' in prompt
    page.on('dialog',lambda d:d.accept('/virtual/example.txt'))
    page.locator('#saveas').click()
    page.wait_for_function('document.querySelector("#filename").textContent === "/virtual/example.txt"')
    assert page.locator('#dirty').inner_text().startswith('Saved')
    with page.expect_download() as info:
        page.locator('#download').click()
    download=info.value
    assert download.suggested_filename=='example.txt'
    page.screenshot(path=str(ROOT/'shell-preview.png'),full_page=True)
    assert not errors,errors
    print('BROWSER PASS: edit, replace-all, undo, redo, prompt packet, virtual save-as, browser download')
    browser.close()
```

## File: notepad/logical_engine.py

```text
"""Independent, bounded logical reference interpreter for the Notepad Anchor contracts.

This is a deterministic *test oracle / preview backend*, NOT an LLM and NOT native
Anchor execution. It deliberately imports no notepad_*.py application modules.
Filesystem and clipboard effects are simulated in memory. There is NO external
filesystem write in dispatch(), and a virtual save is NEVER claimed as a real save.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CONTRACT_PATH = ROOT / 'LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json'
ECONOMY_PATH = ROOT / 'GATE-ECONOMY.json'


class Rejected(Exception):
    pass


class Undetermined(Exception):
    pass


def require(test, why):
    if not test:
        raise Rejected(why)


def exact_object(value, fields):
    require(type(value) is dict and set(value) == set(fields),
            'Payload must be an object with exactly: ' + ', '.join(fields))


def index(value, upper, name):
    require(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= upper,
            f'{name} must be an integer in [0, {upper}] (bool is not an integer)')


def virtual_path(path):
    require(type(path) is str and len(path) > 1 and path.startswith('/') and '\x00' not in path,
            'Virtual file path must be a nonempty absolute path')
    require('..' not in path.split('/'), 'Parent traversal is not supported by the virtual host')
    return path


def digest(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


@dataclass
class ViewResult:
    kind: str = 'snapshot'
    selection: list[int] | None = None
    count: int | None = None

    def as_dict(self):
        d = {'kind': self.kind}
        if self.selection is not None:
            d['selection'] = self.selection
        if self.count is not None:
            d['count'] = self.count
        return d


class LogicalMachine:
    """Rule-driven admission + independently implemented transitions.

    Runtime action admission, action-specific text clauses and participating Gate
    families are always read from declarative JSON. The procedure implementations
    below make it a reference interpreter, not a magical compiler of prose.
    """
    def __init__(self, text='', *, clipboard='', files=None, history_limit=100):
        require(type(text) is str and type(clipboard) is str, 'Initial text/clipboard must be text')
        require(type(history_limit) is int and history_limit > 0, 'Invalid history limit')
        self.contract = json.loads(CONTRACT_PATH.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        self.economy = json.loads(ECONOMY_PATH.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        self.clauses = {c['action']: c for c in self.contract['gate_conditions'] if c['phase']=='RUNTIME'}
        self.gates_for = {a: [] for a in self.clauses}
        for g in self.economy['gate_families']:
            for a in g['actions']:
                self.gates_for[a].append(g['id'])
        self.text = text
        self.baseline = text
        self.revision = 0
        self.undo = []
        self.redo = []
        self.history_limit = history_limit
        self.expected_revision = 0
        self.active_path = None
        self.expected_digest = None
        self.clipboard = clipboard
        self.files = dict(files or {})  # virtual path -> bytes
        self.fail_effects = set()  # deliberately simulated test faults
        self.last_packet = None
        self.last_trace = None
        self._check_contract_integrity()

    def _check_contract_integrity(self):
        ids = [a['id'] for a in self.contract['anchors']]
        require(len(ids)==len(set(ids))==9, 'Not exactly nine distinct Anchor applications')
        require(set(self.clauses)==set(self.contract['conformance']['expected_actions']),
                'Action clauses do not cover the declared command vocabulary')
        all_clauses = {g['gate_id'] for g in self.contract['gate_conditions']}
        covered = [cl for g in self.economy['gate_families'] for cl in g['clauses']]
        require(set(covered)==all_clauses and len(covered)==len(set(covered)),
                'Each original rule clause must have one unique owning Gate family')
        require(all(self.gates_for.values()), 'A command has no gate protection')
        require(all(set(g['participants']).issubset(ids) for g in self.economy['gate_families']),
                'A Gate refers to an unknown Anchor')

    @property
    def dirty(self):
        return self.text != self.baseline

    def state(self):
        return {
            'text': self.text,
            'revision': self.revision,
            'dirty': self.dirty,
            'undo_count': len(self.undo),
            'redo_count': len(self.redo),
            'path': self.active_path,
            'clipboard': self.clipboard,
            'virtual_file_count': len(self.files),
        }

    def logical_state(self):
        """Complete known state for a blind LLM stimulus packet; no source code."""
        return {
            'text': self.text, 'clean_baseline': self.baseline,
            'revision': self.revision,
            'undo_stack': deepcopy(self.undo), 'redo_stack': deepcopy(self.redo),
            'history_expected_revision': self.expected_revision,
            'history_limit': self.history_limit,
            'active_path': self.active_path,
            'expected_digest': self.expected_digest,
            'virtual_clipboard': self.clipboard,
            'virtual_files': {p: {'bytes_hex': b.hex(), 'sha256': digest(b)} for p,b in self.files.items()},
            'simulated_effect_failures': sorted(self.fail_effects),
        }

    def _set(self, value, *, track=True):
        require(type(value) is str, 'Document text must be a string')
        if value == self.text:
            return False
        before = self.text
        self.text = value
        self.revision += 1
        if track:
            self.undo.append([before, value])
            if len(self.undo)>self.history_limit:
                del self.undo[:-self.history_limit]
            self.redo.clear()
        self.expected_revision = self.revision
        return True

    def _history_current(self):
        require(self.expected_revision == self.revision, 'History is stale')

    def _range(self, payload, *, nonempty=False):
        exact_object(payload, ('start','end'))
        start, end = payload['start'], payload['end']
        index(start, len(self.text), 'start'); index(end, len(self.text), 'end')
        require(start < end if nonempty else start <= end,
                'Selection boundaries are invalid')
        return start, end

    def _clipboard_read(self):
        if 'CLIPBOARD_READ' in self.fail_effects:
            raise Rejected('Simulated clipboard read failure')
        return self.clipboard

    def _clipboard_write(self, value):
        if 'CLIPBOARD_WRITE' in self.fail_effects:
            raise Rejected('Simulated clipboard write failure')
        self.clipboard = value

    def _read_file(self, path):
        if 'FS_READ' in self.fail_effects:
            raise Rejected('Simulated file read failure')
        require(path in self.files, 'Virtual file not found')
        b = self.files[path]
        try:
            return b.decode('utf-8'), digest(b)
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            raise Rejected('Virtual file is not UTF-8') from None

    def _write_file(self, path, data):
        if 'FS_WRITE' in self.fail_effects:
            raise Rejected('Simulated file write failure')
        self.files[path] = data
        return digest(data)

    def _match_pattern(self, query, match_case):
        require(type(query) is str and len(query)>0, 'Search query must be nonempty text')
        require(type(match_case) is bool, 'match_case must be Boolean')
        return re.compile(re.escape(query), 0 if match_case else re.IGNORECASE)

    def _dispatch(self, action, payload):
        self._history_current()
        if action == 'read':
            require(payload is None, 'read payload must be null')
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'edit':
            require(type(payload) is str, 'edit payload must be text')
            self._set(payload)
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'insert':
            exact_object(payload, ('offset','text'))
            off=payload['offset'];index(off,len(self.text),'offset')
            require(type(payload['text']) is str,'insert text must be a string')
            self._set(self.text[:off]+payload['text']+self.text[off:]); return ViewResult()
        if action == 'delete':
            start,end=self._range(payload)
            self._set(self.text[:start]+self.text[end:]); return ViewResult()
        if action == 'undo':
            require(payload is None,'undo payload must be null')
            require(bool(self.undo),'No undo history')
            before,after=self.undo[-1]
            require(self.text==after,'History text mismatch')
            self.undo.pop()
            self._set(before,track=False)
            self.redo.append([before,after])
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'redo':
            require(payload is None,'redo payload must be null')
            require(bool(self.redo),'No redo history')
            before,after=self.redo[-1]
            require(self.text==before,'History text mismatch')
            self.redo.pop()
            self._set(after,track=False)
            self.undo.append([before,after])
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'new':
            exact_object(payload,('discard',))
            require(type(payload['discard']) is bool,'discard must be Boolean')
            require(not self.dirty or payload['discard'],'Unsaved changes: discard not confirmed')
            self._set('',track=False)
            self.baseline=''; self.undo.clear();self.redo.clear()
            self.active_path=None;self.expected_digest=None
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'open':
            exact_object(payload,('path','discard'))
            require(type(payload['discard']) is bool,'discard must be Boolean')
            path=virtual_path(payload['path'])
            require(not self.dirty or payload['discard'],'Unsaved changes: discard not confirmed')
            value,d=self._read_file(path)
            self._set(value,track=False)
            self.baseline=value;self.undo.clear();self.redo.clear()
            self.active_path=path;self.expected_digest=d
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'save':
            require(payload is None,'save payload must be null')
            require(self.active_path is not None,'Save requires an associated file')
            if self.active_path in self.files:
                require(digest(self.files[self.active_path])==self.expected_digest,
                        'Virtual file changed outside this session')
            raw=self.text.encode('utf-8')
            self.expected_digest=self._write_file(self.active_path,raw)
            self.baseline=self.text
            return ViewResult()
        if action == 'save-as':
            exact_object(payload,('path','overwrite'))
            path=virtual_path(payload['path'])
            require(type(payload['overwrite']) is bool,'overwrite must be Boolean')
            require(path not in self.files or payload['overwrite'], 'Virtual file exists; overwrite not approved')
            if path==self.active_path and path in self.files:
                require(digest(self.files[path])==self.expected_digest,'Virtual file changed outside session')
            raw=self.text.encode('utf-8')
            d=self._write_file(path,raw)
            self.expected_digest=d;self.active_path=path;self.baseline=self.text
            return ViewResult()
        if action=='copy':
            start,end=self._range(payload,nonempty=True)
            self._clipboard_write(self.text[start:end]); return ViewResult()
        if action=='cut':
            start,end=self._range(payload,nonempty=True)
            self._clipboard_write(self.text[start:end])
            self._set(self.text[:start]+self.text[end:]);return ViewResult()
        if action=='paste':
            start,end=self._range(payload)
            content=self._clipboard_read()
            require(type(content) is str,'Clipboard observation must contain text')
            self._set(self.text[:start]+content+self.text[end:]);return ViewResult()
        if action=='select-all':
            require(payload is None,'select-all payload must be null')
            return ViewResult(kind='selection',selection=[0,len(self.text)])
        if action=='find':
            exact_object(payload,('query','start','match_case','wrap','backwards'))
            start=payload['start'];index(start,len(self.text),'start')
            require(type(payload['wrap']) is bool and type(payload['backwards']) is bool,'Search flags must be Boolean')
            pattern=self._match_pattern(payload['query'],payload['match_case'])
            matches=list(pattern.finditer(self.text))
            if payload['backwards']:
                choices=[m for m in matches if m.end()<=start]
                match=choices[-1] if choices else (matches[-1] if payload['wrap'] and matches else None)
            else:
                choices=[m for m in matches if m.start()>=start]
                match=choices[0] if choices else (matches[0] if payload['wrap'] and matches else None)
            return ViewResult(kind='match',selection=[match.start(),match.end()]) if match else ViewResult(kind='none')
        if action=='replace-one':
            exact_object(payload,('query','replacement','start','end','match_case'))
            require(type(payload['replacement']) is str,'replacement must be text')
            start=payload['start'];end=payload['end']
            index(start,len(self.text),'start');index(end,len(self.text),'end')
            require(start<end,'Selected range must be nonempty')
            p=self._match_pattern(payload['query'],payload['match_case'])
            require(p.fullmatch(self.text[start:end]) is not None,'Selection does not match query')
            self._set(self.text[:start]+payload['replacement']+self.text[end:])
            return ViewResult(kind='replace',count=1)
        if action=='replace-all':
            exact_object(payload,('query','replacement','match_case'))
            require(type(payload['replacement']) is str,'replacement must be text')
            p=self._match_pattern(payload['query'],payload['match_case'])
            value,count=p.subn(lambda _:payload['replacement'],self.text)
            self._set(value)
            return ViewResult(kind='replace',count=count)
        raise Rejected('Unrecognized command')

    def send(self, action, payload=None):
        """Apply one stimulus, return projection + provenance. No physical effects."""
        before = self.logical_state()
        rule = self.clauses.get(action) if type(action) is str else None
        packet = {
            'contract_schema': self.contract['schema'],
            'gate_economy_schema': self.economy['schema'],
            'known_state_before': before,
            'stimulus': {'action':action, 'payload':payload},
            'action_clause': rule,
            'gate_families': self.gates_for.get(action,[]),
            'host_profile': 'VIRTUAL_ONLY: effects run in memory; no OS filesystem/clipboard effects',
        }
        self.last_packet = packet
        try:
            require(rule is not None,'Unsupported action')
            output=self._dispatch(action,payload)
            result={'outcome':'PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS',
                    'result':output.as_dict(), 'state':self.state(),
                    'trace':{'received_by':'BH-EDITOR-INPUT',
                             'authorized_by':'WH-COMMAND-GUARD',
                             'action_clause':rule['gate_id'],
                             'gate_families':self.gates_for[action],
                             'participants':rule['participants'],
                             'external_effects':rule['external_effects'],
                             'effect_scope':'VIRTUAL_IN_MEMORY_ONLY'}}
        except (Rejected,Undetermined) as err:
            result={'outcome':'REJECTED_BY_GATE' if isinstance(err,Rejected) else 'UNDETERMINED',
                    'reason':str(err), 'state':self.state(),
                    'trace':{'received_by':'BH-EDITOR-INPUT',
                             'authorized_by':'WH-COMMAND-GUARD',
                             'action_clause':rule['gate_id'] if rule else None,
                             'gate_families':self.gates_for.get(action,[]),
                             'effect_scope':'NO_EXTERNAL_EFFECT'}}
        self.last_trace = result
        return result

    def reasoning_prompt(self):
        """A portable prompt for an *independent* LLM; contains no Python source."""
        require(self.last_packet is not None, 'Send an action first')
        p=self.last_packet
        return ('You are an Anchor logical interpreter, NOT a code runner. Do not execute code. '
                'Given the canonical Anchor meanings, the Gate rules, the COMPLETE known state, '
                'and one stimulus, predict the resulting state and observable response. '
                'Return JSON with outcome (PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS, REJECTED_BY_GATE, or UNDETERMINED), '
                'reason, updated state, action result and the Gate IDs applied. '
                'Host effects are SIMULATED, not real. Missing observations yield UNDETERMINED. '
                'Apply atomic mutation, revision, dirty, undo/redo, and rejection invariants. '
                'This is a blind interpretation task: do not use Python implementation source, '
                'a known answer, or executable tools.\n\n'
                'CANONICAL TYPES:\n'+json.dumps(self.contract['anchor_definition_types'],ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n\n'
                'SHARED SEMANTICS:\n'+json.dumps(self.contract['semantics'],ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n\n'
                'STIMULUS PACKET:\n'+json.dumps(p,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n')

```

## File: notepad/logical_shell.py

```text
"""Local preview shell for the independent Anchor/Gate logical interpreter.

Run: python logical_shell.py --port 8765
Open: http://127.0.0.1:8765/

Single-user, in-memory experiment. No model API, filesystem save, cross-site
requests, or OS clipboard effects are made by the interpreter. The browser may
read an explicitly chosen file and offer a download on a user click.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
import json
from pathlib import Path
from threading import RLock
from urllib.parse import urlparse
from logical_engine import LogicalMachine, Rejected, virtual_path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MAX_BODY = 262144


class LocalHost(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    allow_reuse_address=True
    daemon_threads=True
    def __init__(self, address, *args):
        super().__init__(address, *args)
        self.machine=LogicalMachine('Hello, Anchor!')
        self.lock=RLock()


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server: LocalHost
    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        pass

    def _send(self, status, content, media):
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Type',media)
        self.send_header('Cache-Control','no-store')
        self.send_header('X-Content-Type-Options','nosniff')
        self.send_header('X-Frame-Options','DENY')
        self.send_header('Referrer-Policy','no-referrer')
        self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy',"default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'")
        self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(content)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(content)

    def _json(self, data, status=200):
        self._send(status,json.dumps(data,ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8'),'application/json; charset=utf-8')

    def _body(self):
        size=self.headers.get('Content-Length')
        if size is None or not size.isdigit() or int(size)>MAX_BODY:
            raise ValueError('Expected bounded JSON request')
        if self.headers.get('Content-Type','').split(';')[0].strip().lower() != 'application/json':
            raise ValueError('JSON content type required')
        origin=self.headers.get('Origin')
        if origin is not None and origin != f'http://{self.headers.get("Host")}':
            raise ValueError('Cross-origin request denied')
        data=json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(size)))
        if type(data) is not dict:raise ValueError('Expected JSON object')
        return data

    def do_GET(self):
        path=urlparse(self.path).path
        if path=='/':path='/shell.html'
        if path in ('/shell.html','/shell.css','/shell.js'):
            suffix=path.rsplit('.',1)[-1]
            media={'html':'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'css':'text/css; charset=utf-8',
                   'js':'application/javascript; charset=utf-8'}[suffix]
            self._send(200,(ROOT/path[1:]).read_bytes(),media)
            return
        with self.server.lock:
            if path=='/api/state':
                self._json({'outcome':'VIEW_ONLY','state':self.server.machine.state(),
                            'gate_family_count':self.server.machine.economy['gate_family_count'],
                            'anchor_count':self.server.machine.economy['anchor_count'],
                            'condition_clause_count':self.server.machine.economy['source_condition_count'],
                            'effect_profile':'VIRTUAL_ONLY'})
                return
            if path=='/api/packet':
                try:self._json({'prompt':self.server.machine.reasoning_prompt()})
                except Rejected as e:self._json({'error':str(e)},400)
                return
        self._json({'error':'Unknown route'},404)

    def do_POST(self):
        try:
            args=self._body()
            path=urlparse(self.path).path
            with self.server.lock:
                if path=='/api/command':
                    if set(args)!={'action','payload'}:raise ValueError('Action and payload required')
                    response=self.server.machine.send(args['action'],args['payload'])
                elif path=='/api/reset':
                    if args:raise ValueError('reset accepts empty object')
                    self.server.machine=LogicalMachine('Hello, Anchor!')
                    response={'outcome':'VIEW_ONLY','state':self.server.machine.state()}
                elif path=='/api/seed-file':
                    if set(args)!={'path','text'}:raise ValueError('Virtual file path and text required')
                    name=virtual_path(args['path'])
                    if type(args['text']) is not str:raise ValueError('Virtual file text must be a string')
                    self.server.machine.files[name]=args['text'].encode('utf-8')
                    response={'outcome':'VIRTUAL_FILE_SEEDED','state':self.server.machine.state()}
                else:
                    self._json({'error':'Unknown route'},404);return
            self._json(response)
        except (ValueError,Rejected,UnicodeError,json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
            self._json({'error':str(error)},400)


def main(argv=None):
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--port',type=int,default=8765)
    args=p.parse_args(argv)
    if not (1024<=args.port<=65535):p.error('Port must be 1024..65535')
    server=LocalHost(('127.0.0.1',args.port),Handler)
    print(f'Anchor Notepad logical shell — http://127.0.0.1:{args.port}/')
    print('Virtual-only host effects; no model API. Press Ctrl+C to stop.')
    try:server.serve_forever(poll_interval=.2)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:pass
    finally:server.server_close()

if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

```text
"""Fidelity 9 — WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE (WhiteHole).

A *build-time*, fail-closed conformance gate for the cumulative Anchor blueprint.
This is NOT a security sandbox, formal AnchorForge review, or KPP satisfaction.
It detects defined kinds of architectural drift before a coding agent presents
its work for review. It has no authority to dispatch editor commands and holds
no runtime document state.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import ast
from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BlueprintReport:
    valid: bool
    anchor_count: int
    obligation_count: int
    preserved_test_count: int
    violations: tuple[str, ...]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "gate": "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE",
            "valid": self.valid,
            "anchor_count": self.anchor_count,
            "obligation_count": self.obligation_count,
            "preserved_test_count": self.preserved_test_count,
            "violations": list(self.violations),
            "scope": "static architecture and declared evidence only; not formal proof",
        }


class WhiteHoleBlueprintGate:
    """WhiteHole gate for *candidate build* contracts, not user intents.

    The blueprint draft is the single source for responsibilities, flows,
    state owners, effects and obligations. `blueprint-manifest.json` maps these
    roles to source/tests and declares additional source-boundary invariants.
    A coding agent must keep both consistent; it cannot claim Baker acceptance.
    """

    anchor_id = "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE"
    canonical_type = "WhiteHole"

    def __init__(self, root: Path | str) -> None:
        self.root = Path(root)

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_json(path: Path, errors: list[str]) -> dict:
        try:
            value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if type(value) is not dict:
                raise ValueError("top level must be an object")
            return value
        except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError) as exc:
            errors.append(f"cannot read {path.name}: {exc}")
            return {}

    @staticmethod
    def _test_ids(tree: ast.AST, filename: str) -> set[str]:
        result: set[str] = set()
        for node in tree.body:
            if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                for method in node.body:
                    if isinstance(method, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)) and method.name.startswith("test_"):
                        result.add(f"{filename}::{node.name}.{method.name}")
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _declared_role(tree: ast.Module, class_name: str) -> tuple[object, object] | None:
        for node in tree.body:
            if not isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) or node.name != class_name:
                continue
            values = {}
            for member in node.body:
                if isinstance(member, ast.Assign) and isinstance(member.value, ast.Constant):
                    for target in member.targets:
                        if isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id in {"anchor_id", "canonical_type"}:
                            values[target.id] = member.value.value
            return values.get("anchor_id"), values.get("canonical_type")
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _stored_attributes(tree: ast.Module) -> set[str]:
        result: set[str] = set()
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and isinstance(node.ctx, (ast.Store, ast.Del)):
                if isinstance(node.value, ast.Name) and node.value.id == "self":
                    result.add(node.attr)
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _action_set(tree: ast.Module) -> set[str] | None:
        for klass in tree.body:
            if not isinstance(klass, ast.ClassDef) or klass.name != "WhiteHoleCommandGuard":
                continue
            for statement in klass.body:
                if not isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
                    continue
                if not any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == "supported_actions" for t in statement.targets):
                    continue
                value = statement.value
                if isinstance(value, ast.Call) and isinstance(value.func, ast.Name) and value.func.id == "frozenset" and len(value.args) == 1:
                    value = value.args[0]
                if isinstance(value, (ast.Set, ast.List, ast.Tuple)) and all(isinstance(x, ast.Constant) and type(x.value) is str for x in value.elts):
                    return {x.value for x in value.elts}
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _source_calls(tree: ast.Module) -> set[str]:
        result: set[str] = set()
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
                continue
            func = node.func
            if isinstance(func, ast.Name):
                result.add(func.id)
            elif isinstance(func, ast.Attribute):
                result.add(func.attr)
                if isinstance(func.value, ast.Name):
                    result.add(f"{func.value.id}.{func.attr}")
                elif (isinstance(func.value, ast.Attribute)
                      and isinstance(func.value.value, ast.Name)
                      and func.value.value.id == "self"):
                    result.add(f"{func.value.attr}.{func.attr}")
        return result

    def evaluate(self) -> BlueprintReport:
        errors: list[str] = []
        blueprint = self._parse_json(self.root / "anchorforge-spec.json", errors)
        manifest = self._parse_json(self.root / "blueprint-manifest.json", errors)
        roles = blueprint.get("responsibilities", [])
        sources = manifest.get("sources", {})
        obligations = blueprint.get("obligations", [])
        states = blueprint.get("state", [])
        effects = blueprint.get("effects", [])
        flows = blueprint.get("flows", [])
        anchor_count = len(roles) if isinstance(roles, list) else 0
        obligation_count = len(obligations) if isinstance(obligations, list) else 0
        if not blueprint or not manifest:
            return BlueprintReport(False, anchor_count, obligation_count, 0, tuple(errors))

        if blueprint.get("schema") != "anchorforge.organ-spec/1":
            errors.append("blueprint schema must be anchorforge.organ-spec/1 (draft)")
        if manifest.get("schema") != "notepad.blueprint-manifest/1":
            errors.append("unknown blueprint-manifest schema")
        depth = blueprint.get("fidelity")
        if type(depth) is not int or depth != manifest.get("fidelity") or depth != anchor_count:
            errors.append("fidelity, manifest fidelity and Anchor count must agree")
        if anchor_count != manifest.get("minimum_anchor_count") or anchor_count < 9:
            errors.append("Anchor count must not fall below preserved Fidelity 9 count")
        if not isinstance(roles, list) or not isinstance(sources, dict):
            errors.append("responsibilities must be list and source map object")
            return BlueprintReport(False, anchor_count, obligation_count, 0, tuple(errors))

        identities = [r.get("id") for r in roles if isinstance(r, dict)]
        known = set(identities)
        if len(identities) != len(roles) or len(known) != len(identities):
            errors.append("every Anchor must have one distinct ID")
        if known != set(sources):
            errors.append("every declared Anchor must map to exactly one implementation (and vice versa)")
        allowed_types = {"BlackHole", "Neutral", "WhiteHole"}
        kinds = {}
        for role in roles:
            if not isinstance(role, dict):
                errors.append("Anchor responsibilities must be objects")
                continue
            id_, kind = role.get("id"), role.get("definition")
            kinds[id_] = kind
            if kind not in allowed_types or not isinstance(role.get("responsibility"), str) or not role["responsibility"].strip():
                errors.append(f"{id_}: missing role type or responsibility truth")
        if kinds.get(self.anchor_id) != "WhiteHole":
            errors.append("Fidelity 9 gate must exist as a WhiteHole Anchor")
        if kinds.get("WH-COMMAND-GUARD") != "WhiteHole":
            errors.append("runtime command guard role has changed")

        trees = {}
        files_used = set()
        for id_, info in sources.items():
            if not isinstance(info, dict):
                errors.append(f"{id_}: source-map item must be an object")
                continue
            module, cls = info.get("file"), info.get("class")
            if not isinstance(module, str) or Path(module).name != module or not module.endswith(".py"):
                errors.append(f"{id_}: invalid source filename")
                continue
            if module in files_used:
                errors.append(f"{id_}: multiple Anchors share a mapped source module")
            files_used.add(module)
            try:
                tree = ast.parse((self.root / module).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=module)
            except (OSError, UnicodeError, SyntaxError) as exc:
                errors.append(f"{id_}: unable to parse {module}: {exc}")
                continue
            trees[id_] = tree
            actual = self._declared_role(tree, cls)
            if actual != (id_, kinds.get(id_)):
                errors.append(f"{id_}: source class {cls} does not declare matching anchor_id/canonical_type")

        owner_by_state = {}
        for state in states if isinstance(states, list) else []:
            if not isinstance(state, dict) or not isinstance(state.get("id"), str):
                errors.append("invalid declared state entry")
                continue
            s, owner = state.get("id"), state.get("owner")
            if s in owner_by_state:
                errors.append(f"duplicate state identity: {s}")
            owner_by_state[s] = owner
            if owner not in known or kinds.get(owner) != "Neutral":
                errors.append(f"state {s} must have exactly one Neutral owner")
        required_state = manifest.get("preserved_state_owners", {})
        for s, owner in required_state.items():
            if owner_by_state.get(s) != owner:
                errors.append(f"state owner drift for {s}: expected {owner}")

        for variable, expected in manifest.get("exclusive_state_attributes", {}).items():
            for id_, tree in trees.items():
                writes = variable in self._stored_attributes(tree)
                if writes and id_ != expected:
                    errors.append(f"{id_}: forbidden writer of {variable}; owner is {expected}")
            if expected in trees and variable not in self._stored_attributes(trees[expected]):
                errors.append(f"{expected}: missing declared owned state attribute {variable}")

        for i, effect in enumerate(effects if isinstance(effects, list) else []):
            if not isinstance(effect, dict):
                errors.append(f"effect {i}: not an object")
                continue
            name = effect.get("id", i)
            for key in ("requester", "guard", "executor"):
                if effect.get(key) not in known:
                    errors.append(f"effect {name}: unknown {key} Anchor")
            if effect.get("guard") != "WH-COMMAND-GUARD":
                errors.append(f"effect {name}: host effect must pass runtime command guard")
            if not str(effect.get("confirmation", "")).strip():
                errors.append(f"effect {name}: no confirmed success condition")
        preserved_effects = manifest.get("preserved_effects", {})
        seen_effects = {e.get("id"): e for e in effects if isinstance(e, dict)}
        for name, owner in preserved_effects.items():
            if seen_effects.get(name, {}).get("executor") != owner:
                errors.append(f"host effect {name} lost original executor {owner}")
        for flow in flows if isinstance(flows, list) else []:
            if not isinstance(flow, dict) or flow.get("from") not in known or flow.get("to") not in known or not str(flow.get("relationship", "")).strip():
                errors.append("flow has unknown endpoint or empty relationship")
        required_flows = manifest.get("required_flows", [])
        flow_pairs = {(f.get("from"), f.get("to")) for f in flows if isinstance(f, dict)}
        for a, b in required_flows:
            if (a, b) not in flow_pairs:
                errors.append(f"required flow missing: {a} -> {b}")

        by_id = {}
        for item in obligations if isinstance(obligations, list) else []:
            if not isinstance(item, dict) or not isinstance(item.get("id"), str):
                errors.append("obligations require stable string IDs")
                continue
            oid = item["id"]
            if oid in by_id:
                errors.append(f"duplicate obligation: {oid}")
            by_id[oid] = item
            if item.get("required") is not True or not str(item.get("statement", "")).strip() or not str(item.get("test", "")).strip():
                errors.append(f"obligation {oid} must be mandatory, testable and stated")
            if type(item.get("depth")) is not int or type(depth) is not int or not 1 <= item["depth"] <= depth:
                errors.append(f"obligation {oid} has invalid fidelity depth")
            anchors = item.get("design_anchors", [])
            if not anchors or any(a not in known for a in anchors):
                errors.append(f"obligation {oid} has missing/unknown Anchor coverage")
        for oid in manifest.get("preserved_obligation_ids", []):
            if oid not in by_id:
                errors.append(f"preserved obligation removed: {oid}")
        for id_ in known:
            if id_ not in {a for o in by_id.values() for a in o.get("design_anchors", [])}:
                errors.append(f"Anchor {id_} is not covered by any mandatory obligation")
        for oid, item in by_id.items():
            for prerequisite in item.get("depends", []):
                if prerequisite not in by_id or prerequisite == oid:
                    errors.append(f"obligation {oid}: invalid dependency {prerequisite}")
        pending, resolved = set(by_id), set()
        while pending:
            ready = {o for o in pending if all(dep in resolved for dep in by_id[o].get("depends", []))}
            if not ready:
                errors.append("obligation dependency cycle or missing dependency")
                break
            resolved.update(ready)
            pending -= ready

        # Concrete source-to-boundary checks. This is drift detection, not a
        # proof that a hostile coder cannot hide a forbidden operation.
        sensitive_calls = {
            "open": {"N-FILE-SESSION"},
            "os.replace": {"N-FILE-SESSION"},
            "NamedTemporaryFile": {"N-FILE-SESSION"},
            "clipboard_get": {"N-EDITOR-SURFACE"},
            "clipboard_clear": {"N-EDITOR-SURFACE"},
            "clipboard_append": {"N-EDITOR-SURFACE"},
        }
        for id_, tree in trees.items():
            calls = self._source_calls(tree)
            for name, permitted in sensitive_calls.items():
                if name in calls and id_ not in permitted:
                    errors.append(f"{id_}: forbidden host call {name}")
            for item in ast.walk(tree):
                if isinstance(item, ast.Import):
                    names = [n.name.split(".")[0] for n in item.names]
                elif isinstance(item, ast.ImportFrom):
                    names = [str(item.module).split(".")[0]]
                else:
                    continue
                for name in names:
                    if name in {"socket", "requests", "urllib", "http", "ftplib"}:
                        errors.append(f"{id_}: undeclared network module imported: {name}")
                    if name == "subprocess" and id_ != self.anchor_id:
                        errors.append(f"{id_}: subprocess use outside offline gate")
        if "WH-COMMAND-GUARD" in trees:
            actions = self._action_set(trees["WH-COMMAND-GUARD"])
            if actions != set(manifest.get("runtime_actions", [])):
                errors.append("runtime command allowlist drift: review all authorized actions")
        if "N-EDITOR-SURFACE" in trees:
            surface = trees["N-EDITOR-SURFACE"]
            calls = self._source_calls(surface)
            if "BlackHoleEditorInput.receive" not in calls or "guard.execute" not in calls:
                errors.append("surface no longer sends captured intent through WH guard")

        found_tests: set[str] = set()
        for filename in sorted(self.root.glob("test_*.py")):
            try:
                tree = ast.parse(filename.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename=filename.name)
            except (OSError, UnicodeError, SyntaxError) as exc:
                errors.append(f"cannot parse test {filename.name}: {exc}")
                continue
            found_tests |= self._test_ids(tree, filename.name)
        preserved_tests = set(manifest.get("preserved_test_ids", []))
        if len(preserved_tests) != len(manifest.get("preserved_test_ids", [])):
            errors.append("preservation test registry contains duplicates")
        missing = preserved_tests - found_tests
        if missing:
            errors.append(f"{len(missing)} inherited test method(s) removed or renamed: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing)[:5]))
        if not any(s.startswith("test_notepad_blueprint_gate.py::") for s in found_tests):
            errors.append("new blueprint gate has no discoverable tests")
        return BlueprintReport(not errors, anchor_count, obligation_count, len(preserved_tests), tuple(errors))


def _suite_is_complete(output: str, minimum_tests: int) -> bool:
    """Fail closed on no tests or skips: unittest exits 0 with skipped GUI tests."""
    match = re.search(r"Ran (\d+) tests? in ", output)
    if match is None or int(match.group(1)) < minimum_tests:
        return False
    skipped = re.search(r"skipped=(\d+)", output)
    return skipped is None or int(skipped.group(1)) == 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Validate candidate Notepad against the cumulative Anchor blueprint")
    parser.add_argument("--root", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent), help="Project root with Anchor spec and code")
    parser.add_argument("--verify", action="store_true", help="Also execute full discovered Python regression suite")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    root = Path(args.root)
    report = WhiteHoleBlueprintGate(root).evaluate()
    print(json.dumps(report.as_dict(), indent=2))
    if not report.valid:
        return 1
    if args.verify:
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "unittest", "discover", "-v", "-p", "test_*.py"]
        # Prefer a fresh virtual display on Linux even when DISPLAY is set:
        # DISPLAY may be stale or unreachable, causing all Tk tests to skip.
        if sys.platform.startswith("linux") and shutil.which("xvfb-run"):
            cmd = ["xvfb-run", "-a", *cmd]
        print("Running cumulative Python test suite:", " ".join(cmd), flush=True)
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=root, check=False, capture_output=True, text=True)
        print(result.stdout, end="", flush=True)
        print(result.stderr, end="", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            return result.returncode
        output = result.stdout + "\n" + result.stderr
        if not _suite_is_complete(output, report.preserved_test_count + 1):
            print("REJECTED: test discovery incomplete or one or more tests skipped; full cumulative verification not established.", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        print("VERIFIED: Python suite executed in full with zero skipped tests.")
        return 0
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

```

## File: notepad/notepad_clipboard.py

```text
"""Round 7: N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION (Neutral).

A document-bound, host-injected clipboard interaction capability. The Anchor
interprets document-relative selections and marshals explicit clipboard
reads/writes after WH-COMMAND-GUARD authorization. It does not own document
text, undo history, or an independent text buffer. The Tk selection is
transient presentation state, not a second authoritative text owner.

The clipboard is an external host effect. A successful write acknowledgement
must precede Cut's deletion; a successful text read must precede Paste's edit.
Only the host adapter has access to the operating-system clipboard.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections.abc import Callable

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory


class ClipboardOperationError(ValueError):
    """A clipboard interaction failed; no guarded document edit was requested."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SelectionRange:
    """Document-codepoint range [start, end), never an editable text copy."""
    start: int
    end: int


class NeutralClipboardSelection:
    """N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION: selection semantics and guarded host clipboard I/O."""

    anchor_id = "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(
        self,
        document: NeutralDocument,
        history: NeutralEditHistory,
        read_text: Callable[[], str],
        write_text: Callable[[str], None],
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(document, NeutralDocument):
            raise TypeError("clipboard selection requires N-DOCUMENT")
        if not isinstance(history, NeutralEditHistory):
            raise TypeError("clipboard selection requires N-EDIT-HISTORY")
        history.validate_for(document)
        if not callable(read_text) or not callable(write_text):
            raise TypeError("clipboard requires readable and writable host adapters")
        self._document = document
        self._history = history
        self._read_text = read_text
        self._write_text = write_text

    def validate_for(self, document: NeutralDocument, history: NeutralEditHistory) -> None:
        if document is not self._document or history is not self._history:
            raise ClipboardOperationError("clipboard selection belongs to another document/history")
        history.validate_for(document)

    @staticmethod
    def select_all(snapshot: DocumentSnapshot) -> SelectionRange:
        if not isinstance(snapshot, DocumentSnapshot):
            raise TypeError("selection requires a document snapshot")
        return SelectionRange(0, len(snapshot.text))

    def write_selection(self, snapshot: DocumentSnapshot, selected: SelectionRange) -> None:
        """Write exactly the selected text; never modify document/history."""
        try:
            self._write_text(snapshot.text[selected.start:selected.end])
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ClipboardOperationError(f"clipboard write failed: {exc}") from exc

    def read_selection_text(self) -> str:
        """Read plain text from host; reject incompatible/missing content."""
        try:
            text = self._read_text()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ClipboardOperationError(f"clipboard read failed: {exc}") from exc
        if type(text) is not str:
            raise ClipboardOperationError("clipboard does not contain plain text")
        return text
```
