## File: notepad/notepad_document.py

```text
"""Notepad Round 2: N-DOCUMENT (Neutral).

Owns an in-memory plain-text document. A valid internal call updates document
state atomically; it does not interpret raw BlackHole intents, authorize a
user command, read/write files, or drive a UI. WH-COMMAND-GUARD authorizes untrusted command dispatch before this
Anchor changes state. N-FILE-SESSION invokes trusted baseline hooks.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DocumentSnapshot:
    """Immutable observation of the document's current state."""
    text: str
    revision: int
    dirty: bool


class NeutralDocument:
    """Neutral N-DOCUMENT: the sole owner of document content and baseline."""

    anchor_id = "N-DOCUMENT"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(self, initial_text: str = "") -> None:
        if not isinstance(initial_text, str):
            raise TypeError("initial_text must be a string")
        self._text = initial_text
        self._baseline_text = initial_text
        self._revision = 0

    def snapshot(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Return a point-in-time value; do not expose mutable state."""
        return DocumentSnapshot(
            text=self._text,
            revision=self._revision,
            dirty=self._text != self._baseline_text,
        )

    def replace_text(self, text: str) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Replace the content. Identical content is a no-op."""
        self._require_text(text)
        self._commit(text)
        return self.snapshot()

    def insert_text(self, offset: int, text: str) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Insert Unicode text at a code-point offset in the current text."""
        self._require_index(offset, "offset")
        self._require_text(text)
        if offset > len(self._text):
            raise ValueError("offset is beyond the end of the document")
        self._commit(self._text[:offset] + text + self._text[offset:])
        return self.snapshot()

    def delete_range(self, start: int, end: int) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Delete Unicode code points in the half-open range [start, end)."""
        self._require_index(start, "start")
        self._require_index(end, "end")
        if start > end or end > len(self._text):
            raise ValueError("invalid document range")
        self._commit(self._text[:start] + self._text[end:])
        return self.snapshot()

    def load_clean_text(self, text: str) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Trusted internal API: replace with the content of a new/opened file.

        Reset the clean baseline, maintaining sole ownership of text/revision.
        The caller must reset the bound edit history after this transaction.
        """
        self._require_text(text)
        self._commit(text)
        self._baseline_text = text
        return self.snapshot()

    def mark_saved(self, expected_revision: int) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Trusted internal API: acknowledge a completed write of this revision."""
        if type(expected_revision) is not int or expected_revision != self._revision:
            raise ValueError("document changed during save")
        self._baseline_text = self._text
        return self.snapshot()

    @staticmethod
    def _require_text(text: str) -> None:
        if not isinstance(text, str):
            raise TypeError("text must be a string")

    @staticmethod
    def _require_index(index: int, label: str) -> None:
        if type(index) is not int:
            raise TypeError(f"{label} must be an integer")
        if index < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{label} must be nonnegative")

    def _commit(self, updated: str) -> None:
        if updated != self._text:
            self._text = updated
            self._revision += 1

```

## File: notepad/notepad_files.py

```text
"""Round 6: N-FILE-SESSION (Neutral) — file association and persistence.

Only the WH-COMMAND-GUARD may dispatch untrusted file intents here. This is
not a standalone security sandbox: its methods are trusted implementation
APIs. The GUI asks for human confirmation, then submits explicit proof flags
that the WhiteHole checks before any destructive change.

Plain-text UTF-8 files only. Writes are prepared in a sibling temporary file
and atomically replace their target when supported by the host filesystem.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory


class FileSessionError(ValueError):
    """File or association change rejected; no successful save was recorded."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FileSessionSnapshot:
    path: str | None


class NeutralFileSession:
    """Neutral N-FILE-SESSION: owns active file association and disk I/O."""

    anchor_id = "N-FILE-SESSION"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(self, document: NeutralDocument, history: NeutralEditHistory) -> None:
        if not isinstance(document, NeutralDocument):
            raise TypeError("file session requires N-DOCUMENT")
        if not isinstance(history, NeutralEditHistory):
            raise TypeError("file session requires N-EDIT-HISTORY")
        history.validate_for(document)
        self._document = document
        self._history = history
        self._path: str | None = None
        self._expected_hash: str | None = None

    def snapshot(self) -> FileSessionSnapshot:
        return FileSessionSnapshot(self._path)

    def validate_for(self, document: NeutralDocument, history: NeutralEditHistory) -> None:
        if document is not self._document or history is not self._history:
            raise FileSessionError("file session belongs to another document/history")
        history.validate_for(document)

    @staticmethod
    def require_path(path: object) -> str:
        """Explicit absolute regular-file destination/source only."""
        if type(path) is not str or not path or "\x00" in path or not os.path.isabs(path):
            raise FileSessionError("file path must be an absolute nonempty string")
        normalized = os.path.abspath(path)
        if os.path.isdir(normalized):
            raise FileSessionError("a directory cannot be used as a text file")
        return normalized

    def new_document(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        snapshot = self._document.load_clean_text("")
        self._history.reset_for_document_load(self._document)
        self._path = None
        self._expected_hash = None
        return snapshot

    def open_file(self, path: str) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        normalized = self.require_path(path)
        try:
            # Read/decode completely before mutating any document/session state.
            with open(normalized, "rb") as handle:
                data = handle.read()
            text = data.decode("utf-8")
        except (OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
            raise FileSessionError(f"cannot open UTF-8 text file: {exc}") from exc
        snapshot = self._document.load_clean_text(text)
        self._history.reset_for_document_load(self._document)
        self._path = normalized
        self._expected_hash = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        return snapshot

    def save_file(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        if self._path is None:
            raise FileSessionError("untitled document requires Save As")
        return self._write(self._path, overwrite=True)

    def save_file_as(self, path: str, overwrite: bool) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self._write(self.require_path(path), overwrite=overwrite)

    def _write(self, path: str, overwrite: bool) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        if type(overwrite) is not bool:
            raise FileSessionError("overwrite confirmation must be a boolean")
        path = self.require_path(path)
        snapshot = self._document.snapshot()
        data = snapshot.text.encode("utf-8")
        temporary: str | None = None
        try:
            # Reject symlink targets and refuse to overwrite an unknown file
            # without an explicit overwrite grant from the GUI/operator.
            if os.path.islink(path):
                raise FileSessionError("refusing to overwrite a symbolic link")
            exists = os.path.exists(path)
            if exists and not os.path.isfile(path):
                raise FileSessionError("target is not a regular file")
            if exists and not overwrite:
                raise FileSessionError("existing file requires overwrite confirmation")
            if path == self._path and self._expected_hash is not None:
                if not exists:
                    raise FileSessionError("the original file disappeared since opening/saving")
                with open(path, "rb") as handle:
                    current_hash = hashlib.sha256(handle.read()).hexdigest()
                if current_hash != self._expected_hash:
                    raise FileSessionError("file changed externally; use Save As to another path")
            folder = os.path.dirname(path)
            if not os.path.isdir(folder):
                raise FileSessionError("destination folder does not exist")
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode="wb", dir=folder, prefix=".anchornotepad-",
                                             suffix=".tmp", delete=False) as handle:
                temporary = handle.name
                handle.write(data)
                handle.flush()
                os.fsync(handle.fileno())
            os.replace(temporary, path)
            temporary = None
        except (OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
            raise FileSessionError(f"unable to save file: {exc}") from exc
        finally:
            if temporary is not None:
                try:
                    os.unlink(temporary)
                except OSError:
                    pass
        # No clean baseline or file association until the host confirms a write.
        self._path = path
        self._expected_hash = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        return self._document.mark_saved(snapshot.revision)

```

```text
"""Notepad Round 8: WH-COMMAND-GUARD (WhiteHole).

BH-EDITOR-INPUT => WH-COMMAND-GUARD => N-DOCUMENT, optionally coordinating
N-EDIT-HISTORY, N-FILE-SESSION, N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION and N-SEARCH-REPLACE. Direct Neutral methods are trusted
internal APIs and are not security barriers; the host/UI routes untrusted
commands through this WhiteHole. Only validated, opt-in file operations
and clipboard effects are authorized; network effects remain denied.
"""

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument
from notepad_history import HistoryConflict, NeutralEditHistory
from notepad_files import FileSessionError, NeutralFileSession
from notepad_clipboard import ClipboardOperationError, NeutralClipboardSelection, SelectionRange
from notepad_search import MatchRange, NeutralSearchReplace, ReplaceResult, SearchOperationError
from notepad_input import EditorIntent


class CommandRejected(ValueError):
    """The command was not authorized; no document update was requested."""


class WhiteHoleCommandGuard:
    """WhiteHole WH-COMMAND-GUARD: validate, authorize, dispatch."""

    anchor_id = "WH-COMMAND-GUARD"
    canonical_type = "WhiteHole"
    supported_actions = frozenset({"edit", "insert", "delete", "read", "undo", "redo", "new", "open", "save", "save-as", "copy", "cut", "paste", "select-all", "find", "replace-one", "replace-all"})

    def execute(
        self,
        intent: EditorIntent,
        document: NeutralDocument,
        history: NeutralEditHistory | None = None,
        file_session: NeutralFileSession | None = None,
        clipboard: NeutralClipboardSelection | None = None,
        search: NeutralSearchReplace | None = None,
    ) -> DocumentSnapshot | SelectionRange | MatchRange | ReplaceResult | None:
        """Process one validated in-memory command with optional undo history.

        Backward compatible: edit/insert/delete/read without a history behave
        exactly as in Round 3. Undo/redo require a bound, current history.
        Clipboard commands require an explicitly bound N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION.
        """
        if not isinstance(intent, EditorIntent):
            raise CommandRejected("expected a captured EditorIntent")
        if not isinstance(document, NeutralDocument):
            raise CommandRejected("expected an N-DOCUMENT owner")
        action = intent.action
        if type(action) is not str or action not in self.supported_actions:
            raise CommandRejected("unsupported editor command")
        if history is not None and not isinstance(history, NeutralEditHistory):
            raise CommandRejected("expected N-EDIT-HISTORY")

        payload = intent.payload
        if action in ("find", "replace-one", "replace-all"):
            if history is None or not isinstance(search, NeutralSearchReplace):
                raise CommandRejected("search actions require an explicitly bound search owner")
            try:
                search.validate_for(document, history)
            except (SearchOperationError, HistoryConflict) as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            before = document.snapshot()
            if action == "find":
                self._require_fields(payload, {"query", "start", "match_case", "wrap", "backwards"})
                self._require_index(payload["start"], "start")
                if payload["start"] > len(before.text):
                    raise CommandRejected("search start exceeds document length")
                self._require_bool(payload["match_case"], "find.match_case")
                self._require_bool(payload["wrap"], "find.wrap")
                self._require_bool(payload["backwards"], "find.backwards")
                try:
                    return search.find(before, payload["query"], payload["start"],
                                       payload["match_case"], payload["wrap"], payload["backwards"])
                except SearchOperationError as exc:
                    raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            if action == "replace-one":
                self._require_fields(payload, {"query", "replacement", "start", "end", "match_case"})
                self._require_index(payload["start"], "start")
                self._require_index(payload["end"], "end")
                if type(payload["replacement"]) is not str:
                    raise CommandRejected("replacement must be plain text")
                try:
                    matched = search.selected_match(before, payload["query"], payload["start"],
                                                    payload["end"], payload["match_case"])
                except SearchOperationError as exc:
                    raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
                if not matched:
                    raise CommandRejected("selected text does not match the search query")
                after = document.replace_text(before.text[:payload["start"]]
                                              + payload["replacement"] + before.text[payload["end"]:])
                history.record_transition(before, after)
                return ReplaceResult(after, 1)
            self._require_fields(payload, {"query", "replacement", "match_case"})
            self._require_bool(payload["match_case"], "replace-all.match_case")
            try:
                updated, count = search.replace_all_text(before, payload["query"],
                                                         payload["replacement"], payload["match_case"])
            except SearchOperationError as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            after = document.replace_text(updated)
            history.record_transition(before, after)
            return ReplaceResult(after, count)
        if action in ("copy", "cut", "paste", "select-all"):
            if history is None or not isinstance(clipboard, NeutralClipboardSelection):
                raise CommandRejected("clipboard actions require an explicitly bound clipboard selection")
            try:
                clipboard.validate_for(document, history)
            except (ClipboardOperationError, HistoryConflict) as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            before = document.snapshot()
            if action == "select-all":
                if payload is not None:
                    raise CommandRejected("select-all takes no payload")
                return clipboard.select_all(before)
            self._require_fields(payload, {"start", "end"})
            start, end = payload["start"], payload["end"]
            self._require_index(start, "start")
            self._require_index(end, "end")
            if start > end or end > len(before.text):
                raise CommandRejected("invalid clipboard selection range")
            if action in ("copy", "cut") and start == end:
                raise CommandRejected("copy/cut requires selected text")
            try:
                if action == "paste":
                    inserted = clipboard.read_selection_text()
                else:
                    # Before Cut changes text, the host must confirm copying.
                    clipboard.write_selection(before, SelectionRange(start, end))
                # Host callbacks are externally supplied. Recheck state after
                # effects, preventing edits to a stale range/history.
                history.validate_for(document)
                if document.snapshot() != before:
                    raise CommandRejected("document changed during clipboard operation")
            except (ClipboardOperationError, HistoryConflict) as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            if action == "copy":
                return before
            if action == "cut":
                after = document.delete_range(start, end)
            else:
                after = document.replace_text(before.text[:start] + inserted + before.text[end:])
            history.record_transition(before, after)
            return after
        if action in ("new", "open", "save", "save-as"):
            if history is None or file_session is None or not isinstance(file_session, NeutralFileSession):
                raise CommandRejected("file operations require an explicitly bound file session")
            try:
                file_session.validate_for(document, history)
            except (FileSessionError, HistoryConflict) as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            if action == "new":
                self._require_fields(payload, {"discard"})
                self._require_bool(payload["discard"], "new.discard")
                if document.snapshot().dirty and not payload["discard"]:
                    raise CommandRejected("unsaved changes require confirmed discard")
                return file_session.new_document()
            if action == "open":
                self._require_fields(payload, {"path", "discard"})
                self._require_bool(payload["discard"], "open.discard")
                if document.snapshot().dirty and not payload["discard"]:
                    raise CommandRejected("unsaved changes require confirmed discard")
                path = self._require_path(payload["path"])
                try:
                    return file_session.open_file(path)
                except FileSessionError as exc:
                    raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            if action == "save":
                if payload is not None:
                    raise CommandRejected("save takes no payload")
                try:
                    return file_session.save_file()
                except FileSessionError as exc:
                    raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
            self._require_fields(payload, {"path", "overwrite"})
            self._require_bool(payload["overwrite"], "save-as.overwrite")
            path = self._require_path(payload["path"])
            try:
                return file_session.save_file_as(path, payload["overwrite"])
            except FileSessionError as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc

        if action in ("undo", "redo", "read"):
            if payload is not None:
                raise CommandRejected(f"{action} takes no payload")
            if action == "read":
                return document.snapshot()
            if history is None:
                raise CommandRejected(f"{action} requires editing history")
            try:
                history.validate_for(document)
                return history.undo() if action == "undo" else history.redo()
            except HistoryConflict as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc

        # Validate the entire command payload before requesting any state change.
        if action == "edit":
            if type(payload) is not str:
                raise CommandRejected("edit requires plain text")
        elif action == "insert":
            self._require_fields(payload, {"offset", "text"})
            self._require_index(payload["offset"], "offset")
            if type(payload["text"]) is not str:
                raise CommandRejected("insert.text must be plain text")
            if payload["offset"] > len(document.snapshot().text):
                raise CommandRejected("insert.offset exceeds document length")
        else:
            self._require_fields(payload, {"start", "end"})
            self._require_index(payload["start"], "start")
            self._require_index(payload["end"], "end")
            if payload["start"] > payload["end"] or payload["end"] > len(document.snapshot().text):
                raise CommandRejected("invalid delete range")

        if history is not None:
            try:
                history.validate_for(document)
            except HistoryConflict as exc:
                raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc
        before = document.snapshot()
        if action == "edit":
            after = document.replace_text(payload)
        elif action == "insert":
            after = document.insert_text(payload["offset"], payload["text"])
        else:
            after = document.delete_range(payload["start"], payload["end"])
        if history is not None:
            history.record_transition(before, after)
        return after

    @staticmethod
    def _require_bool(value: object, label: str) -> None:
        if type(value) is not bool:
            raise CommandRejected(f"{label} must be a boolean")

    @staticmethod
    def _require_path(value: object) -> str:
        try:
            return NeutralFileSession.require_path(value)
        except FileSessionError as exc:
            raise CommandRejected(str(exc)) from exc

    @staticmethod
    def _require_fields(payload: object, expected: set[str]) -> None:
        if type(payload) is not dict or payload.keys() != expected:
            raise CommandRejected(f"payload must have exactly {sorted(expected)}")

    @staticmethod
    def _require_index(value: object, label: str) -> None:
        if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
            raise CommandRejected(f"{label} must be a nonnegative integer")
```

## File: notepad/notepad_history.py

```text
"""Round 4, N-EDIT-HISTORY (Neutral): bounded, document-bound undo/redo.

History owns the transition stacks, not document text, clean baseline, or
revision. Untrusted user intents must enter through WH-COMMAND-GUARD. This
module does not perform filesystem, clipboard, or network effects.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument


class HistoryConflict(ValueError):
    """History is stale, mismatched, or has no requested transition."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HistorySnapshot:
    """Read-only counts and the revision of the bound document."""

    undo_count: int
    redo_count: int
    expected_revision: int


class NeutralEditHistory:
    """Neutral N-EDIT-HISTORY: owns bounded undo/redo transitions."""

    anchor_id = "N-EDIT-HISTORY"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(self, document: NeutralDocument, max_entries: int = 100) -> None:
        if not isinstance(document, NeutralDocument):
            raise TypeError("history requires N-DOCUMENT")
        if type(max_entries) is not int or max_entries < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_entries must be a positive integer")
        self._document = document
        self._max_entries = max_entries
        self._undo: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self._redo: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self._expected_revision = document.snapshot().revision

    def snapshot(self) -> HistorySnapshot:
        return HistorySnapshot(len(self._undo), len(self._redo), self._expected_revision)

    def validate_for(self, document: NeutralDocument) -> None:
        """Refuse stale histories or histories attached to another document."""
        if document is not self._document:
            raise HistoryConflict("history belongs to another document")
        if document.snapshot().revision != self._expected_revision:
            raise HistoryConflict("document changed outside this history")

    def reset_for_document_load(self, document: NeutralDocument) -> None:
        """Trusted internal hook: new/open establishes a fresh edit timeline."""
        if document is not self._document:
            raise HistoryConflict("history belongs to another document")
        self._undo.clear()
        self._redo.clear()
        self._expected_revision = document.snapshot().revision

    def record_transition(self, before: DocumentSnapshot, after: DocumentSnapshot) -> None:
        """Record an already-applied, validated, document transition.

        This is a trusted internal hook, called only after WhiteHole dispatch.
        """
        if not isinstance(before, DocumentSnapshot) or not isinstance(after, DocumentSnapshot):
            raise HistoryConflict("record requires snapshots")
        if before.revision != self._expected_revision:
            raise HistoryConflict("unexpected history starting revision")
        if after != self._document.snapshot():
            raise HistoryConflict("transition does not match current document")
        if after.text == before.text:
            if after.revision != before.revision:
                raise HistoryConflict("no-op advanced the document revision")
            return
        if after.revision != before.revision + 1:
            raise HistoryConflict("edit must be a single document revision")
        self._undo.append((before.text, after.text))
        if len(self._undo) > self._max_entries:
            self._undo.pop(0)
        self._redo.clear()
        self._expected_revision = after.revision

    def undo(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Restore prior text via the document's owner; preserve its baseline."""
        self.validate_for(self._document)
        if not self._undo:
            raise HistoryConflict("nothing to undo")
        previous_text, resulting_text = self._undo[-1]
        if self._document.snapshot().text != resulting_text:
            raise HistoryConflict("document does not match recorded edit")
        result = self._document.replace_text(previous_text)
        self._undo.pop()
        self._redo.append((previous_text, resulting_text))
        self._expected_revision = result.revision
        return result

    def redo(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Reapply the most recently undone edit through N-DOCUMENT."""
        self.validate_for(self._document)
        if not self._redo:
            raise HistoryConflict("nothing to redo")
        previous_text, resulting_text = self._redo[-1]
        if self._document.snapshot().text != previous_text:
            raise HistoryConflict("document does not match recorded undo")
        result = self._document.replace_text(resulting_text)
        self._redo.pop()
        self._undo.append((previous_text, resulting_text))
        self._expected_revision = result.revision
        return result
```

```text
"""Notepad — Round 1, BH-EDITOR-INPUT.

This module implements exactly one Anchor application. It captures raw editor
intent without validating, applying, persisting, or otherwise interpreting it.
Those operations belong to future WhiteHole and Neutral applications.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EditorIntent:
    """An input observation. Not an approved or executed command."""
    action: Any
    payload: Any = None


class BlackHoleEditorInput:
    """BlackHole BH-EDITOR-INPUT: receive a user/editor input event."""

    anchor_id = "BH-EDITOR-INPUT"
    canonical_type = "BlackHole"

    @staticmethod
    def receive(action: Any, payload: Any = None) -> EditorIntent:
        """Capture inputs as supplied; do not interpret, guard, or mutate them."""
        return EditorIntent(action=action, payload=payload)
```

## File: notepad/notepad_search.py

```text
"""Round 8: N-SEARCH-REPLACE (Neutral).

Document/history-bound, stateless literal substring search and replacement
semantics. WH validates every untrusted request and authorizes edits. This
Anchor never owns document text, revision, clean baseline, or undo history.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import re

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory


class SearchOperationError(ValueError):
    """Search parameters or a requested match are invalid."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MatchRange:
    start: int
    end: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReplaceResult:
    snapshot: DocumentSnapshot
    count: int


class NeutralSearchReplace:
    """N-SEARCH-REPLACE: calculate literal matches, not document state."""

    anchor_id = "N-SEARCH-REPLACE"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(self, document: NeutralDocument, history: NeutralEditHistory):
        if not isinstance(document, NeutralDocument):
            raise TypeError("search requires N-DOCUMENT")
        if not isinstance(history, NeutralEditHistory):
            raise TypeError("search requires N-EDIT-HISTORY")
        history.validate_for(document)
        self._document = document
        self._history = history

    def validate_for(self, document: NeutralDocument, history: NeutralEditHistory):
        if document is not self._document or history is not self._history:
            raise SearchOperationError("search belongs to another document/history")
        history.validate_for(document)

    @staticmethod
    def _pattern(query: str, match_case: bool) -> re.Pattern:
        if type(query) is not str or not query:
            raise SearchOperationError("search query must be nonempty plain text")
        if type(match_case) is not bool:
            raise SearchOperationError("match_case must be a boolean")
        return re.compile(re.escape(query), 0 if match_case else re.IGNORECASE)

    @classmethod
    def find(cls, snapshot: DocumentSnapshot, query: str, start: int,
             match_case: bool, wrap: bool, backwards: bool = False) -> MatchRange | None:
        """Find next/previous non-overlapping literal occurrence, with wrap."""
        if not isinstance(snapshot, DocumentSnapshot):
            raise SearchOperationError("expected document snapshot")
        if type(start) is not int or not 0 <= start <= len(snapshot.text):
            raise SearchOperationError("start must be a valid document codepoint offset")
        if type(wrap) is not bool or type(backwards) is not bool:
            raise SearchOperationError("wrap/backwards must be booleans")
        pattern = cls._pattern(query, match_case)
        matches = tuple(pattern.finditer(snapshot.text))
        if backwards:
            chosen = next((m for m in reversed(matches) if m.end() <= start), None)
            if chosen is None and wrap:
                chosen = next((m for m in reversed(matches) if m.end() > start), None)
        else:
            chosen = next((m for m in matches if m.start() >= start), None)
            if chosen is None and wrap:
                chosen = next((m for m in matches if m.start() < start), None)
        return MatchRange(chosen.start(), chosen.end()) if chosen is not None else None

    @classmethod
    def selected_match(cls, snapshot: DocumentSnapshot, query: str,
                       start: int, end: int, match_case: bool) -> bool:
        pattern = cls._pattern(query, match_case)
        if type(start) is not int or type(end) is not int or not 0 <= start < end <= len(snapshot.text):
            raise SearchOperationError("replacement requires a valid selected match")
        return pattern.fullmatch(snapshot.text[start:end]) is not None

    @classmethod
    def replace_all_text(cls, snapshot: DocumentSnapshot, query: str,
                         replacement: str, match_case: bool) -> tuple[str, int]:
        if not isinstance(snapshot, DocumentSnapshot):
            raise SearchOperationError("expected document snapshot")
        if type(replacement) is not str:
            raise SearchOperationError("replacement must be plain text")
        pattern = cls._pattern(query, match_case)
        # Lambda avoids interpreting replacement as a regex substitution template.
        return pattern.subn(lambda _match: replacement, snapshot.text)

```

## File: notepad/notepad_surface.py

```text
"""Round 8 extension of N-EDITOR-SURFACE (Neutral): desktop Notepad.

The window is a projection of the existing document state, not an additional
owner of text, clean baseline, or editing history. Every user-driven text
change and undo/redo operation is captured by BH-EDITOR-INPUT and authorized
by WH-COMMAND-GUARD before the document owner changes.

Round 6 opts the GUI into guarded file operations via N-FILE-SESSION.
Round 7 routes clipboard commands via N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION and WH.
Round 8 routes Find/Replace via N-SEARCH-REPLACE and WH.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable

from notepad_document import DocumentSnapshot, NeutralDocument
from notepad_guard import CommandRejected, WhiteHoleCommandGuard
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory
from notepad_files import NeutralFileSession
from notepad_input import BlackHoleEditorInput
from notepad_clipboard import NeutralClipboardSelection, SelectionRange
from notepad_search import NeutralSearchReplace, MatchRange, ReplaceResult


class NeutralEditorSurface:
    """Neutral N-EDITOR-SURFACE: map UI interactions to guarded core actions.

    This controller intentionally does not keep a parallel editable text
    value. Tk widgets may transiently display keystrokes, but after every
    dispatch their content is projected from the authoritative snapshot.
    """

    anchor_id = "N-EDITOR-SURFACE"
    canonical_type = "Neutral"

    def __init__(
        self,
        document: NeutralDocument | None = None,
        history: NeutralEditHistory | None = None,
        guard: WhiteHoleCommandGuard | None = None,
        file_session: NeutralFileSession | None = None,
        clipboard: NeutralClipboardSelection | None = None,
        search: NeutralSearchReplace | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.document = document if document is not None else NeutralDocument()
        self.history = history if history is not None else NeutralEditHistory(self.document)
        self.guard = guard if guard is not None else WhiteHoleCommandGuard()
        if not isinstance(self.document, NeutralDocument):
            raise TypeError("surface requires N-DOCUMENT")
        if not isinstance(self.history, NeutralEditHistory):
            raise TypeError("surface requires N-EDIT-HISTORY")
        if not isinstance(self.guard, WhiteHoleCommandGuard):
            raise TypeError("surface requires WH-COMMAND-GUARD")
        self.history.validate_for(self.document)
        if file_session is not None and not isinstance(file_session, NeutralFileSession):
            raise TypeError("surface requires N-FILE-SESSION")
        if file_session is not None:
            file_session.validate_for(self.document, self.history)
        self.file_session = file_session
        if clipboard is not None and not isinstance(clipboard, NeutralClipboardSelection):
            raise TypeError("surface requires N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION")
        if clipboard is not None:
            clipboard.validate_for(self.document, self.history)
        self.clipboard = clipboard
        if search is not None and not isinstance(search, NeutralSearchReplace):
            raise TypeError("surface requires N-SEARCH-REPLACE")
        if search is not None:
            search.validate_for(self.document, self.history)
        self.search = search

    def enable_files(self) -> None:
        """Explicitly opt in to the guarded file operations capability."""
        if self.file_session is None:
            self.file_session = NeutralFileSession(self.document, self.history)

    def enable_clipboard(self, read_text: Callable[[], str],
                         write_text: Callable[[str], None]) -> None:
        """Opt in to the current host's explicitly guarded clipboard adapter."""
        if self.clipboard is not None:
            raise ValueError("clipboard adapter already enabled")
        self.clipboard = NeutralClipboardSelection(
            self.document, self.history, read_text, write_text
        )

    def enable_search(self) -> None:
        """Explicit opt-in for document/history-bound search capability."""
        if self.search is None:
            self.search = NeutralSearchReplace(self.document, self.history)

    def find(self, query: str, start: int = 0, match_case: bool = False,
             wrap: bool = True, backwards: bool = False) -> MatchRange | None:
        return self.dispatch("find", {"query": query, "start": start,
                                      "match_case": match_case, "wrap": wrap,
                                      "backwards": backwards})

    def replace_one(self, query: str, replacement: str, start: int, end: int,
                    match_case: bool = False) -> ReplaceResult:
        return self.dispatch("replace-one", {"query": query, "replacement": replacement,
                                             "start": start, "end": end, "match_case": match_case})

    def replace_all(self, query: str, replacement: str,
                    match_case: bool = False) -> ReplaceResult:
        return self.dispatch("replace-all", {"query": query, "replacement": replacement,
                                             "match_case": match_case})

    def copy_selection(self, start: int, end: int) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("copy", {"start": start, "end": end})

    def cut_selection(self, start: int, end: int) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("cut", {"start": start, "end": end})

    def paste_at(self, start: int, end: int) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("paste", {"start": start, "end": end})

    def select_all(self) -> SelectionRange:
        return self.dispatch("select-all")

    def new_document(self, discard: bool = False) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("new", {"discard": discard})

    def open_file(self, path: str, discard: bool = False) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("open", {"path": path, "discard": discard})

    def save_file(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("save")

    def save_file_as(self, path: str, overwrite: bool = False) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("save-as", {"path": path, "overwrite": overwrite})

    def dispatch(self, action: str, payload: object = None) -> DocumentSnapshot | SelectionRange | MatchRange | ReplaceResult | None:
        """BH captures; WH validates; Neutral state owners apply/read."""
        intent = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive(action, payload)
        return self.guard.execute(intent, self.document, self.history, self.file_session, self.clipboard, self.search)

    def display(self, sink: Callable[[DocumentSnapshot], None]) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        """Render only a guarded read of authoritative document state."""
        if not callable(sink):
            raise TypeError("display sink must be callable")
        snap = self.dispatch("read")
        sink(snap)
        return snap

    def text_changed(self, text: str) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("edit", text)

    def undo(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("undo")

    def redo(self) -> DocumentSnapshot:
        return self.dispatch("redo")

    def can_exit(self, confirm_discard: Callable[[], bool]) -> bool:
        """Prevent accidental data loss without inventing a save capability."""
        if not self.dispatch("read").dirty:
            return True
        if not callable(confirm_discard):
            raise TypeError("confirmation callback must be callable")
        return confirm_discard() is True


class TkNotepadWindow:
    """Tk host adapter for the one N-EDITOR-SURFACE Anchor.

    Toolkit display interaction is a host effect. This adapter is neither a
    second Anchor nor a state owner; it only forwards user intents and paints
    the resulting snapshots. File and clipboard effects require WH authorization.
    """

    # These unsupported native Text operations must remain blocked: all
    # supported clipboard operations instead use the guarded command path.
    BLOCKED_CLIPBOARD_VIRTUAL_EVENTS = ("<<PasteSelection>>", "<<Clear>>")
    BLOCKED_CLIPBOARD_KEYS = ("<Button-2>", "<ButtonRelease-2>")
    ROUTED_CLIPBOARD_EVENTS = {
        "<<Copy>>": "copy_selection", "<<Cut>>": "cut_selection",
        "<<Paste>>": "paste_clipboard",
    }
    ROUTED_CLIPBOARD_KEYS = {
        "<Control-c>": "copy_selection", "<Control-x>": "cut_selection",
        "<Control-v>": "paste_clipboard", "<Control-C>": "copy_selection",
        "<Control-X>": "cut_selection", "<Control-V>": "paste_clipboard",
        "<Command-c>": "copy_selection", "<Command-x>": "cut_selection",
        "<Command-v>": "paste_clipboard", "<Command-C>": "copy_selection",
        "<Command-X>": "cut_selection", "<Command-V>": "paste_clipboard",
        "<Control-Insert>": "copy_selection", "<Shift-Insert>": "paste_clipboard",
        "<Shift-Delete>": "cut_selection", "<Control-a>": "select_all",
        "<Control-A>": "select_all", "<Command-a>": "select_all",
        "<Command-A>": "select_all",
    }

    def __init__(self, root=None, surface: NeutralEditorSurface | None = None) -> None:
        # Lazy import keeps headless controller tests usable without Tk installed.
        import tkinter as tk
        from tkinter import messagebox, filedialog

        self.tk = tk
        self.messagebox = messagebox
        self.filedialog = filedialog
        self.root = root if root is not None else tk.Tk()
        self.surface = surface if surface is not None else NeutralEditorSurface()
        if not isinstance(self.surface, NeutralEditorSurface):
            raise TypeError("window requires N-EDITOR-SURFACE")
        self.surface.enable_files()
        self.surface.enable_search()
        if self.surface.clipboard is None:
            self.surface.enable_clipboard(self._read_clipboard, self._write_clipboard)
        self._painting = False
        self.search_dialog = None
        self.find_query = tk.StringVar(master=self.root, value="")
        self.replace_value = tk.StringVar(master=self.root, value="")
        self.find_match_case = tk.BooleanVar(master=self.root, value=False)
        self.root.geometry("780x520")
        self.root.minsize(360, 240)

        self._make_menus()
        frame = tk.Frame(self.root)
        frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        scroll = tk.Scrollbar(frame)
        scroll.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        self.text = tk.Text(
            frame, wrap="word", undo=False, exportselection=False,
            yscrollcommand=scroll.set, font=("TkFixedFont", 12),
            padx=12, pady=12,
        )
        self.text.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        scroll.config(command=self.text.yview)
        self.status_var = tk.StringVar(master=self.root, value="")
        self.status = tk.Label(self.root, textvariable=self.status_var,
                               anchor="w", padx=10)
        self.status.pack(fill="x")

        self.text.bind("<<Modified>>", self._on_text_modified)
        self.text.bind("<Control-z>", self._undo_event)
        self.text.bind("<Control-y>", self._redo_event)
        self.text.bind("<Control-Shift-Z>", self._redo_event)
        self.text.bind("<Command-z>", self._undo_event)
        self.text.bind("<Command-Shift-Z>", self._redo_event)
        for binding in self.BLOCKED_CLIPBOARD_VIRTUAL_EVENTS + self.BLOCKED_CLIPBOARD_KEYS:
            self.text.bind(binding, self._blocked_clipboard)
        for binding, method_name in (list(self.ROUTED_CLIPBOARD_EVENTS.items())
                                     + list(self.ROUTED_CLIPBOARD_KEYS.items())):
            self.text.bind(binding, self._guarded_clipboard_event(method_name))
        for keysym, method in (("n", self.new_file), ("o", self.open_file),
                               ("s", self.save_file)):
            for modifier in ("Control", "Command"):
                self.root.bind(f"<{modifier}-{keysym}>", lambda _e, m=method: (m(), "break")[1])
        for modifier in ("Control", "Command"):
            for target in (self.root, self.text):
                target.bind(f"<{modifier}-f>", lambda _e: (self.open_find(), "break")[1])
                target.bind(f"<{modifier}-h>", lambda _e: (self.open_replace(), "break")[1])
        for target in (self.root, self.text):
            target.bind("<F3>", lambda _e: (self.find_next(), "break")[1])
            target.bind("<Shift-F3>", lambda _e: (self.find_previous(), "break")[1])
        for modifier in ("Control", "Command"):
            self.root.bind(f"<{modifier}-Shift-S>", lambda _e: (self.save_file_as(), "break")[1])
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.request_close)
        self.surface.display(self._paint)
        self.text.focus_set()

    def _make_menus(self) -> None:
        tk = self.tk
        bar = tk.Menu(self.root)
        file_menu = tk.Menu(bar, tearoff=False)
        file_menu.add_command(label="New", command=self.new_file, accelerator="Ctrl+N")
        file_menu.add_command(label="Open…", command=self.open_file, accelerator="Ctrl+O")
        file_menu.add_command(label="Save", command=self.save_file, accelerator="Ctrl+S")
        file_menu.add_command(label="Save As…", command=self.save_file_as, accelerator="Ctrl+Shift+S")
        file_menu.add_separator()
        file_menu.add_command(label="Exit", command=self.request_close)
        bar.add_cascade(label="File", menu=file_menu)

        self.edit_menu = tk.Menu(bar, tearoff=False)
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Undo", command=self.undo, state="disabled")
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Redo", command=self.redo, state="disabled")
        self.edit_menu.add_separator()
        for name, method, accelerator in (
            ("Cut", self.cut_selection, "Ctrl+X"),
            ("Copy", self.copy_selection, "Ctrl+C"),
            ("Paste", self.paste_clipboard, "Ctrl+V"),
            ("Select All", self.select_all, "Ctrl+A"),
        ):
            self.edit_menu.add_command(label=name, command=method, accelerator=accelerator)
        self.edit_menu.add_separator()
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Find…", command=self.open_find, accelerator="Ctrl+F")
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Find Next", command=self.find_next, accelerator="F3")
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Find Previous", command=self.find_previous, accelerator="Shift+F3")
        self.edit_menu.add_command(label="Replace…", command=self.open_replace, accelerator="Ctrl+H")
        bar.add_cascade(label="Edit", menu=self.edit_menu)
        self.root.config(menu=bar)
        self.file_menu = file_menu

    @staticmethod
    def _blocked_clipboard(_event) -> str:
        return "break"

    def _read_clipboard(self) -> str:
        """OS clipboard reads happen only after an authorized paste intent."""
        return self.root.clipboard_get()

    def _write_clipboard(self, text: str) -> None:
        """OS clipboard writes happen only after an authorized cut/copy intent."""
        self.root.clipboard_clear()
        self.root.clipboard_append(text)
        self.root.update_idletasks()

    def _guarded_clipboard_event(self, method_name: str):
        def callback(_event):
            getattr(self, method_name)()
            return "break"  # prevent Tk class binding from bypassing WH
        return callback

    def _selected_offsets(self) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
        """Convert transient Tk selection to document-codepoint offsets."""
        try:
            start = self.text.index("sel.first")
            end = self.text.index("sel.last")
        except self.tk.TclError:
            return None
        # Tcl's "chars" count can count supplementary Unicode as two units.
        # Python's document offsets count Unicode code points, so derive the
        # offsets from the actual Tk text prefix instead.
        return (len(self.text.get("1.0", start)), len(self.text.get("1.0", end)))

    def _selection_or_caret(self) -> tuple[int, int]:
        selected = self._selected_offsets()
        if selected is not None:
            return selected
        offset = len(self.text.get("1.0", "insert"))
        return offset, offset

    def _clipboard_action(self, action, new_cursor: int | None = None) -> bool:
        try:
            snapshot = action()
            self._paint(snapshot)
            if new_cursor is not None:
                self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{new_cursor}c")
                self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
            return True
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.surface.display(self._paint)
            self.messagebox.showerror("Clipboard operation failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False

    def copy_selection(self) -> bool:
        selected = self._selected_offsets()
        if selected is None or selected[0] == selected[1]:
            return False
        return self._clipboard_action(lambda: self.surface.copy_selection(*selected))

    def cut_selection(self) -> bool:
        selected = self._selected_offsets()
        if selected is None or selected[0] == selected[1]:
            return False
        return self._clipboard_action(lambda: self.surface.cut_selection(*selected), selected[0])

    def paste_clipboard(self) -> bool:
        start, end = self._selection_or_caret()
        try:
            # The guarded read must be the one that supplies the inserted text.
            # Compute the cursor from the actual document delta, not an unguarded
            # second clipboard read that could return a different value.
            before = self.surface.dispatch("read")
            after = self.surface.paste_at(start, end)
            length = len(after.text) - (len(before.text) - (end - start))
            self._paint(after)
            self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{start + length}c")
            self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
            return True
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.surface.display(self._paint)
            self.messagebox.showerror("Clipboard operation failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False

    def select_all(self) -> bool:
        try:
            selection = self.surface.select_all()
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.messagebox.showerror("Selection failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False
        self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
        if selection.end > selection.start:
            self.text.tag_add("sel", f"1.0+{selection.start}c", f"1.0+{selection.end}c")
            self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{selection.end}c")
        return True

    def _open_search_dialog(self, replacing: bool) -> None:
        """Nonmodal transient Tk inputs; never an authoritative document store."""
        tk = self.tk
        if self.search_dialog is not None and self.search_dialog.winfo_exists():
            if replacing != self._replace_widgets_shown:
                self._close_search_dialog()
            else:
                self.search_dialog.lift()
                self.search_entry.focus_set()
                return
        dialog = tk.Toplevel(self.root)
        dialog.title("Replace" if replacing else "Find")
        dialog.transient(self.root)
        dialog.resizable(False, False)
        self.search_dialog = dialog
        self._replace_widgets_shown = replacing
        tk.Label(dialog, text="Find what:").grid(row=0, column=0, padx=8, pady=6, sticky="w")
        self.search_entry = tk.Entry(dialog, textvariable=self.find_query, width=32)
        self.search_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=8, pady=6, sticky="ew")
        if replacing:
            tk.Label(dialog, text="Replace with:").grid(row=1, column=0, padx=8, pady=6, sticky="w")
            tk.Entry(dialog, textvariable=self.replace_value, width=32).grid(row=1, column=1, padx=8, pady=6)
        opts = tk.Frame(dialog)
        opts.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", padx=8)
        tk.Checkbutton(opts, text="Match case", variable=self.find_match_case).pack(side="left")
        controls = tk.Frame(dialog)
        controls.grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, padx=8, pady=8)
        tk.Button(controls, text="Find Next", command=self.find_next).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        if replacing:
            tk.Button(controls, text="Replace", command=self.replace_selection).pack(side="left", padx=3)
            tk.Button(controls, text="Replace All", command=self.replace_all).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Button(controls, text="Close", command=self._close_search_dialog).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        dialog.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._close_search_dialog)
        dialog.bind("<Escape>", lambda _e: (self._close_search_dialog(), "break")[1])
        self.search_entry.focus_set()
        self.search_entry.selection_range(0, "end")

    def _close_search_dialog(self) -> None:
        if self.search_dialog is not None:
            try:
                self.search_dialog.destroy()
            except self.tk.TclError:
                pass
            self.search_dialog = None
        self.text.focus_set()

    def open_find(self) -> None:
        self._open_search_dialog(False)

    def open_replace(self) -> None:
        self._open_search_dialog(True)

    def _find_in_direction(self, backwards: bool) -> bool:
        query = self.find_query.get()
        if not query:
            self.open_find()
            return False
        selected = self._selected_offsets()
        # The next match starts after the current selection; previous search
        # begins before its start. No document mutation from a search.
        origin = (selected[0] if backwards else selected[1]) if selected else None
        if origin is None:
            origin = len(self.text.get("1.0", "insert"))
        try:
            match = self.surface.find(query, origin, bool(self.find_match_case.get()),
                                      wrap=True, backwards=backwards)
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.messagebox.showerror("Find failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False
        if match is None:
            self.root.bell()
            return False
        self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
        self.text.tag_add("sel", f"1.0+{match.start}c", f"1.0+{match.end}c")
        self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{match.start}c")
        self.text.see(f"1.0+{match.start}c")
        return True

    def find_next(self) -> bool:
        return self._find_in_direction(False)

    def find_previous(self) -> bool:
        return self._find_in_direction(True)

    def replace_selection(self) -> bool:
        selected = self._selected_offsets()
        if selected is None:
            return self.find_next()
        try:
            result = self.surface.replace_one(self.find_query.get(),
                                              self.replace_value.get(), *selected,
                                              match_case=bool(self.find_match_case.get()))
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.messagebox.showerror("Replace failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False
        self._paint(result.snapshot)
        self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
        self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{selected[0] + len(self.replace_value.get())}c")
        return True

    def replace_all(self) -> int:
        try:
            result = self.surface.replace_all(self.find_query.get(), self.replace_value.get(),
                                              match_case=bool(self.find_match_case.get()))
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.messagebox.showerror("Replace failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return 0
        self._paint(result.snapshot)
        self.text.tag_remove("sel", "1.0", "end")
        self.messagebox.showinfo("Replace All", f"Replaced {result.count} occurrence(s).", parent=self.root)
        return result.count

    def _on_text_modified(self, _event=None) -> None:
        if self._painting or not self.text.edit_modified():
            return
        changed = self.text.get("1.0", "end-1c")
        try:
            snap = self.surface.text_changed(changed)
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            # Never leave a rejected widget edit diverging from the document.
            self.surface.display(self._paint)
            self.messagebox.showerror("Edit rejected", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return
        self._paint(snap)

    def _paint(self, snapshot: DocumentSnapshot) -> None:
        # A Tk Text field is a cache of the snapshot, NEVER a second owner.
        self._painting = True
        try:
            displayed = self.text.get("1.0", "end-1c")
            if displayed != snapshot.text:
                offset = len(self.text.get("1.0", "insert"))
                self.text.delete("1.0", "end")
                self.text.insert("1.0", snapshot.text)
                self.text.mark_set("insert", f"1.0+{min(offset, len(snapshot.text))}c")
            self.text.edit_modified(False)
            path = self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path if self.surface.file_session else None
            import os
            filename = os.path.basename(path) if path else "Untitled"
            self.root.title(("● " if snapshot.dirty else "") + filename + " — Anchor Notepad")
            self.status_var.set(
                ("Unsaved edits" if snapshot.dirty else "No unsaved edits")
                + "  |  " + (path if path else "Untitled (not saved)")
            )
            history = self.surface.history.snapshot()
            self.edit_menu.entryconfigure("Undo", state="normal" if history.undo_count else "disabled")
            self.edit_menu.entryconfigure("Redo", state="normal" if history.redo_count else "disabled")
        finally:
            self._painting = False

    def undo(self) -> None:
        try:
            self._paint(self.surface.undo())
        except CommandRejected:
            self.surface.display(self._paint)

    def redo(self) -> None:
        try:
            self._paint(self.surface.redo())
        except CommandRejected:
            self.surface.display(self._paint)

    def _undo_event(self, _event) -> str:
        self.undo()
        return "break"

    def _redo_event(self, _event) -> str:
        self.redo()
        return "break"

    def _confirm_discard(self) -> bool:
        if not self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty:
            return True
        return self.messagebox.askyesno(
            "Unsaved changes", "Discard your unsaved edits?", parent=self.root
        ) is True

    def _file_action(self, action) -> bool:
        try:
            self._paint(action())
            return True
        except CommandRejected as exc:
            self.surface.display(self._paint)
            self.messagebox.showerror("File operation failed", str(exc), parent=self.root)
            return False

    def new_file(self) -> bool:
        if not self._confirm_discard():
            return False
        return self._file_action(lambda: self.surface.new_document(discard=True))

    def open_file(self) -> bool:
        path = self.filedialog.askopenfilename(
            parent=self.root, title="Open text file",
            filetypes=[("Text files", "*.txt"), ("All files", "*")],
        )
        if not path or not self._confirm_discard():
            return False
        return self._file_action(lambda: self.surface.open_file(path, discard=True))

    def save_file(self) -> bool:
        session = self.surface.file_session
        if session is None or session.snapshot().path is None:
            return self.save_file_as()
        return self._file_action(self.surface.save_file)

    def save_file_as(self) -> bool:
        path = self.filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            parent=self.root, title="Save text file",
            defaultextension=".txt", confirmoverwrite=True,
            filetypes=[("Text files", "*.txt"), ("All files", "*")],
        )
        if not path:
            return False
        # The native file dialog handles overwrite confirmation; the explicit
        # permission flag is also checked by WH before any host write.
        return self._file_action(lambda: self.surface.save_file_as(path, overwrite=True))

    def request_close(self) -> bool:
        def confirm() -> bool:
            return self.messagebox.askyesno(
                "Discard unsaved changes?",
                "This document is not saved. Closing will discard its text. Exit anyway?",
                parent=self.root,
            )
        if not self.surface.can_exit(confirm):
            return False
        self.root.destroy()
        return True

    def run(self) -> None:
        self.root.mainloop()


def main() -> None:
    """Launch the local, file-capable Notepad desktop application."""
    TkNotepadWindow().run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```text
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

  <title>Anchor Notepad — Logical Shell</title>

  <script src="/shell.js" defer></script>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="application">
    <header class="masthead">
      <div class="brand"><span class="glyph">◧</span><div><strong>Anchor Notepad</strong><span class="subhead">Logical execution preview · 9 Anchors / 9 Gate families</span></div></div>
      <div class="pill">VIRTUAL HOST · NO MODEL CALLS</div>
    </header>
    <div class="menu" role="toolbar" aria-label="File and edit actions">
      <button id="new" type="button">New</button>
      <button id="open" type="button">Open</button>
      <button id="save" type="button">Save</button>
      <button id="saveas" type="button">Save As</button>
      <button id="download" type="button">Download text</button>
      <span class="sep"></span>
      <button id="undo" type="button">↶ Undo</button>
      <button id="redo" type="button">↷ Redo</button>
      <button id="copy" type="button">Copy</button>
      <button id="cut" type="button">Cut</button>
      <button id="paste" type="button">Paste</button>
      <button id="selectall" type="button">Select all</button>
    </div>
    <div class="workarea">
      <section class="editor-pane">
        <div class="editor-heading"><span id="filename">Untitled · virtual</span><span id="dirty" aria-label="Saved status">Saved</span></div>
        <label for="editor" class="sr-only">Document text</label>
        <textarea id="editor" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Notepad document text"></textarea>
        <div class="find-bar">
          <label>Find <input id="query" placeholder="Text to find" /></label>
          <label>Replace <input id="replacement" placeholder="Replacement text" /></label>
          <label class="check"><input id="matchcase" type="checkbox" /> Match case</label>
          <button id="find" type="button">Find next</button>
          <button id="replace" type="button">Replace selection</button>
          <button id="replaceall" type="button">Replace all</button>
        </div>
        <footer class="statusbar"><span id="docstatus">Ready</span><span id="counts">Rev 0 · 0 undo / 0 redo</span></footer>
      </section>
      <aside class="inspector">
        <div class="panel-heading"><strong>Anchor reasoning trace</strong><span>Independent reference interpreter</span></div>
        <p class="hint">The shell projects the results of in-memory Anchor/Gate logic. It does not use the real Python Notepad and does not call an LLM.</p>
        <div class="trace" id="trace" aria-live="polite">Choose an editor action to see the applied rule and Gates.</div>
        <div class="inspector-actions"><button id="packet" type="button">Copy LLM stimulus packet</button><button id="reset" type="button">Reset demo</button></div>
        <div class="miniheader">Last model-ready packet</div>
        <textarea id="packetview" readonly aria-label="LLM interpretation prompt" placeholder="After an action, click Copy LLM stimulus packet."></textarea>
        <p class="hint small">The packet contains the Anchor rules, known state, stimulus, and Gate references—but no reference implementation or answer. Give it to an independent LLM to test mental execution.</p>
      </aside>
    </div>
    <input type="file" id="filepicker" accept=".txt,text/plain" hidden />
  </div>
</body>
</html>
```

```text
'use strict';
// Presentation only. All authorized transitions are computed by logical_engine.py.
// Not a model call; browser file read/download are explicit user interactions.
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const editor = $('editor');
let snapshot = null;
let localDraft = false;
let busy = false;
let highlighted = null;

function status(text, bad=false) {
  $('docstatus').textContent = text;
  $('docstatus').style.color = bad ? '#b33030' : '#5b6b7c';
}
function render(state, {preserveDraft=false}={}) {
  snapshot=state;
  if(!preserveDraft){editor.value=state.text;localDraft=false;}
  $('filename').textContent=state.path || 'Untitled · virtual';
  $('dirty').textContent=state.dirty || localDraft ? 'Unsaved' : 'Saved (virtual)';
  $('dirty').classList.toggle('unsaved',!!(state.dirty || localDraft));
  $('counts').textContent=`Rev ${state.revision} · ${state.undo_count} undo / ${state.redo_count} redo`;
}
function showTrace(reply, action='') {
  const tr=$('trace');
  tr.classList.toggle('error',reply.outcome==='REJECTED_BY_GATE'||reply.outcome==='UNDETERMINED');
  const lines=[
    `STIMULUS: ${action || 'view'}`,
    `OUTCOME: ${reply.outcome}`,
    reply.reason ? `REASON: ${reply.reason}` : null,
    reply.trace?.action_clause ? `CLAUSE: ${reply.trace.action_clause}` : null,
    reply.trace?.gate_families?.length ? `GATES: ${reply.trace.gate_families.join(', ')}` : null,
    reply.trace?.participants ? `ANCHORS: ${reply.trace.participants.join(' → ')}` : null,
    reply.trace?.external_effects?.length ? `EFFECTS: ${reply.trace.external_effects.join(', ')} (SIMULATED)` : null,
    reply.result?.kind ? `RESULT: ${JSON.stringify(reply.result)}` : null,
    reply.state ? `DOCUMENT: ${JSON.stringify(reply.state.text)}\nREVISION: ${reply.state.revision}; DIRTY: ${reply.state.dirty}` : null,
    `\nNo model call or actual filesystem/OS clipboard effect.`
  ].filter(Boolean);
  tr.textContent=lines.join('\n');
  status(reply.reason || `Action ${action||'view'}: ${reply.outcome}`,reply.outcome==='REJECTED_BY_GATE');
}
async function api(path,body){
  const init = body===undefined ? {} : {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)};
  const res=await fetch(path,init);const data=await res.json();
  if(!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
  return data;
}
function pointToCp(offset){return Array.from(editor.value.slice(0,offset)).length;}
function cpToPoint(offset){return Array.from(editor.value).slice(0,offset).join('').length;}
function selection(){return {start:pointToCp(editor.selectionStart),end:pointToCp(editor.selectionEnd)};}
function applySelection(range){
  if(!range)return;
  editor.focus();editor.setSelectionRange(cpToPoint(range[0]),cpToPoint(range[1]));
  highlighted=range;
}
async function send(action,payload, {skipFlush=false}={}) {
  if(busy)return null;
  busy=true;
  try {
    if(!skipFlush && localDraft && action!=='edit') {
      const committed=await api('/api/command',{action:'edit',payload:editor.value});
      render(committed.state);
      if(committed.outcome!=='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS'){showTrace(committed,'edit');return committed;}
    }
    const reply=await api('/api/command',{action,payload});
    render(reply.state);
    showTrace(reply,action);
    if(reply.result?.selection){applySelection(reply.result.selection);}
    return reply;
  } catch(e){status(`Shell error: ${e.message}`,true);$('trace').textContent=String(e);return null;}
  finally{busy=false;}
}
async function flushDraft(){
  if(!localDraft)return true;
  const from=editor.selectionStart,to=editor.selectionEnd;
  const reply=await send('edit',editor.value,{skipFlush:true});
  if(reply?.outcome==='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS')editor.setSelectionRange(from,to);
  return !!reply && reply.outcome==='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS';
}
async function run(action,payload){
  // For range operations, commit pending draft before calculating offsets from committed state.
  if(localDraft && !await flushDraft())return;
  return send(action,payload,{skipFlush:true});
}
editor.addEventListener('input',()=>{
  localDraft=true;
  $('dirty').textContent='Draft (not yet committed)';
  $('dirty').classList.add('unsaved');
  status('Editing local display draft · commits on next command');
});
editor.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'){
    e.preventDefault(); run(e.shiftKey?'redo':'undo',null);
  }
});
$('new').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!await flushDraft())return;
  const discard=snapshot?.dirty ? confirm('Discard the unsaved virtual document changes?') : false;
  if(snapshot?.dirty && !discard)return;
  send('new',{discard},{skipFlush:true});
});
$('open').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;$('filepicker').click();});
$('filepicker').addEventListener('change',async e=>{
  const file=e.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;
  try{
    const text=await file.text();
    const path='/virtual/'+file.name.replace(/[^\w.() -]/g,'_');
    await api('/api/seed-file',{path,text});
    const discard=!!snapshot?.dirty && confirm('Discard unsaved virtual changes before opening the selected file?');
    if(snapshot?.dirty && !discard)return;
    await send('open',{path,discard},{skipFlush:true});
    status(`Opened browser-selected ${file.name} into virtual file storage`);
  }catch(err){status(`Open failed: ${err.message}`,true)}finally{e.target.value='';}
});
$('save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;await send('save',null,{skipFlush:true});});
$('saveas').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!await flushDraft())return;
  const path=prompt('Choose a VIRTUAL filename (not a disk path):','/virtual/notes.txt');
  if(path===null)return;
  const reply=await send('save-as',{path,overwrite:false},{skipFlush:true});
  if(reply?.outcome==='REJECTED_BY_GATE' && reply.reason?.includes('overwrite')){
    if(confirm('Overwrite the existing virtual file?'))await send('save-as',{path,overwrite:true},{skipFlush:true});
  }
});
$('download').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!await flushDraft())return;
  const blob=new Blob([editor.value],{type:'text/plain;charset=utf-8'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download=(snapshot?.path?.split('/').pop()||'anchor-notepad.txt');
  document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),3000);
  status('Browser download requested · disk save not verified');
});
$('undo').addEventListener('click',()=>run('undo',null));
$('redo').addEventListener('click',()=>run('redo',null));
$('copy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;await run('copy',selection());});
$('cut').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;await run('cut',selection());});
$('paste').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;await run('paste',selection());});
$('selectall').addEventListener('click',()=>run('select-all',null));
function searchArgs(){return {query:$('query').value,match_case:$('matchcase').checked}}
$('find').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!await flushDraft())return;const s=selection();await run('find',{...searchArgs(),start:s.end,wrap:true,backwards:false});});
$('replace').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!await flushDraft())return;
  const s=selection();await run('replace-one',{...searchArgs(),replacement:$('replacement').value,...s});
});
$('replaceall').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!await flushDraft())return;
  await run('replace-all',{...searchArgs(),replacement:$('replacement').value});
});
$('reset').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  if(!confirm('Reset the entire virtual Notepad session?'))return;
  try{render((await api('/api/reset',{})).state);$('trace').textContent='Reset complete · no user file changed.';status('Reset virtual session')}catch(err){status(err.message,true)}
});
$('packet').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  try{
    const packet=(await api('/api/packet')).prompt;
    $('packetview').value=packet;
    try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(packet);status('LLM interpretation packet copied. No model call was made.');}
    catch{status('Packet displayed below. Select and copy it manually.');}
  }catch(err){status(err.message,true)}
});
async function init(){try{const r=await api('/api/state');render(r.state);status('Ready · virtual file/clipboard effects only')}catch(e){status('Local shell unavailable: '+e.message,true)}}
init();
```
