```text
"""Round 7 guard/clipboard integration and cumulative preservation tests."""
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

from notepad_clipboard import NeutralClipboardSelection, SelectionRange
from notepad_document import NeutralDocument
from notepad_files import NeutralFileSession
from notepad_guard import CommandRejected, WhiteHoleCommandGuard
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory
from notepad_input import BlackHoleEditorInput
from notepad_surface import NeutralEditorSurface, TkNotepadWindow


class MemoryClipboard:
    """Host test adapter, not an Anchor or extra document owner."""
    def __init__(self, text=""):
        self.text = text
        self.read_count = 0
        self.write_count = 0
        self.fail_read = False
        self.fail_write = False

    def read(self):
        self.read_count += 1
        if self.fail_read:
            raise RuntimeError("host clipboard unavailable")
        return self.text

    def write(self, text):
        self.write_count += 1
        if self.fail_write:
            raise RuntimeError("host clipboard write unavailable")
        self.text = text


class TestClipboardSelection(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.host = MemoryClipboard()
        self.surface = NeutralEditorSurface()
        self.surface.enable_clipboard(self.host.read, self.host.write)

    def snapshot_triplet(self):
        return (self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot(),
                self.host.text)

    def test_seventh_anchor_identity_and_explicit_host_binding(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.clipboard.anchor_id, "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.clipboard.canonical_type, "Neutral")
        self.assertIs(self.surface.clipboard._document, self.surface.document)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "already enabled"):
            self.surface.enable_clipboard(self.host.read, self.host.write)

    def test_no_new_document_text_or_history_owners(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abc")
        self.surface.copy_selection(0, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "abc")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "a")

    def test_unbound_clipboard_command_rejected_before_effect(self):
        surface = NeutralEditorSurface()
        surface.text_changed("keep")
        previous = (surface.document.snapshot(), surface.history.snapshot())
        for action, payload in (("copy", {"start":0, "end":2}),
                                ("cut", {"start":0, "end":2}),
                                ("paste", {"start":0, "end":0}),
                                ("select-all", None)):
            with self.subTest(action=action):
                with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                    surface.dispatch(action, payload)
                self.assertEqual((surface.document.snapshot(), surface.history.snapshot()), previous)

    def test_clipboard_capture_alone_has_no_host_or_document_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        intent = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("cut", {"start":0, "end":2})
        self.assertEqual(intent.action, "cut")
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 0)

    def test_copy_unicode_selected_text_exactly_without_mutation(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("a📝b\nc")
        before = (self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.copy_selection(1, 3).text, "a📝b\nc")
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "📝b")
        self.assertEqual((self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot()), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 1)

    def test_cut_is_one_undoable_edit_and_pastes_correctly(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abcdef")
        after = self.surface.cut_selection(1, 4)
        self.assertEqual(after.text, "aef")
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "bcd")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "abcdef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.redo().text, "aef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.paste_at(1, 1).text, "abcdef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "aef")

    def test_paste_replaces_selection_atomically_and_undoes_once(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello world")
        self.host.text = "📝\n"
        after = self.surface.paste_at(6, 11)
        self.assertEqual(after.text, "hello 📝\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "hello world")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.redo().text, "hello 📝\n")

    def test_paste_empty_clipboard_can_delete_selected_text(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.paste_at(1, 2).text, "AC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "ABC")

    def test_paste_empty_clipboard_at_caret_is_noop(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        previous = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        result = self.surface.paste_at(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(result.text, "ABC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), previous)

    def test_select_all_returns_unicode_offsets_without_side_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("📝 hello\nworld")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, len("📝 hello\nworld")))
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)
        self.assertEqual((self.host.read_count, self.host.write_count), (0, 0))

    def test_select_all_empty_document(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, 0))

    def test_reject_bad_range_without_clipboard_or_history_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abcd")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        cases = [
            {"start":0,"end":5}, {"start":3,"end":2}, {"start":-1,"end":2},
            {"start":False,"end":2}, {"start":0,"end":2.0},
            {"start":"0","end":2}, {"start":0,"end":2,"extra":True},
            {"start":0}, None, "bad",
        ]
        for payload in cases:
            with self.subTest(payload=payload):
                for action in ("copy", "cut", "paste"):
                    with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                        self.surface.dispatch(action, payload)
                    self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.read_count, 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 0)

    def test_copy_and_cut_empty_selection_rejected_without_host_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abc")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        for action in ("copy", "cut"):
            with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                self.surface.dispatch(action, {"start":1,"end":1})
            self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)

    def test_select_all_payload_rejected_effect_free(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        for payload in (0, "all", {}, {"start":0,"end":5}):
            with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                self.surface.dispatch("select-all", payload)
            self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_bad_clipboard_read_preserves_document_and_history(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("original")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.fail_read = True
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "clipboard read failed"):
            self.surface.paste_at(0, 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_bad_clipboard_write_prevents_cut(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("original")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.fail_write = True
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "clipboard write failed"):
            self.surface.cut_selection(0, 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_clipboard_nonstr_returns_rejected_no_change(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.text = 42
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "plain text"):
            self.surface.paste_at(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet()[0:2], before[0:2])

    def test_reentrant_clipboard_read_does_not_apply_stale_paste(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        def stale_read():
            self.surface.document.replace_text("changed by host")
            return "X"
        clip = self.surface.clipboard
        clip._read_text = stale_read
        before_history = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.paste_at(1, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "changed by host")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), before_history)

    def test_reentrant_clipboard_write_does_not_apply_stale_cut(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        def stale_write(_text):
            self.surface.document.replace_text("changed by host")
        self.surface.clipboard._write_text = stale_write
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.cut_selection(1, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "changed by host")

    def test_cross_document_binding_is_rejected(self):
        other = NeutralEditorSurface()
        other.text_changed("abc")
        original = other.document.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "another document"):
            NeutralClipboardSelection(other.document, self.surface.history, self.host.read, self.host.write)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "another document"):
            other.clipboard = self.surface.clipboard
            other.select_all()
        self.assertEqual(other.document.snapshot(), original)

    def test_invalid_clipboard_host_adapters_are_rejected(self):
        no_host = NeutralEditorSurface()
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            no_host.enable_clipboard(None, lambda x: None)
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            no_host.enable_clipboard(lambda: "text", 5)
        self.assertIsNone(no_host.clipboard)

    def test_undo_redo_preserves_clean_baseline_with_copy_cut_paste(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "note.txt"
            self.surface.enable_files()
            self.surface.text_changed("keep")
            self.surface.save_file_as(str(path))
            self.surface.copy_selection(0, 2)
            self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.cut_selection(0, 2)
            self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.undo()
            self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.redo()
            self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.assertEqual(path.read_text(), "keep")

    def test_clipboard_capability_persists_across_new_open(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "a.txt"
            path.write_text("opened 📝", encoding="utf-8")
            self.surface.enable_files()
            self.surface.text_changed("draft")
            self.surface.new_document(discard=True)
            self.surface.open_file(str(path))
            self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, len("opened 📝")))
            self.surface.copy_selection(0, 6)
            self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "opened")
            self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)

    def test_legacy_edit_undo_redo_unbound_guard_still_works(self):
        doc = NeutralDocument()
        guard = WhiteHoleCommandGuard()
        intent = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("edit", "one")
        self.assertEqual(guard.execute(intent, doc).text, "one")
        history = NeutralEditHistory(doc)
        self.assertEqual(guard.execute(BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("read"), doc).text, "one")
        self.assertEqual(history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)


class TestTkClipboard(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            self.root = tk.Tk()
            self.root.withdraw()
        except (ImportError, tk.TclError) as exc:
            self.skipTest(f"Tk display not available: {exc}")
        self.window = TkNotepadWindow(root=self.root)
        self.window.messagebox.showerror = lambda *args, **kwargs: self.fail(f"Unexpected error {args}")
        self.root.update()

    def tearDown(self):
        if getattr(self, "root", None):
            try:
                self.root.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def set_text(self, text):
        self.window.surface.text_changed(text)
        self.window.surface.display(self.window._paint)
        self.root.update()

    def choose(self, start, end):
        self.window.text.tag_add("sel", f"1.0+{start}c", f"1.0+{end}c")

    def test_gui_menu_and_keyboard_routes(self):
        for name in ("Copy", "Cut", "Paste", "Select All"):
            self.assertEqual(self.window.edit_menu.entrycget(name, "state"), "normal")
        for sequence in ("<<Copy>>", "<<Cut>>", "<<Paste>>", "<Control-a>",
                         "<Control-c>", "<Control-x>", "<Control-v>"):
            self.assertIn("callback", self.window.text.bind(sequence))
        # Round 8 explicitly authorizes Find/Replace through the new
        # document-bound search owner and existing WhiteHole.
        for name in ("Find…", "Replace…"):
            self.assertEqual(self.window.edit_menu.entrycget(name, "state"), "normal")

    def test_select_all_projects_selection_without_history(self):
        self.set_text("hello 📝\nworld")
        before = (self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertTrue(self.window.select_all())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.get("sel.first", "sel.last"), "hello 📝\nworld")
        self.assertEqual((self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot()), before)

    def test_copy_cut_paste_real_tk_clipboard(self):
        self.set_text("123📝567")
        self.choose(1, 4)
        self.assertTrue(self.window.copy_selection())
        self.assertEqual(self.root.clipboard_get(), "23📝")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "123📝567")
        self.assertTrue(self.window.cut_selection())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "1567")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.get("1.0", "end-1c"), "1567")
        self.assertEqual(self.root.clipboard_get(), "23📝")
        self.window.text.mark_set("insert", "1.0+1c")
        self.assertTrue(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "123📝567")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.undo().text, "1567")

    def test_paste_over_selection_and_correct_caret(self):
        self.set_text("hello world")
        self.root.clipboard_clear()
        self.root.clipboard_append("📝")
        self.choose(6, 11)
        self.assertTrue(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "hello 📝")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.index("insert"), "1.8")
        self.assertFalse(bool(self.window.text.tag_ranges("sel")))

    def test_native_unsupported_clipboard_effects_blocked(self):
        self.set_text("keep")
        self.assertEqual(self.window._blocked_clipboard(None), "break")
        for sequence in ("<<PasteSelection>>", "<<Clear>>", "<Button-2>"):
            self.assertIn("_blocked_clipboard", self.window.text.bind(sequence))
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "keep")

    def test_no_selection_cut_copy_are_effect_free(self):
        self.set_text("abcd")
        before = (self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertFalse(self.window.cut_selection())
        self.assertFalse(self.window.copy_selection())
        self.assertEqual((self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot()), before)

    def test_tk_paste_clipboard_failure_preserves_document(self):
        self.set_text("keep")
        before = self.window.surface.document.snapshot()
        self.window.messagebox.showerror = lambda *args, **kw: None
        self.window._read_clipboard = lambda: self.fail("adapter is already bound")
        self.window.surface.clipboard._read_text = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError("not available"))
        self.assertFalse(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), before)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```text
"""Blind Anchor logical interpretation protocol tests (no actual model calls)."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import unittest

import blind_trial as b


class TestModelNeutralBlindProtocol(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.public, cls.private = b.build_trial_data()
        cls.control = {a['trial_id']:{k:a[k] for k in b.FIELDS} for a in cls.private['answers']}

    def test_opaque_41_stimuli_with_no_expected_results(self):
        public=self.public
        self.assertEqual(41,len(public['trials']))
        self.assertEqual([f'T{i:04}' for i in range(1,42)],[t['id'] for t in public['trials']])
        self.assertEqual(9,public['anchor_count'])
        self.assertEqual(9,public['gate_family_count'])
        self.assertEqual(19,public['gate_condition_count'])
        for t in public['trials']:
            self.assertEqual(set(t),{'id','initial_state','stimulus','simulated_effect_failures',
                                     'action_clause','gate_families','host_profile',
                                     'file_session_observation'})
            self.assertNotIn('result',t)
            self.assertNotIn('outcome',t)
            self.assertNotIn('source_case',t)
            self.assertNotIn('expected_final',t)

    def test_all_19_clauses_and_nine_families_are_public_and_consistent(self):
        registry=self.public['gate_registry']
        all_conditions=registry['gate_conditions']
        all_families=registry['gate_families']
        self.assertEqual(19,len(all_conditions))
        self.assertEqual(9,len(all_families))
        self.assertEqual(19,len({g['gate_id'] for g in all_conditions}))
        self.assertEqual({'G-BUILD-01','G-UI-01'},
                         {g['gate_id'] for g in all_conditions if g['phase']!='RUNTIME'})
        owned=[c for family in all_families for c in family['clauses']]
        self.assertEqual(sorted(c['gate_id'] for c in all_conditions),sorted(owned))
        self.assertEqual(len(owned),len(set(owned)))
        self.assertEqual({f['id'] for f in all_families},
                         set(f['id'] for f in all_families))

    def test_all_17_supported_action_result_variants_are_enumerated(self):
        contract=self.public['result_contract']
        by_action=contract['allowed_result_kinds_by_action']
        kinds=contract['result_kinds']
        self.assertEqual(17,len(by_action))
        self.assertEqual({'snapshot','selection','match','none','replace'},set(kinds))
        for trial in self.public['trials']:
            act=trial['stimulus']['action']
            if act in by_action:
                self.assertIn(self.control[trial['id']]['result']['kind'],by_action[act]) if self.control[trial['id']]['result'] is not None else None
        self.assertEqual(['match','none'],by_action['find'])
        self.assertEqual(['replace'],by_action['replace-all'])

    def test_save_disk_equality_is_explicit_not_inferred_from_current_file(self):
        trials=self.public['trials']
        saves=[t for t in trials if t['stimulus']['action']=='save']
        self.assertEqual(2,len(saves))
        statuses={t['file_session_observation']['disk_comparison'] for t in saves}
        self.assertEqual({'NO_ASSOCIATION','MATCH'},statuses)
        self.assertEqual('MATCH',next(t for t in saves if t['initial_state']['active_path'])['file_session_observation']['disk_comparison'])
        self.assertNotIn('expected_digest',self.public['expected_response_schema']['state_fields'])
        for t in trials:
            self.assertEqual(t['initial_state']['active_path'],t['file_session_observation']['active_path'])
            self.assertEqual('TRUSTED_VIRTUAL_HOST_PRECOMMAND',t['file_session_observation']['observation_source'])
            self.assertIn(t['file_session_observation']['disk_comparison'],
                          self.public['file_session_observation_contract']['comparison_values'])

    def test_file_session_comparison_distinguishes_external_change_and_missing(self):
        from logical_engine import LogicalMachine, digest
        machine=LogicalMachine('same',files={'/virtual/doc.txt':b'old'})
        machine.active_path='/virtual/doc.txt'
        machine.expected_digest=digest(b'old')
        self.assertEqual('MATCH',b.file_session_observation(machine)['disk_comparison'])
        machine.files[machine.active_path]=b'outsider'
        self.assertEqual('MISMATCH',b.file_session_observation(machine)['disk_comparison'])
        outcome=machine.send('save')
        self.assertEqual('REJECTED_BY_GATE',outcome['outcome'])
        machine.files.pop(machine.active_path)
        self.assertEqual('MISSING',b.file_session_observation(machine)['disk_comparison'])
        machine.expected_digest=None
        machine.files[machine.active_path]=b'outside'
        self.assertEqual('UNKNOWN',b.file_session_observation(machine)['disk_comparison'])
        machine.active_path=None
        self.assertEqual('NO_ASSOCIATION',b.file_session_observation(machine)['disk_comparison'])

    def test_success_result_schema_is_not_ambiguous(self):
        self.assertEqual('notepad.blind-logic-challenge/2',self.public['schema'])
        for answer in self.private['answers']:
            result=answer['result']
            if result is None:
                continue
            spec=self.public['result_contract']['result_kinds'][result['kind']]
            self.assertEqual(set(spec['required_keys']),set(result))
            if 'selection' in result:
                self.assertEqual(2,len(result['selection']))
                self.assertTrue(all(type(v) is int for v in result['selection']))
            if 'count' in result:
                self.assertIs(type(result['count']),int)
                self.assertGreaterEqual(result['count'],0)

    def test_no_implementation_or_key_in_public_export(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            ch=Path(root)/'public';seal=Path(root)/'sealed'
            b.prepare(ch,seal)
            self.assertEqual({'challenge.json','START-HERE.md','response-template.jsonl'},
                             {p.name for p in ch.iterdir()})
            self.assertEqual({'private-key.json','SEAL-README.md'},
                             {p.name for p in seal.iterdir()})
            for p in ch.iterdir():
                raw=p.read_text('utf-8')
                self.assertNotIn('source_case',raw)
                self.assertNotIn('expected_final',raw)
                self.assertNotIn('def _dispatch(',raw)
                self.assertNotIn('def score(',raw)
            self.assertEqual(self.public,b.parse_json(ch/'challenge.json'))

    def test_control_41_of_41_not_model_evidence(self):
        scored=b.score(self.public,self.private,self.control)
        self.assertTrue(scored['complete_pass'])
        self.assertEqual((41,41,574),(scored['total_trials'],scored['passed_trials'],scored['field_passes']))
        self.assertIn('no evidence of independent LLM',scored['scope'])

    def test_tampered_packet_rejected_by_fingerprint(self):
        tampered=deepcopy(self.public)
        tampered['trials'][0]['stimulus']['action']='edit'
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'fingerprint'):
            b.score(tampered,self.private,self.control)

    def test_wrong_text_detected_not_accepted(self):
        candidate=deepcopy(self.control)
        candidate['T0001']['state']['text']='INJECTED'
        result=b.score(self.public,self.private,candidate)
        self.assertFalse(result['complete_pass'])
        self.assertEqual(40,result['passed_trials'])
        self.assertIn('state.text',result['results'][0]['mismatches'])

    def test_wrong_history_detected(self):
        candidate=deepcopy(self.control)
        candidate['T0001']['state']['undo_stack']=[['bad','bad']]
        scored=b.score(self.public,self.private,candidate)
        self.assertIn('state.undo_stack',scored['results'][0]['mismatches'])

    def test_wrong_host_file_effect_detected(self):
        candidate=deepcopy(self.control)
        candidate['T0001']['state']['virtual_files']={'/unauthorized':{'text':'bad'}}
        scored=b.score(self.public,self.private,candidate)
        self.assertIn('state.virtual_files',scored['results'][0]['mismatches'])

    def test_missing_case_is_explicit_failure(self):
        candidate=deepcopy(self.control)
        candidate.pop('T0001')
        scored=b.score(self.public,self.private,candidate)
        self.assertEqual(40,scored['submitted_trials'])
        self.assertEqual(['MISSING_SUBMISSION'],scored['results'][0]['mismatches'])

    def test_unknown_case_not_admitted(self):
        candidate=deepcopy(self.control)
        candidate['fake']=candidate['T0001']
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Unknown trial'):
            b.score(self.public,self.private,candidate)

    def test_duplicate_response_ids_rejected(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            f=Path(root)/'responses.jsonl'
            r=self.control['T0001']
            f.write_text(b.canonical(r)+'\n'+b.canonical(r)+'\n','utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'duplicate trial_id'):
                b.load_responses(f)

    def test_invalid_json_duplicate_fields_and_nan_rejected(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            f=Path(root)/'response.jsonl'
            r=b.canonical(self.control['T0001'])
            f.write_text(r.replace('"trial_id":"T0001"','"trial_id":"T0001","trial_id":"T0001"')+'\n','utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Duplicate JSON field'):
                b.load_responses(f)
            f.write_text(r.replace('"revision":0','"revision":NaN')+'\n','utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'Nonfinite JSON'):
                b.load_responses(f)

    def test_invalid_result_variants_and_bad_selection_rejected(self):
        success=next(v for v in self.control.values() if v['outcome']=='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS')
        for bad_result,pattern in [({'kind':'DocumentSnapshot'},'result kind'),
                                   ({'kind':'snapshot','count':0},'result fields'),
                                   ({'kind':'match','selection':[True,2]},'selection'),
                                   ({'kind':'replace','count':True},'count')]:
            with self.subTest(result=bad_result), TemporaryDirectory() as root:
                entry=deepcopy(success);entry['result']=bad_result
                path=Path(root)/'model.jsonl';path.write_text(b.canonical(entry)+'\n','utf-8')
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,pattern):b.load_responses(path)

    def test_rejection_must_explain_and_leave_null_result(self):
        rejected=next(k for k,v in self.control.items() if v['outcome']=='REJECTED_BY_GATE')
        wrong=deepcopy(self.control[rejected]);wrong['reason']=''
        with TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            f=Path(root)/'res.jsonl';f.write_text(b.canonical(wrong)+'\n','utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'reason'):
                b.load_responses(f)

    def test_missing_state_keys_fail_validation(self):
        wrong=deepcopy(self.control['T0001']);wrong['state'].pop('dirty')

            f=Path(root)/'res.jsonl';f.write_text(b.canonical(wrong)+'\n','utf-8')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'State projection fields'):
                b.load_responses(f)

    def test_separate_paths_required_for_prepare(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            base=Path(root)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'disjoint'):
                b.prepare(base/'one',base/'one')
            (base/'already').mkdir()
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'must be new'):
                b.prepare(base/'already',base/'separate')

    def test_deterministic_challenge_and_key(self):
        next_public,next_private=b.build_trial_data()
        self.assertEqual(self.public,next_public)
        self.assertEqual(self.private,next_private)
        self.assertEqual(b.fingerprint(self.public),self.private['challenge_sha256'])

    def test_all_17_action_clauses_present(self):
        self.assertEqual(17,len({t['stimulus']['action'] for t in self.public['trials'] if t['action_clause']}))
        for trial in self.public['trials']:
            if trial['action_clause'] is not None:
                self.assertEqual(trial['action_clause']['action'],trial['stimulus']['action'])
                self.assertTrue(trial['gate_families'])
```

```text
"""Fidelity-9 follow-on: independent logical interpreter and browser shell.

No app implementation is imported as an oracle. The frozen scenario corpus
supplies expected results and the existing 218 tests independently exercise
and compare the actual Notepad implementation.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Thread
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
from urllib.error import HTTPError
import json
import time
import unittest

from logical_engine import LogicalMachine
from logical_shell import Handler, LocalHost

HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CONTRACT=json.loads((HERE/'LOGICAL-CONTRACT.json').read_text('utf-8'))
ECONOMY=json.loads((HERE/'GATE-ECONOMY.json').read_text('utf-8'))
CASES=json.loads((HERE/'LOGICAL-TRACE-CORPUS.json').read_text('utf-8'))['cases']


def do_case(case):
    files={}
    aliases={}
    for k, value in case.get('files',{}).items():
        name='/virtual/'+k+'.txt'
        aliases['@'+k]=name
        files[name]=bytes.fromhex(value['hex']) if 'hex' in value else value['text'].encode('utf-8')
    for k in case.get('expected_files',{}):
        aliases.setdefault('@'+k,'/virtual/'+k+'.txt')
    machine=LogicalMachine(case['initial_text'],clipboard=case.get('clipboard',''),files=files)
    if case.get('fail_clipboard_read'):machine.fail_effects.add('CLIPBOARD_READ')
    if case.get('fail_clipboard_write'):machine.fail_effects.add('CLIPBOARD_WRITE')
    records=[]
    for step in case['steps']:
        payload=deepcopy(step.get('payload'))
        if type(payload) is dict:
            payload={k:aliases.get(v,v) if type(v) is str else v for k,v in payload.items()}
        response=machine.send(step['action'],payload)
        records.append(response)
        if step['outcome']=='rejected':
            if response['outcome']!='REJECTED_BY_GATE':
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected rejected {step['action']} got {response['outcome']}")
        else:
            if response['outcome']!='PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS':
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected success {step['action']} got {response}")
            result=response['result']
            k=step['outcome']
            if result['kind'] != k:
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected {k} got {result}")
            if k=='selection' and result['selection']!=step['selection']:
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected selection {step['selection']} got {result}")
            if k=='match' and result['selection']!=step['match']:
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected match {step['match']} got {result}")
            if k=='replace' and result['count']!=step['count']:
                raise AssertionError(f"Expected replacement count {step['count']} got {result}")
    snap=machine.state()
    final={key:snap[key] for key in ('text','revision','dirty','undo_count','redo_count')}
    if 'clipboard' in case['expected_final']:
        final['clipboard']=snap['clipboard']
    if 'path' in case['expected_final']:
        final['path']=next((k for k,v in aliases.items() if snap['path']==v),None)
    if final!=case['expected_final']:
        raise AssertionError(f"Expected {case['expected_final']} got {final}")
    for k, value in case.get('expected_files',{}).items():
        if machine.files[aliases['@'+k]].decode('utf-8')!=value:
            raise AssertionError(f'Virtual file {k} not saved correctly')
    return machine, records


class TestLogicalInterpreter(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_32_source_independent_traces(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(CASES),32)
        for c in CASES:
            with self.subTest(c['id']):
                do_case(c)

    def test_replay_determinism(self):
        for c in CASES:
            with self.subTest(c['id']):
                first,rec1=do_case(c)
                second,rec2=do_case(c)
                self.assertEqual(first.logical_state(),second.logical_state())
                self.assertEqual(rec1,rec2)

    def test_gate_economy_preserves_every_clause_without_duplicate_families(self):
        m=LogicalMachine()
        self.assertEqual(m.economy['gate_family_count'],len(m.economy['gate_families']))
        self.assertEqual(m.economy['gate_family_count'],9)
        self.assertEqual(m.economy['source_condition_count'],19)
        all_clauses=[c for g in m.economy['gate_families'] for c in g['clauses']]
        self.assertEqual(set(all_clauses),{c['gate_id'] for c in CONTRACT['gate_conditions']})
        self.assertEqual(len(all_clauses),len(set(all_clauses)))
        self.assertEqual(len(set(g['id'] for g in m.economy['gate_families'])),9)
        self.assertTrue(all(m.gates_for.values()))
        self.assertEqual(set(m.clauses),set(CONTRACT['conformance']['expected_actions']))

    def test_gate_family_contract_cannot_lose_one_clause(self):
        from unittest.mock import patch
        from logical_engine import Rejected
        bad=deepcopy(ECONOMY)
        bad['gate_families'][1]['clauses'].pop()
        import logical_engine
        original_read=Path.read_text
        def fake_read(path, *args, **kwargs):
            if str(path).endswith('GATE-ECONOMY.json'):
                return json.dumps(bad)
            return original_read(path,*args,**kwargs)
        with patch.object(Path,'read_text',fake_read):
            with self.assertRaises(Rejected):logical_engine.LogicalMachine()

    def test_invalid_boolean_index_never_mutates(self):
        m=LogicalMachine('abc')
        before=m.logical_state()
        r=m.send('insert',{'offset':True,'text':'X'})
        self.assertEqual(r['outcome'],'REJECTED_BY_GATE')
        self.assertEqual(before,m.logical_state())

    def test_unavailable_effect_preserves_document_and_baseline(self):
        m=LogicalMachine('abc')
        m.fail_effects.add('FS_WRITE')
        before=m.logical_state()
        r=m.send('save-as',{'path':'/virtual/test.txt','overwrite':False})
        self.assertEqual(r['outcome'],'REJECTED_BY_GATE')
        self.assertEqual(m.logical_state(),before)

    def test_llm_packet_contains_truth_without_expected_answer_or_python_source(self):
        m=LogicalMachine('hello')
        m.send('insert',{'offset':5,'text':' world'})
        p=m.reasoning_prompt()
        self.assertIn('"action": "insert"',p)
        self.assertIn('"text": "hello"',p)
        self.assertIn('"gate_families":',p)
        self.assertNotIn('"text": "hello world"',p)
        self.assertNotIn('def _dispatch',p)
        self.assertNotIn('notepad_guard.py',p)

    def test_all_known_initial_state_included_for_history_prediction(self):
        m=LogicalMachine('a')
        m.send('edit','b');m.send('undo',None)
        m.send('redo',None)
        pkt=m.last_packet
        self.assertEqual(pkt['known_state_before']['text'],'a')
        self.assertEqual(pkt['known_state_before']['redo_stack'],[['a','b']])
        self.assertEqual(pkt['stimulus']['action'],'redo')

    def test_virtual_effect_distinguished_from_actual_host_effect(self):
        m=LogicalMachine('note')
        r=m.send('save-as',{'path':'/virtual/a.txt','overwrite':False})
        self.assertEqual(r['outcome'],'PREDICTED_FROM_ANCHORS')
        self.assertEqual(m.files['/virtual/a.txt'],b'note')
        self.assertEqual(r['trace']['effect_scope'],'VIRTUAL_IN_MEMORY_ONLY')
        self.assertIn('VIRTUAL_ONLY',m.reasoning_prompt())

    def test_independent_runner_imports_no_app_source(self):
        source=(HERE/'logical_engine.py').read_text('utf-8')
        self.assertNotIn('from notepad_',source)
        self.assertNotIn('import notepad_',source)
        self.assertNotIn('subprocess.',source)
        self.assertNotIn('eval(',source)
        self.assertNotIn('exec(',source)


class TestBrowserShell(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.server=LocalHost(('127.0.0.1',0),Handler)
        cls.url=f'http://127.0.0.1:{cls.server.server_port}'
        cls.thread=Thread(target=cls.server.serve_forever,daemon=True)
        cls.thread.start()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.server.shutdown()
        cls.server.server_close()
        cls.thread.join(timeout=2)

    def request(self,path,body=None):
        init={'data':json.dumps(body).encode('utf-8'), 'headers':{'Content-Type':'application/json'},'method':'POST'} if body is not None else {}
        with urlopen(Request(self.url+path,**init),timeout=3) as r:
            return r.status, r.headers.get('Content-Type'), r.read().decode('utf-8')

    def test_shell_loads_all_static_resources_and_real_http_routes(self):
        for path,media in [('/','text/html'),('/shell.css','text/css'),('/shell.js','application/javascript')]:
            with self.subTest(path):
                status,ctype,contents=self.request(path)
                self.assertEqual(status,200)
                self.assertIn(media,ctype)
                self.assertGreater(len(contents),100)
        _,_,state=self.request('/api/state')
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(state)['gate_family_count'],9)

    def test_interactive_sequence_and_llm_packet_no_app_source(self):
        self.request('/api/reset',{})
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'edit','payload':'Hello'})
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw)['state']['text'],'Hello')
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'insert','payload':{'offset':5,'text':' World'}})
        r=json.loads(raw)
        self.assertEqual(r['state']['text'],'Hello World')
        self.assertEqual(r['trace']['action_clause'],'G-CMD-02')
        _,_,packet=self.request('/api/packet')
        p=json.loads(packet)['prompt']
        self.assertIn('"text": "Hello"',p)
        self.assertNotIn('"text": "Hello World"',p)
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'undo','payload':None})
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw)['state']['text'],'Hello')

    def test_open_seeded_browser_text_and_virtual_save(self):
        self.request('/api/reset',{})
        self.request('/api/seed-file',{'path':'/virtual/hello.txt','text':'Loaded 📝'})
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'open','payload':{'path':'/virtual/hello.txt','discard':False}})
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw)['state']['text'],'Loaded 📝')
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'save','payload':None})
        self.assertFalse(json.loads(raw)['state']['dirty'])

    def test_rejected_server_request_does_not_bypass_contract(self):
        self.request('/api/reset',{})
        _,_,raw=self.request('/api/command',{'action':'exec','payload':{'anything':'yes'}})
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw)['outcome'],'REJECTED_BY_GATE')
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(raw)['state']['text'],'Hello, Anchor!')
        with self.assertRaises(HTTPError):self.request('/api/seed-file',{'path':'../real.txt','text':'X'})


if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()
```

```text
"""Round 9: the build-time WhiteHole rejects drift in copied candidates.

The 181 inherited tests remain unchanged. These tests never edit working
source in place: every negative test operates on an isolated temporary copy.
"""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

from notepad_blueprint_gate import WhiteHoleBlueprintGate, _suite_is_complete


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


class BlueprintGateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.baseline = WhiteHoleBlueprintGate(ROOT).evaluate()

    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.tmp.name)
        for path in ROOT.glob("*.py"):
            shutil.copy2(path, self.root / path.name)
        for name in ("anchorforge-spec.json", "blueprint-manifest.json"):
            shutil.copy2(ROOT / name, self.root / name)

    def _json(self, filename):
        return json.loads((self.root / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def _write(self, filename, content):
        (self.root / filename).write_text(content if isinstance(content, str) else json.dumps(content), encoding="utf-8")

    def _rejects(self, phrase):
        report = WhiteHoleBlueprintGate(self.root).evaluate()
        self.assertFalse(report.valid, report.as_dict())
        self.assertIn(phrase, "\n".join(report.violations))

    def test_valid_complete_blueprint(self):
        report = self.baseline
        self.assertTrue(report.valid, report.as_dict())
        self.assertEqual(report.anchor_count, 9)
        self.assertEqual(report.obligation_count, 27)
        self.assertEqual(report.preserved_test_count, 181)
        self.assertEqual(report.as_dict()["gate"], "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE")

    def test_unittest_completed_suite_accepted(self):
        self.assertTrue(_suite_is_complete("Ran 213 tests in 3.2s\n\nOK", 182))

    def test_unittest_skipped_suite_rejected(self):
        self.assertFalse(_suite_is_complete("Ran 213 tests in 3.2s\n\nOK (skipped=30)", 182))

    def test_unittest_missing_summary_rejected(self):
        self.assertFalse(_suite_is_complete("OK", 182))

    def test_unittest_insufficient_count_rejected(self):
        self.assertFalse(_suite_is_complete("Ran 181 tests in 1.2s\n\nOK", 182))

    def test_declared_gate_is_not_a_runtime_command(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        self.assertEqual([a["id"] for a in spec["responsibilities"]].count("WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE"), 1)
        self.assertNotIn("blueprint-gate", self._json("blueprint-manifest.json")["runtime_actions"])

    def test_does_not_modify_candidate_files(self):
        paths = sorted(self.root.glob("*.py")) + sorted(self.root.glob("*.json"))
        before = [hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths]
        self.assertTrue(WhiteHoleBlueprintGate(self.root).evaluate().valid)
        after = [hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths]
        self.assertEqual(before, after)

    def test_missing_anchor_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["responsibilities"].pop(0)
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("fidelity, manifest fidelity and Anchor count")

    def test_duplicate_anchor_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["responsibilities"].append(spec["responsibilities"][0])
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("every Anchor must have one distinct ID")

    def test_renamed_source_anchor_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_document.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text().replace('anchor_id = "N-DOCUMENT"', 'anchor_id = "N-OTHER"'))
        self._rejects("does not declare matching anchor_id")

    def test_missing_source_rejected(self):
        (self.root / "notepad_history.py").unlink()
        self._rejects("unable to parse notepad_history.py")

    def test_changed_state_owner_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["state"][0]["owner"] = "N-EDIT-HISTORY"
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("state owner drift")

    def test_duplicate_protected_writer_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_surface.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text() + "\nclass DuplicateDocument:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._text = 'shadow'\n")
        self._rejects("forbidden writer of _text")

    def test_owner_removed_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_document.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text().replace("self._baseline_text", "self._renamed_baseline"))
        self._rejects("missing declared owned state attribute _baseline_text")

    def test_effect_guard_change_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["effects"][0]["guard"] = "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE"
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("must pass runtime command guard")

    def test_effect_confirmation_removed_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["effects"][0]["confirmation"] = ""
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("no confirmed success condition")

    def test_effect_executor_changed_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["effects"][2]["executor"] = "N-DOCUMENT"
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("lost original executor")

    def test_undeclared_network_import_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_document.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text() + "\nimport socket\n")
        self._rejects("undeclared network module imported")

    def test_unguarded_file_open_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_search.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text() + "\ndef illicit_read(path):\n    return open(path).read()\n")
        self._rejects("forbidden host call open")

    def test_unguarded_clipboard_call_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_document.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text() + "\ndef illicit_clip(root):\n    return root.clipboard_get()\n")
        self._rejects("forbidden host call clipboard_get")

    def test_runtime_command_action_added_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_guard.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text().replace('"replace-all"})', '"replace-all", "network"})'))
        self._rejects("runtime command allowlist drift")

    def test_missing_whitehole_route_rejected(self):
        filename = "notepad_surface.py"
        self._write(filename, (self.root / filename).read_text().replace("self.guard.execute(intent,", "self.guard.not_guarded(intent,"))
        self._rejects("surface no longer sends captured intent through WH guard")

    def test_missing_required_flow_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["flows"] = [f for f in spec["flows"] if f["from"] != "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE"]
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("required flow missing")

    def test_unknown_flow_endpoint_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["flows"][0]["to"] = "N-INVENTED"
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("flow has unknown endpoint")

    def test_removed_prior_obligation_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["obligations"].pop(0)
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("preserved obligation removed")

    def test_obligation_cycle_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["obligations"][0]["depends"] = [spec["obligations"][1]["id"]]
        spec["obligations"][1]["depends"] = [spec["obligations"][0]["id"]]
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)

    def test_uncovered_new_anchor_rejected(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")

            obj["design_anchors"] = [a for a in obj["design_anchors"] if a != "WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE"]
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        self._rejects("Anchor WH-BLUEPRINT-GATE is not covered")

    def test_missing_inherited_test_rejected(self):
        filename = "test_notepad_input.py"
        text = (self.root / filename).read_text()
        import re
        match = re.search(r"\bdef (test_\w+)\(", text)
        self.assertIsNotNone(match)
        self._write(filename, text.replace("def " + match.group(1) + "(", "def renamed_" + match.group(1) + "(", 1))
        self._rejects("inherited test method(s) removed or renamed")

    def test_missing_manifest_rejected(self):
        (self.root / "blueprint-manifest.json").unlink()
        self._rejects("cannot read blueprint-manifest.json")

    def test_syntax_invalid_candidate_rejected(self):
        self._write("notepad_document.py", "def this is not valid Python\n")
        self._rejects("unable to parse notepad_document.py")

    def test_cli_fails_closed_on_drift(self):
        spec = self._json("anchorforge-spec.json")
        spec["fidelity"] = 8
        self._write("anchorforge-spec.json", spec)
        output = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / "notepad_blueprint_gate.py"), "--root", str(self.root)],

        self.assertIn('"valid": false', output.stdout)

                                capture_output=True, text=True)
```

## File: notepad/test_notepad_clipboard.py

```text
"""Round 7 guard/clipboard integration and cumulative preservation tests."""
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

from notepad_clipboard import NeutralClipboardSelection, SelectionRange
from notepad_document import NeutralDocument
from notepad_files import NeutralFileSession
from notepad_guard import CommandRejected, WhiteHoleCommandGuard
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory
from notepad_input import BlackHoleEditorInput
from notepad_surface import NeutralEditorSurface, TkNotepadWindow


class MemoryClipboard:
    """Host test adapter, not an Anchor or extra document owner."""
    def __init__(self, text=""):
        self.text = text
        self.read_count = 0
        self.write_count = 0
        self.fail_read = False
        self.fail_write = False

    def read(self):
        self.read_count += 1
        if self.fail_read:
            raise RuntimeError("host clipboard unavailable")
        return self.text

    def write(self, text):
        self.write_count += 1
        if self.fail_write:
            raise RuntimeError("host clipboard write unavailable")
        self.text = text


class TestClipboardSelection(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.host = MemoryClipboard()
        self.surface = NeutralEditorSurface()
        self.surface.enable_clipboard(self.host.read, self.host.write)

    def snapshot_triplet(self):
        return (self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot(),
                self.host.text)

    def test_seventh_anchor_identity_and_explicit_host_binding(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.clipboard.anchor_id, "N-CLIPBOARD-SELECTION")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.clipboard.canonical_type, "Neutral")
        self.assertIs(self.surface.clipboard._document, self.surface.document)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "already enabled"):
            self.surface.enable_clipboard(self.host.read, self.host.write)

    def test_no_new_document_text_or_history_owners(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abc")
        self.surface.copy_selection(0, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "abc")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "a")

    def test_unbound_clipboard_command_rejected_before_effect(self):
        surface = NeutralEditorSurface()
        surface.text_changed("keep")
        previous = (surface.document.snapshot(), surface.history.snapshot())
        for action, payload in (("copy", {"start":0, "end":2}),
                                ("cut", {"start":0, "end":2}),
                                ("paste", {"start":0, "end":0}),
                                ("select-all", None)):
            with self.subTest(action=action):
                with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                    surface.dispatch(action, payload)
                self.assertEqual((surface.document.snapshot(), surface.history.snapshot()), previous)

    def test_clipboard_capture_alone_has_no_host_or_document_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        intent = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("cut", {"start":0, "end":2})
        self.assertEqual(intent.action, "cut")
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 0)

    def test_copy_unicode_selected_text_exactly_without_mutation(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("a📝b\nc")
        before = (self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.copy_selection(1, 3).text, "a📝b\nc")
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "📝b")
        self.assertEqual((self.surface.document.snapshot(), self.surface.history.snapshot()), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 1)

    def test_cut_is_one_undoable_edit_and_pastes_correctly(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abcdef")
        after = self.surface.cut_selection(1, 4)
        self.assertEqual(after.text, "aef")
        self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "bcd")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "abcdef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.redo().text, "aef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.paste_at(1, 1).text, "abcdef")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "aef")

    def test_paste_replaces_selection_atomically_and_undoes_once(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello world")
        self.host.text = "📝\n"
        after = self.surface.paste_at(6, 11)
        self.assertEqual(after.text, "hello 📝\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "hello world")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.redo().text, "hello 📝\n")

    def test_paste_empty_clipboard_can_delete_selected_text(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.paste_at(1, 2).text, "AC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.undo().text, "ABC")

    def test_paste_empty_clipboard_at_caret_is_noop(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        previous = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        result = self.surface.paste_at(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(result.text, "ABC")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), previous)

    def test_select_all_returns_unicode_offsets_without_side_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("📝 hello\nworld")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, len("📝 hello\nworld")))
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)
        self.assertEqual((self.host.read_count, self.host.write_count), (0, 0))

    def test_select_all_empty_document(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, 0))

    def test_reject_bad_range_without_clipboard_or_history_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abcd")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        cases = [
            {"start":0,"end":5}, {"start":3,"end":2}, {"start":-1,"end":2},
            {"start":False,"end":2}, {"start":0,"end":2.0},
            {"start":"0","end":2}, {"start":0,"end":2,"extra":True},
            {"start":0}, None, "bad",
        ]
        for payload in cases:
            with self.subTest(payload=payload):
                for action in ("copy", "cut", "paste"):
                    with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                        self.surface.dispatch(action, payload)
                    self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.read_count, 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.host.write_count, 0)

    def test_copy_and_cut_empty_selection_rejected_without_host_effect(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("abc")
        previous = self.snapshot_triplet()
        for action in ("copy", "cut"):
            with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                self.surface.dispatch(action, {"start":1,"end":1})
            self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), previous)

    def test_select_all_payload_rejected_effect_free(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        for payload in (0, "all", {}, {"start":0,"end":5}):
            with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                self.surface.dispatch("select-all", payload)
            self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_bad_clipboard_read_preserves_document_and_history(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("original")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.fail_read = True
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "clipboard read failed"):
            self.surface.paste_at(0, 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_bad_clipboard_write_prevents_cut(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("original")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.fail_write = True
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "clipboard write failed"):
            self.surface.cut_selection(0, 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet(), before)

    def test_clipboard_nonstr_returns_rejected_no_change(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("ABC")
        before = self.snapshot_triplet()
        self.host.text = 42
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, "plain text"):
            self.surface.paste_at(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.snapshot_triplet()[0:2], before[0:2])

    def test_reentrant_clipboard_read_does_not_apply_stale_paste(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        def stale_read():
            self.surface.document.replace_text("changed by host")
            return "X"
        clip = self.surface.clipboard
        clip._read_text = stale_read
        before_history = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.paste_at(1, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "changed by host")
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), before_history)

    def test_reentrant_clipboard_write_does_not_apply_stale_cut(self):
        self.surface.text_changed("hello")
        def stale_write(_text):
            self.surface.document.replace_text("changed by host")
        self.surface.clipboard._write_text = stale_write
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.cut_selection(1, 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.document.snapshot().text, "changed by host")

    def test_cross_document_binding_is_rejected(self):
        other = NeutralEditorSurface()
        other.text_changed("abc")
        original = other.document.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "another document"):
            NeutralClipboardSelection(other.document, self.surface.history, self.host.read, self.host.write)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "another document"):
            other.clipboard = self.surface.clipboard
            other.select_all()
        self.assertEqual(other.document.snapshot(), original)

    def test_invalid_clipboard_host_adapters_are_rejected(self):
        no_host = NeutralEditorSurface()
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            no_host.enable_clipboard(None, lambda x: None)
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            no_host.enable_clipboard(lambda: "text", 5)
        self.assertIsNone(no_host.clipboard)

    def test_undo_redo_preserves_clean_baseline_with_copy_cut_paste(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "note.txt"
            self.surface.enable_files()
            self.surface.text_changed("keep")
            self.surface.save_file_as(str(path))
            self.surface.copy_selection(0, 2)
            self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.cut_selection(0, 2)
            self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.undo()
            self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.surface.redo()
            self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch("read").dirty)
            self.assertEqual(path.read_text(), "keep")

    def test_clipboard_capability_persists_across_new_open(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "a.txt"
            path.write_text("opened 📝", encoding="utf-8")
            self.surface.enable_files()
            self.surface.text_changed("draft")
            self.surface.new_document(discard=True)
            self.surface.open_file(str(path))
            self.assertEqual(self.surface.select_all(), SelectionRange(0, len("opened 📝")))
            self.surface.copy_selection(0, 6)
            self.assertEqual(self.host.text, "opened")
            self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)

    def test_legacy_edit_undo_redo_unbound_guard_still_works(self):
        doc = NeutralDocument()
        guard = WhiteHoleCommandGuard()
        intent = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("edit", "one")
        self.assertEqual(guard.execute(intent, doc).text, "one")
        history = NeutralEditHistory(doc)
        self.assertEqual(guard.execute(BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("read"), doc).text, "one")
        self.assertEqual(history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)


class TestTkClipboard(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            self.root = tk.Tk()
            self.root.withdraw()
        except (ImportError, tk.TclError) as exc:
            self.skipTest(f"Tk display not available: {exc}")
        self.window = TkNotepadWindow(root=self.root)
        self.window.messagebox.showerror = lambda *args, **kwargs: self.fail(f"Unexpected error {args}")
        self.root.update()

    def tearDown(self):
        if getattr(self, "root", None):
            try:
                self.root.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def set_text(self, text):
        self.window.surface.text_changed(text)
        self.window.surface.display(self.window._paint)
        self.root.update()

    def choose(self, start, end):
        self.window.text.tag_add("sel", f"1.0+{start}c", f"1.0+{end}c")

    def test_gui_menu_and_keyboard_routes(self):
        for name in ("Copy", "Cut", "Paste", "Select All"):
            self.assertEqual(self.window.edit_menu.entrycget(name, "state"), "normal")
        for sequence in ("<<Copy>>", "<<Cut>>", "<<Paste>>", "<Control-a>",
                         "<Control-c>", "<Control-x>", "<Control-v>"):
            self.assertIn("callback", self.window.text.bind(sequence))
        # Round 8 explicitly authorizes Find/Replace through the new
        # document-bound search owner and existing WhiteHole.
        for name in ("Find…", "Replace…"):
            self.assertEqual(self.window.edit_menu.entrycget(name, "state"), "normal")

    def test_select_all_projects_selection_without_history(self):
        self.set_text("hello 📝\nworld")
        before = (self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertTrue(self.window.select_all())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.get("sel.first", "sel.last"), "hello 📝\nworld")
        self.assertEqual((self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot()), before)

    def test_copy_cut_paste_real_tk_clipboard(self):
        self.set_text("123📝567")
        self.choose(1, 4)
        self.assertTrue(self.window.copy_selection())
        self.assertEqual(self.root.clipboard_get(), "23📝")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "123📝567")
        self.assertTrue(self.window.cut_selection())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "1567")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.get("1.0", "end-1c"), "1567")
        self.assertEqual(self.root.clipboard_get(), "23📝")
        self.window.text.mark_set("insert", "1.0+1c")
        self.assertTrue(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "123📝567")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.undo().text, "1567")

    def test_paste_over_selection_and_correct_caret(self):
        self.set_text("hello world")
        self.root.clipboard_clear()
        self.root.clipboard_append("📝")
        self.choose(6, 11)
        self.assertTrue(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "hello 📝")
        self.assertEqual(self.window.text.index("insert"), self.window.text.index("end-1c"))
        self.assertFalse(bool(self.window.text.tag_ranges("sel")))

    def test_native_unsupported_clipboard_effects_blocked(self):
        self.set_text("keep")
        self.assertEqual(self.window._blocked_clipboard(None), "break")
        for sequence in ("<<PasteSelection>>", "<<Clear>>", "<Button-2>"):
            self.assertIn("_blocked_clipboard", self.window.text.bind(sequence))
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot().text, "keep")

    def test_no_selection_cut_copy_are_effect_free(self):
        self.set_text("abcd")
        before = (self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot())
        self.assertFalse(self.window.cut_selection())
        self.assertFalse(self.window.copy_selection())
        self.assertEqual((self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), self.window.surface.history.snapshot()), before)

    def test_tk_paste_clipboard_failure_preserves_document(self):
        self.set_text("keep")
        before = self.window.surface.document.snapshot()
        self.window.messagebox.showerror = lambda *args, **kw: None
        self.window._read_clipboard = lambda: self.fail("adapter is already bound")
        self.window.surface.clipboard._read_text = lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError("not available"))
        self.assertFalse(self.window.paste_clipboard())
        self.assertEqual(self.window.surface.document.snapshot(), before)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```text
"""Round 2 local checks for Neutral document state and cross-Anchor safety."""
import unittest
from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError

from notepad_document import NeutralDocument, DocumentSnapshot
from notepad_input import BlackHoleEditorInput


class TestNeutralDocument(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_new_document_is_empty_and_clean(self):
        self.assertEqual(NeutralDocument().snapshot(), DocumentSnapshot("", 0, False))

    def test_initial_content_is_its_clean_baseline(self):
        self.assertEqual(NeutralDocument("existing").snapshot(), DocumentSnapshot("existing", 0, False))

    def test_replace_text_updates_state_and_marks_dirty(self):
        d = NeutralDocument()
        self.assertEqual(d.replace_text("Draft\nSecond line"), DocumentSnapshot("Draft\nSecond line", 1, True))

    def test_repeated_identical_edit_is_no_op(self):
        d = NeutralDocument()
        d.replace_text("draft")
        self.assertEqual(d.replace_text("draft").revision, 1)

    def test_insert_text_at_unicode_codepoint_offset(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("A📝B")
        self.assertEqual(d.insert_text(2, "!").text, "A📝!B")
        self.assertTrue(d.snapshot().dirty)

    def test_delete_range_and_revision(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("abcd")
        self.assertEqual(d.delete_range(1, 3), DocumentSnapshot("ad", 1, True))

    def test_dirty_is_derived_and_clears_when_reverted(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("saved")
        d.replace_text("unsaved")
        self.assertTrue(d.snapshot().dirty)
        self.assertEqual(d.replace_text("saved"), DocumentSnapshot("saved", 2, False))

    def test_invalid_text_does_not_change_any_state(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("hello")
        before = d.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            d.replace_text(None)
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            d.insert_text(1, 123)
        self.assertEqual(before, d.snapshot())

    def test_invalid_offsets_are_rejected_atomically(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("abc")
        before = d.snapshot()
        for op in (
            lambda: d.insert_text(-1, "x"),
            lambda: d.insert_text(4, "x"),
            lambda: d.insert_text(True, "x"),
            lambda: d.delete_range(2, 1),
            lambda: d.delete_range(0, 4),
            lambda: d.delete_range(-1, 2),
        ):
            with self.assertRaises((TypeError, ValueError)):
                op()
            self.assertEqual(before, d.snapshot())

    def test_no_op_insert_and_delete_leave_revision_unchanged(self):
        d = NeutralDocument("abc")
        d.insert_text(2, "")
        d.delete_range(1, 1)
        self.assertEqual(d.snapshot(), DocumentSnapshot("abc", 0, False))

    def test_snapshots_are_immutable_and_independent(self):
        d = NeutralDocument()
        old = d.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            old.text = "changed"
        d.replace_text("new")
        self.assertEqual(old, DocumentSnapshot("", 0, False))
        self.assertEqual(d.snapshot().text, "new")

    def test_blackhole_intents_do_not_implicitly_execute_in_neutral(self):
        d = NeutralDocument()
        before = d.snapshot()
        event = BlackHoleEditorInput.receive("edit", "should not be applied yet")
        self.assertEqual((event.action, event.payload), ("edit", "should not be applied yet"))
        self.assertEqual(d.snapshot(), before)

    def test_separate_documents_do_not_share_state(self):
        first, second = NeutralDocument(), NeutralDocument()
        first.replace_text("first")
        self.assertEqual(second.snapshot(), DocumentSnapshot("", 0, False))

    def test_no_filesystem_effect_on_edit(self):
        # No path or save API is present on this Anchor.
        d = NeutralDocument()
        self.assertFalse(hasattr(d, "save"))
        self.assertFalse(hasattr(d, "open_file"))
        self.assertEqual(d.replace_text("draft").text, "draft")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```text
"""Round 6 file capability and Round 1-5 cumulative preservation checks."""
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from notepad_document import NeutralDocument
from notepad_files import FileSessionError, NeutralFileSession
from notepad_guard import CommandRejected, WhiteHoleCommandGuard
from notepad_history import NeutralEditHistory
from notepad_input import BlackHoleEditorInput
from notepad_surface import NeutralEditorSurface, TkNotepadWindow


class TestFileSession(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.folder = Path(self.tmp.name)
        self.surface = NeutralEditorSurface()
        self.surface.enable_files()
        self.path = self.folder / 'notes 📝.txt'

    def test_identity_sixth_anchor(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.file_session.anchor_id, 'N-FILE-SESSION')
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.file_session.canonical_type, 'Neutral')
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path, None)

    def test_capture_only_does_not_save(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('abc')
        BlackHoleEditorInput.receive('save-as', {'path': str(self.path), 'overwrite': False})
        self.assertFalse(self.path.exists())
        self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch('read').dirty)

    def test_save_as_utf8_file_clean_document(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('one 📝\nsecond\n')
        revision = self.surface.dispatch('read').revision
        snap = self.surface.save_file_as(str(self.path))
        self.assertEqual(self.path.read_bytes(), 'one 📝\nsecond\n'.encode('utf-8'))
        self.assertFalse(snap.dirty)
        self.assertEqual(snap.revision, revision)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path, str(self.path))
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 1)

    def test_save_existing_association_and_undo_after_save(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('first')
        self.surface.save_file_as(str(self.path))
        self.surface.text_changed('second')
        self.surface.save_file()
        self.assertEqual(self.path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), 'second')
        self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch('read').dirty)
        self.surface.undo()
        self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch('read').dirty)
        self.surface.redo()
        self.assertFalse(self.surface.dispatch('read').dirty)

    def test_untitled_save_does_not_create_file_or_change_baseline(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('unsaved')
        before = self.surface.dispatch('read')
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.save_file()
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.dispatch('read'), before)
        self.assertIsNone(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path)

    def test_open_valid_file_clears_old_history_and_dirty(self):
        self.path.write_bytes('loaded 📝\n'.encode('utf-8'))
        self.surface.text_changed('old')
        self.surface.text_changed('changed')
        before = self.surface.dispatch('read').revision
        snap = self.surface.open_file(str(self.path), discard=True)
        self.assertEqual(snap.text, 'loaded 📝\n')
        self.assertEqual(snap.revision, before + 1)
        self.assertFalse(snap.dirty)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.undo()

    def test_open_dirty_without_confirm_preserves_everything(self):
        self.path.write_text('file', encoding='utf-8')
        self.surface.text_changed('unsaved')
        before = self.surface.dispatch('read')
        hist = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, 'unsaved changes'):
            self.surface.open_file(str(self.path))
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.dispatch('read'), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), hist)
        self.assertIsNone(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path)

    def test_new_dirty_requires_discard(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('unsaved')
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.new_document()
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.dispatch('read').text, 'unsaved')
        snap = self.surface.new_document(discard=True)
        self.assertEqual(snap.text, '')
        self.assertFalse(snap.dirty)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot().undo_count, 0)

    def test_new_clears_file_association_even_when_clean(self):
        self.surface.text_changed('abc')
        self.surface.save_file_as(str(self.path))
        self.surface.new_document()
        self.assertIsNone(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path)
        self.assertTrue(self.path.exists())
        self.assertEqual(self.path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), 'abc')
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.save_file()

    def test_open_invalid_utf8_preserves_unsaved_and_history(self):
        self.path.write_bytes(b'\xff\xfe')
        self.surface.text_changed('old')
        before = self.surface.dispatch('read')
        hist = self.surface.history.snapshot()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, 'UTF-8'):
            self.surface.open_file(str(self.path), discard=True)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.dispatch('read'), before)
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.history.snapshot(), hist)

    def test_open_missing_file_is_effect_free(self):
        before = self.surface.dispatch('read')
        with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
            self.surface.open_file(str(self.path))
        self.assertEqual(self.surface.dispatch('read'), before)

    def test_reject_relative_paths_and_nul(self):
        for path in ('../outside.txt', 'file.txt', '', '/tmp/bad\x00path'):
            with self.subTest(path=path):
                with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                    self.surface.open_file(path)
                with self.assertRaises(CommandRejected):
                    self.surface.save_file_as(path)
        self.assertIsNone(self.surface.file_session.snapshot().path)

    def test_existing_other_file_requires_overwrite_grant(self):
        self.path.write_text('original', encoding='utf-8')
        self.surface.text_changed('replacement')
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CommandRejected, 'overwrite confirmation'):
            self.surface.save_file_as(str(self.path))
        self.assertEqual(self.path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), 'original')
        self.assertTrue(self.surface.dispatch('read').dirty)
        self.surface.save_file_as(str(self.path), overwrite=True)
        self.assertEqual(self.path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), 'replacement')
```
